These results suggest that range-aware BO can provide a practical and sample-efficient foundation for specification-driven design, particularly when design flexibility and solution diversity are important considerations.

中文摘要翻译: 在许多材料和产品设计问题中，理想的候选解往往表现出落在可接受范围内的性质，而非达到单一最优值。恢复多个满足此类规格的不同解也具有实际价值，因为某些候选解可能由于成本、可加工性或鲁棒性等原因而更受青睐，而这些因素很难直接编码到目标函数中。在此，我们开发了一个范围感知的贝叶斯优化（BO）框架，其中采集函数直接评估候选解满足目标范围的后验概率。该框架自然地扩展到在共享候选空间上并行寻找多个不同规格。在基准测试任务中，与标准BO基线和近期目标寻求方法相比，范围感知采集一致地恢复出更大且更多样化的有效设计集。其效用进一步在两个实际驱动的设计案例研究中得到证明，涉及聚合物合成的反应条件优化以及基于量子化学计算的支持下，为指定光学吸收带发现序列定义的低聚物。这些结果表明，范围感知BO可以为规格驱动型设计提供实用且样本高效的框架，特别是当设计灵活性和解多样性是重要考虑因素时。

</details>


### [151] [Beyond the Golden Teacher: Enhancing Graph Learning through LLM-GNN Co-teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11583)
*Zhuoyi Peng,Hanlin Gu,Lixin Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-GNN Co-Teaching框架，放弃传统“黄金教师”假设，通过GNN与LLM双向互教和基于轮次的伪标签偏好优化（RPL-PO），在半监督少样本图学习中显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM-GNN方法都采用“黄金教师”范式：指定一个模型为教师，用其输出指导另一个模型。但在稀疏监督下，两个模型都不是完美的，强行指定教师会将盲区传递给学生。作者希望避免指定任何模型为黄金教师，仍能实现有效的图学习。

Method: 提出LLM-GNN Co-Teaching双向互教框架：GNN和LLM在每个训练轮次中按小损失准则交换各自最自信的伪标签，并共同更新。进一步提出基于轮次的伪标签偏好优化（RPL-PO）：当一个节点在t轮时两模型矛盾，在t+1轮转为一致，则LLM对同一输入的两个回答构成偏好对（旧的矛盾回答 < 新的被对方认可的回答），用于DPO训练。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，LLM-GNN Co-Teaching一致优于GNN-as-Judge及所有先前方法，3-shot在Cora上绝对提升7.86%，在ogbn-arxiv上提升7.73%；5-shot和零样本跨数据集迁移也均有提升。误差结构分析表明，放弃黄金教师假设显著提升了LLM在困难样本上的图学习能力。

Conclusion: 放弃黄金教师假设、采用双向互教范式能有效克服单一模型的盲区，在半监督少样本文本属性图学习任务上取得显著性能提升。

Original Abstract: Text-attributed graphs (TAGs) underlie real-world applications such as citation networks, social media, and e-commerce. Few-shot graph learning on TAGs is hard: with only a handful of labels per class and the rest of the graph unannotated, neither GNNs nor LLMs can learn well on their own. GNNs read topology and fail on cold nodes; LLMs read text and fail on text-ambiguous nodes. Existing LLM-GNN methods all follow the same recipe: designate one model as the golden teacher and use its outputs (e.g., features or pseudo-labels) to supervise the other. We argue this golden-teacher assumption breaks under sparse supervision: neither model is golden, and treating either as such transfers its blind spots into the student. We therefore ask: can we avoid designating either model as the golden teacher, and still perform effective graph learning? We answer with LLM-GNN Co-Teaching, a bidirectional co-teaching framework in which neither model is fixed as teacher. The GNN and LLM exchange their most confident pseudo-labels under an architecture-specific small-loss criterion, and both update every round. Supervision is then mined from the trajectory: whenever a node moves from cross-model contradiction at round t to cross-model agreement at round t+1, the LLM's two answers on the same input form a preference pair (old contradicting self < new peer-endorsed self) for DPO training. We call this Round-based Pseudo-Label Preference Optimization (RPL-PO). On six benchmarks, LLM-GNN Co-Teaching consistently outperforms GNN-as-Judge and all prior methods, with absolute 3-shot gains of 7.86% on Cora and 7.73% on ogbn-arxiv; improvements carry over to 5-shot and to zero-shot cross-dataset transfer. Error-structure analysis further shows that abandoning the golden-teacher assumption substantially improves the LLM's graph learning capability on challenging samples.

中文摘要翻译: 文本属性图(TAG)支撑着引文网络、社交媒体和电子商务等实际应用。TAG上的少样本图学习是困难的：每类仅有个位数的标签而图的其余部分未标注时，GNN和LLM都无法单独学好。GNN读取拓扑结构，在冷启动节点上失败；LLM读取文本，在文本模糊节点上失败。现有的LLM-GNN方法都遵循同一范式：指定一个模型为黄金教师，用其输出（如特征或伪标签）来监督另一个。我们认为，在稀疏监督下，这种黄金教师假设是站不住脚的：两个模型都不是完美的，将任一模型视为黄金教师会将其盲区传递给学生。因此我们问：能否避免指定任一模型为黄金教师，仍能实现有效的图学习？我们的答案是LLM-GNN Co-Teaching，一种双向互教框架，其中没有模型被固定为教师。GNN和LLM在架构特定的小损失准则下交换各自最自信的伪标签，并且每轮都共同更新。然后从轨迹中挖掘监督信号：当一个节点从t轮的跨模型矛盾状态转变为t+1轮的跨模型一致状态时，LLM对同一输入的两个回答构成一个偏好对（旧的矛盾自我 < 新的被对方认可的自我），用于DPO训练。我们称这种方法为基于轮次的伪标签偏好优化(RPL-PO)。在六个基准测试上，LLM-GNN Co-Teaching持续优于GNN-as-Judge和所有先前方法，在Cora上3-shot绝对提升7.86%，在ogbn-arxiv上提升7.73%；改进也延续到5-shot和零样本跨数据集迁移。误差结构分析进一步表明，放弃黄金教师假设显著提升了LLM在困难样本上的图学习能力。

</details>


### [152] [Kuramoto Attention: Synchronizing Self-Attention on the Torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11585)
*Joshua Nunley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Kuramoto注意力层，将每个隐藏坐标建模为角度，通过门控余弦相似度评分和注意力加权圆均值更新实现自注意力，在语言建模任务上达到与强基线Transformer接近的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索自注意力机制与相位同步之间的深层联系，通过将注意力层约束到紧致群（圆环面）的几何结构上，构建一个兼具理论优雅性和实用性的注意力层，同时验证这种几何约束在语言建模中的可行性。

Method: 将每个隐藏状态表示为角度（相位），使用门控余弦相似度作为评分函数，注意力权重通过softmax计算；更新规则为Kuramoto耦合项：θ_t ← θ_t + Σ_u A_{t,u} sin(θ_u - θ_t)，即每个token被拉向其注意力加权圆均值；旋转位置编码作为位置相关的相位漂移融入评分中。

Result: 在enwiki8字符级语言建模上：1M参数时BPC为1.637±0.010（基线1.616±0.004）；5M参数时中位数1.448 vs 1.452（均值上Transformer略优：1.468 vs 1.456）。

Conclusion: 这种受约束的几何结构在百万参数规模上是可行的语言模型，为自注意力与相位同步之间建立了简洁的桥梁；结构本身及其同步解读是主要贡献。

Original Abstract: We introduce Kuramoto attention, a self-attention layer in which each hidden coordinate is an angle. The layer scores tokens by gated cosine similarity, attends over previous phase states, and updates each token by the tangent component of the attention-weighted circular mean. Because the values are the raw phase states, this update is exactly the Kuramoto coupling term $\sum_u A_{t,u}\sin(θ_u-θ_t)$, with the attention matrix acting as an adaptive, content-dependent coupling kernel. Equivalently, the gated score is a learned metric on the torus that selects which tokens couple, and the update pulls each token toward the circular mean of the tokens it selects, tightening their phase agreement. The same two ingredients, an invariant similarity score and an on-manifold mean, define such a layer on any compact group; the torus is the abelian case, where both are closed-form. The softmax weights solve an entropy-regularized phase-retrieval problem, and rotary position enters as a position-dependent phase drift in the score. On enwiki8 character-level language modeling, the layer trains as a functional language model whose bits-per-character stays close to a strong matched RoPE+SwiGLU transformer: within $0.02$ BPC at one million parameters ($1.637\pm0.010$ versus $1.616\pm0.004$) and level on the median at five million ($1.448$ versus $1.452$ over five seeds) with the transformer ahead on the mean ($1.468$ versus $1.456$). These experiments establish that the constrained geometric structure is a viable language model at this scale; the structure itself, and its synchronization reading, is the contribution. Ablations isolate the load-bearing components, and the result gives a compact bridge between self-attention and phase synchronization.

中文摘要翻译: 我们引入了Kuramoto注意力，一种自注意力层，其中每个隐藏坐标都是一个角度。该层通过门控余弦相似度对token进行评分，关注先前的相位状态，并通过注意力加权的圆均值的切向量分量更新每个token。由于值就是原始的相位状态，该更新恰好是Kuramoto耦合项$\sum_u A_{t,u}\sin(θ_u-θ_t)$，其中注意力矩阵充当一个自适应的、内容相关的耦合核。等价地，门控分数是在环面上学习的一种度量，用于选择哪些token发生耦合，而更新则将每个token拉向其选择token的圆均值，从而增强它们的相位一致性。相同的两个要素——一个不变相似度评分和一个流形上的均值——可以在任何紧致群上定义这样的层；环面是阿贝尔情形，此时两者都有闭式解。softmax权重求解了一个熵正则化的相位恢复问题，而旋转位置编码则作为分数中一个与位置相关的相位漂移进入模型。在enwiki8字符级语言建模上，该层作为一个功能性的语言模型进行训练，其每字符比特数与强匹配的RoPE+SwiGLU Transformer保持接近：在一百万参数规模下相差0.02 BPC（1.637±0.010对比1.616±0.004），在五百万参数规模下中位数持平（五个种子下1.448对比1.452），而Transformer在均值上略领先（1.468对比1.456）。这些实验表明，这种受约束的几何结构在此规模下是一个可行的语言模型；其结构本身及其同步解读是本文的贡献。消融实验分离了承重组件，该结果为自注意力与相位同步之间建立了一座紧凑的桥梁。

</details>


### [153] [Physics-Distilled Neural Network enabled by Large Language Models for Manufacturing Process-Property Predictive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11605)
*Ge Song,Kiarash Naghavi Khanghah,Anandkumar Patel,Rajiv Malhotra,Hongyi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种知识蒸馏框架，利用大语言模型从文献中提取物理先验知识，通过图掩码注意力层建模变量间复杂依赖，将知识从教师模型蒸馏到轻量级学生模型，在数据稀缺的制造场景中实现高精度、高容错且支持实时边缘部署（>6000 Hz）的预测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 制造领域中预测工艺-性能关系面临两大挑战：实验成本高昂导致数据稀缺，以及复杂黑箱模型可解释性差。现有方法难以在有限数据下同时保证预测精度和模型可解释性。

Method: 提出一种知识蒸馏框架，包含：(1) 利用大语言模型从科学文献中系统提取解析物理先验知识；(2) 构建包含图掩码注意力层的特权教师模型，捕捉输入变量间严格的设定点或静态与高频时间序列组合的复杂物理依赖；(3) 将特权知识蒸馏到轻量级学生预测器用于推理；(4) 采用重复K折交叉验证以保障小数据集下的统计可靠性。

Result: (1) 在五种不同制造过程中均持续实现高预测精度；(2) 即使LLM推导的解析先验不完美或不完整时，模型仍保持鲁棒的预测性能，表现出显著的容错能力；(3) 学生预测器推理频率超过6000 Hz，可在标准工业硬件上实现实时边缘部署。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，在数据受限的环境下弥合理论物理与实时工业监测之间的鸿沟。

Original Abstract: Predicting process-property relationships in manufacturing is often challenged by high experimental costs and the limited interpretability of complex 'black-box' models. This paper proposes a novel knowledge distillation framework designed to achieve high-accuracy predictions in data-scarce scenarios. The framework integrates analytical physics priors, which are systematically extracted from scientific literature via Large Language Models, into a privileged teacher model. We employ a Graph-Masked Attention layer to capture the complex physical dependencies among input variables showing strict setpoints or a combination of static and high-frequency temporal signatures. This privileged knowledge is distilled into a lightweight student predictor for inference. The feasibility and robustness of the framework are evaluated through a comprehensive experiment across five diverse manufacturing processes. To ensure statistical reliability, given the small dataset sizes, a repeated K-fold cross-validation technique is employed to quantify model stability and generalization. Results indicate that the proposed framework consistently achieves high predictive accuracy across all evaluated domains. Most importantly, the architecture demonstrates significant fault tolerance by maintaining robust predictive performance even in scenarios where LLM-derived analytical priors are suboptimal or incomplete. Furthermore, the student predictor achieves an inference frequency exceeding 6000 Hz, which facilitates real-time edge deployment on standard industrial hardware. This work provides a scalable solution for bridging the gap between theoretical physics and real-time industrial monitoring in data-limited environments.

中文摘要翻译: 预测制造过程中的工艺-性能关系往往面临高昂的实验成本和复杂"黑箱"模型可解释性有限的挑战。本文提出了一种新颖的知识蒸馏框架，旨在数据稀缺场景下实现高精度预测。该框架将解析物理先验（通过大语言模型从科学文献中系统提取）整合到特权教师模型中。我们采用图掩码注意力层来捕捉输入变量之间复杂的物理依赖关系，这些变量表现为严格的设定点或静态与高频时间序列特征的组合。该特权知识被蒸馏到轻量级学生预测器中进行推理。通过涵盖五种不同制造过程的综合实验，评估了该框架的可行性和鲁棒性。为确保统计可靠性，针对小数据集规模采用了重复K折交叉验证技术来量化模型的稳定性和泛化能力。结果表明，所提框架在所有评估领域均持续实现高预测精度。最重要的是，即使在LLM推导的解析先验不理想或不完整的情况下，该架构仍能保持鲁棒的预测性能，表现出显著的容错能力。此外，学生预测器的推理频率超过6000 Hz，便于在标准工业硬件上进行实时边缘部署。这项工作为在数据受限环境中弥合理论物理与实时工业监测之间的鸿沟提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

</details>


### [154] [Information-Theoretic Decomposition for Multimodal Interaction Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11614)
*Zequn Yang,Yake Wei,Haotian Ni,Zhihao Xu,Di Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分解的多模态交互学习范式（DMIL），通过显式建模样本特定的交互成分（冗余、独特、协同），实现对动态多模态交互的自适应学习，在多种任务和架构上取得了一致优越的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态学习中存在一个关键但未被充分探索的挑战：模态间的隐式交互（冗余、独特、协同信息）随样本动态变化，而传统范式（模态集成方法难以捕捉协同信息，联合学习范式常未充分利用冗余信息）无法自适应地学习这些样本特定的交互类型。

Method: 提出DMIL框架：1）设计变分解耦架构，将模态交互分离为冗余、独特、协同三种成分；2）采用新的学习策略，在微调过程中利用这些显式交互成分，实现全面的交互学习。

Result: 在多种任务和架构上的大量实验表明，DMIL通过适应整体样本特定交互，持续取得了优越的性能。

Conclusion: DMIL建立了一种以交互为中心的多模态学习新范式，灵活且具有广泛的适用性。

Original Abstract: Multimodal learning hinges on capturing redundant, unique, and synergistic information across modalities, which collectively constitute multimodal interactions. A critical yet underexplored challenge is that these implicit interactions vary dynamically across samples. In this work, we present the first systematic, information-theoretic analysis highlighting why learning these dynamic, sample-specific interactions is critical for effective multimodal learning. Our analysis further reveals deficits in conventional paradigms at learning these distinct interaction types: modality ensemble approaches struggle to capture synergy, while joint learning paradigms often under-utilize redundant information. This highlights the need for an approach that can adaptively learn from different interaction types on a per-sample basis. To this end, we propose Decomposition-based Multimodal Interaction Learning (DMIL), a novel paradigm that explicitly models and learns from sample-specific interactions. First, we design a variational decomposition architecture to isolate the constituent interaction components. Second, we employ a new learning strategy that leverages these explicit interaction components in a fine-tuning process to achieve comprehensive interaction learning. Extensive experiments across diverse tasks and architectures demonstrate that DMIL consistently achieves superior performance by adapting to holistic sample-specific interactions. Our framework is flexible and broadly applicable, establishing an interaction-centric paradigm for multimodal learning. The code is available at https://github.com/GeWu-Lab/DMIL.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态学习的关键在于捕捉跨模态的冗余、独特和协同信息，这些信息共同构成了多模态交互。一个关键但尚未充分探索的挑战是，这些隐式交互会随样本动态变化。在这项工作中，我们首次进行了系统的、基于信息论的分析，阐明了为什么学习这些动态的、样本特定的交互对于有效的多模态学习至关重要。我们的分析进一步揭示了传统范式在学习这些不同交互类型时的缺陷：模态集成方法难以捕捉协同信息，而联合学习范式往往未能充分利用冗余信息。这突显了需要一种能够在每个样本基础上自适应地从不同交互类型中学习的方法。为此，我们提出了基于分解的多模态交互学习（DMIL），一种显式建模并学习样本特定交互的新范式。首先，我们设计了一个变分解耦架构来分离出构成性的交互成分。其次，我们采用了一种新的学习策略，在微调过程中利用这些显式交互成分，实现全面的交互学习。跨多种任务和架构的大量实验表明，DMIL通过适应整体的样本特定交互，持续取得了优越的性能。我们的框架灵活且具有广泛的适用性，建立了一种以交互为中心的多模态学习范式。代码可在 https://github.com/GeWu-Lab/DMIL 获取。

</details>


### [155] [DeMix: Debugging Training Data with Mixed Data Error Types by Investigating Influence Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11616)
*Jiale Deng,Yanyan Shen,Xiaogang Shi,Chai Junjun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeMix是一个同时检测训练数据中错误样本及其错误类型的框架，通过影响向量捕获不同错误类型对模型行为的独特模式，将数据调试转化为多标签分类问题，在11个任务上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实训练数据集中常包含标签错误、特征错误和虚假关联等多种混合错误类型，现有数据清洗和归因方法无法同时满足检测错误样本和识别具体错误类型的双重需求，导致无法进行针对性修复。

Method: 提出DeMix框架，核心思路是利用影响向量（influence vectors）刻画每个训练样本对所有验证样本模型预测的影响，从而捕获不同错误类型的独特模式；将训练数据调试形式化为多标签分类问题，训练分类器直接从影响向量预测错误类型；引入基于干预的学习策略，引导分类器捕获每种错误类型的不变因果特征（invariant rationales），提升泛化能力。

Result: 在表格数据预测、推荐系统和LLM对齐等11个任务上，DeMix在数据调试F1分数上比最先进方法提升22.61%，数据修复后的任务模型性能提升9.32%。

Conclusion: DeMix能够有效诊断训练数据中的多种错误类型，显著优于现有方法，为高质量训练数据的构建提供了实用工具。

Original Abstract: High-quality training data is essential for the success of machine learning models. However, real-world datasets often contain mixed types of errors arising from systematic flaws in data preparation pipelines, including label errors, feature errors, and spurious correlations. Effective debugging of training data requires both detecting erroneous samples and identifying their specific error types to enable targeted repair, yet existing data cleaning and attribution methods fail to adequately address this dual requirement. In this paper, we propose DeMix, a novel framework that simultaneously diagnoses erroneous samples and their error types. Our key insight is that different error types produce distinct patterns on model behavior. DeMix captures such error-specific patterns by influence vectors that characterize how each training sample affects model predictions across all validation samples. We formulate training data debugging as a multi-label classification problem where a classifier is developed to predict error types directly from influence vectors. We further introduce an intervention-based learning strategy that guides the classifier to capture invariant rationales specific to each error type, ensuring the learned classifier generalizes effectively. Empirical evaluations on 11 tasks across tabular data prediction, recommendation systems, and LLM alignment demonstrate that DeMix significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, achieving a 22.61% improvement in data debugging F1-score and a 9.32% gain in task model performance after data repair. Code is available at: https://github.com/SJTU-DMTai/DeMix.

中文摘要翻译: 高质量的训练数据对于机器学习模型的成功至关重要。然而，真实世界的数据集通常包含因数据准备流程中的系统性缺陷而产生的混合类型错误，包括标签错误、特征错误和虚假关联。有效的训练数据调试需要同时检测错误样本并识别其具体错误类型，以便进行针对性修复，但现有的数据清洗和归因方法未能充分满足这一双重需求。在本文中，我们提出DeMix，一种能够同时诊断错误样本及其错误类型的新型框架。我们的关键洞察在于，不同的错误类型会在模型行为上产生独特的模式。DeMix通过影响向量（influence vectors）捕获这些错误特定的模式，该向量刻画每个训练样本对所有验证样本模型预测的影响。我们将训练数据调试形式化为一个多标签分类问题，训练一个分类器直接从影响向量预测错误类型。我们进一步引入一种基于干预的学习策略，引导分类器捕获每种错误类型特有的不变因果特征（invariant rationales），确保所学的分类器具有良好的泛化能力。在表格数据预测、推荐系统和LLM对齐等11个任务上的实证评估表明，DeMix显著优于最先进的方法，在数据调试F1分数上实现了22.61%的提升，在数据修复后的任务模型性能上获得了9.32%的提升。代码已在以下地址开源：https://github.com/SJTU-DMTai/DeMix。

</details>


### [156] [TimeRouter: Efficient and Adaptive Routing of Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11625)
*Kanghui Ning,Yushan Jiang,Kashif Rasul,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka,Dongjin Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimeRouter 是一个轻量级路由框架，通过可学习路由头、选择性门控和集成回退机制，在无需 LLM 推理开销的情况下，实现对预训练时间序列基础模型池的自适应专家选择，在 GIFT-EVAL 榜单上达到 SOTA 性能（LB MASE 0.6765）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型（TSFM）具有异构归纳偏置，单一模型难以在所有预测场景中持续最优，而当前基于 LLM 的控制器进行专家选择的方案推理开销巨大。因此需要一种高效、轻量级的专家路由机制来替代 LLM 控制器。

Method: TimeRouter 包含三个核心组件：1）可学习路由头（learned routing head）进行轻量级判别式路由；2）选择性门控（selective gating）控制模型激活；3）集成回退（ensemble fallback）作为兜底策略。三者结合实现了无需 LLM 推理的自适应专家选择。

Result: 在 GIFT-EVAL 榜单上取得 SOTA 性能，LB MASE 达到 0.6765。消融实验揭示了模型池组成和选择性门控对路由设计的重要性。

Conclusion: TimeRouter 作为一种模块化、轻量级的路由层，可有效应用于基于基础模型池的未来智能体时间序列系统。

Original Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) are increasingly explored as predictive experts within emerging agentic time-series systems. However, TSFMs exhibit heterogeneous inductive biases, and no single model consistently dominates across forecasting regimes, making expert selection a critical challenge. Existing systems often delegate this decision to LLM-based controllers, incurring substantial inference overhead. We present TimeRouter, an efficient routing framework that leverages empirical complementarity across a pool of pretrained TSFMs through lightweight discriminative routing, selective gating, and ensemble fallback. Concretely, TimeRouter combines a learned routing head, a selective gate, and an ensemble fallback, enabling adaptive expert selection without invoking an LLM at inference time. TimeRouter achieves state-of-the-art performance on the GIFT-EVAL leaderboard, with an LB MASE of 0.6765. Beyond benchmark performance, our ablation studies provide empirical insights into TSFM routing design, highlighting the importance of pool composition and selective gating. Taken together, these results position TimeRouter as a modular and lightweight routing layer for future agentic time-series systems built upon foundation-model pools. Our code is available at https://github.com/UConn-DSIS/TimeRouter.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在新兴的智能体时间序列系统中越来越多地被用作预测专家。然而，TSFMs 表现出异构的归纳偏置，没有任何单一模型能在所有预测场景中持续表现优异，这使得专家选择成为一个关键挑战。现有系统通常将此决策委托给基于大语言模型（LLM）的控制器，从而产生大量的推理开销。我们提出了 TimeRouter，一种高效的路由框架，通过轻量级判别式路由、选择性门控和集成回退机制，利用预训练 TSFMs 池中经验上的互补性。具体而言，TimeRouter 结合了可学习路由头、选择性门控和集成回退，能够在推理时无需调用 LLM 即可实现自适应专家选择。TimeRouter 在 GIFT-EVAL 榜单上取得了最先进的性能，LB MASE 达到 0.6765。除基准性能外，我们的消融研究提供了关于 TSFM 路由设计的经验性见解，强调了模型池组成和选择性门控的重要性。综合来看，这些结果使 TimeRouter 成为未来基于基础模型池的智能体时间序列系统中一种模块化且轻量级的路由层。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/UConn-DSIS/TimeRouter 获取。

</details>


### [157] [When Context Returns: Toward Robust Internalization in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11627)
*Xun Wang,Ruishuo Chen,Zhuoran Li,Yu Chen,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个有趣的现象：在策略蒸馏中，将原有的特权上下文重新引入蒸馏后的学生模型反而会降低其性能，即使对那些无需上下文就能正确解决的实例也是如此。作者将这种现象称为“上下文引发的性能退化”，并提出了一种轻量级的一致性正则化方法来解决该问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的策略蒸馏方法虽然能将特权上下文（如系统提示或任务提示）内化到学生模型中，从而在推理时无需上下文，但作者发现了一个此前未被研究的有趣现象：重新引入原始特权上下文到蒸馏后的学生模型时，实际上会降低其性能。这促使作者提出需要一种更强的内化标准——不仅需要匹配教师模型的上下文条件行为，还需要在上下文重新引入时保持稳定。

Method: 提出一种轻量级的一致性正则化方法：首先通过stop-gradient锚定学生模型的无上下文输出，然后使用前向KL散度惩罚上下文条件输出与锚定输出之间的偏差。该方法每个训练步骤仅需额外一次前向传播。

Result: 在涵盖不同领域和模型家族的12种配置中，该方法在大多数设置下提高了上下文条件准确率，在11/12的设置中减少了上下文引发的性能损害，并有效消除了响应长度膨胀。机制案例研究进一步证实，上下文可移除性在表征层面得以实现，无论上下文是否存在，隐藏状态几乎保持一致。

Conclusion: 上下文可移除性（context removability）是评估知识内化质量的重要新标准，所提出的一致性正则化方法简单有效，能够在不增加复杂性的情况下同时提升无上下文性能和上下文条件稳定性。

Original Abstract: Recent work has shown that on-policy distillation can internalize privileged context, such as system prompts or task hints, into a student model so that the context is no longer needed at inference time. Although this approach successfully improves the student's no-context performance, we identify an interesting and previously unstudied phenomenon: in many settings, reintroducing the original privileged context to the distilled student actually degrades its performance, even on instances it already solves correctly without context. We term this context-induced degradation and argue that robust internalization demands not only matching the teacher's context-conditioned behavior, but also remaining stable when the context is reintroduced, a property we call context removability. Motivated by this observation, we propose a lightweight consistency regularizer that first anchors the student's no-context output via stop-gradient, then penalizes the context-conditioned output for deviating from it via forward KL divergence. This simple addition requires only one extra forward pass per training step, yet it effectively mitigates context-induced degradation and, in many cases, even improves no-context performance. Across 12 configurations spanning diverse domains and model families, our method improves context-conditioned accuracy in the majority of settings, reduces context-induced harm in 11 out of 12 settings, and effectively eliminates response-length inflation. A mechanistic case study further confirms that context removability is achieved at the representation level, with hidden states remaining nearly identical regardless of whether the context is present.

中文摘要翻译: 近期研究表明，策略蒸馏可以将特权上下文（如系统提示或任务提示）内化到学生模型中，使得推理时不再需要该上下文。虽然这种方法成功提升了学生的无上下文性能，但我们发现了一个有趣且此前未被研究过的现象：在许多设置下，将原始特权上下文重新引入蒸馏后的学生模型实际上会降低其性能，即使对那些无需上下文就能正确解决的实例也是如此。我们将此现象称为“上下文引发的性能退化”，并认为稳健的内化不仅需要匹配教师模型的上下文条件行为，还需要在上下文重新引入时保持稳定，我们将这一性质称为“上下文可移除性”。受此观察启发，我们提出了一种轻量级的一致性正则化方法：首先通过停止梯度（stop-gradient）锚定学生的无上下文输出，然后使用前向KL散度惩罚上下文条件输出偏离该锚定输出的程度。这一简单的改进每个训练步骤仅需额外一次前向传播，却能有效缓解上下文引发的性能退化，并且在许多情况下甚至能进一步提升无上下文性能。在涵盖不同领域和模型家族的12种配置中，我们的方法在大多数设置下提高了上下文条件准确率，在11/12的设置中减少了上下文引发的性能损害，并有效消除了响应长度膨胀。一项机制案例研究进一步证实，上下文可移除性在表征层面得以实现——无论上下文是否存在，隐藏状态几乎保持一致。

</details>


### [158] [TAROT: Task-Adaptive Refinement of LLM-prior Graphs for Few-shot Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11640)
*Ruxue Shi,Yili Wang,Mengnan Du,Hangting Ye,Yi Chang,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TAROT，一种基于GNN的少样本表格学习框架，通过构建和优化任务自适应语义图来建模特征间的语义关系，从而提升预测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有少样本表格学习方法存在两大问题：传统方法需额外训练导致计算开销大；LLM方法直接输入原始表格数据存在隐私合规风险。更重要的是，两类方法都忽略了特征间的语义关系，而这能提供构建语义图的结构和语义先验，对建模有意义的特征交互至关重要。

Method: TAROT包含三个核心模块：(1) 统一语义表格节点编码器(USTNE)，将异构表格数据编码为统一节点语义表示；(2) 基于任务描述和特征名提示LLM推断特征间语义关系，构建初始语义图；(3) 任务自适应语义图精炼模块，剪除由LLM幻觉引入的虚假或不相关边，并补充缺失的任务相关边，使图结构与下游目标对齐；最后GNN在精炼图上进行消息传递以捕获任务相关语义依赖进行预测。

Result: 在多个少样本表格学习基准上的大量实验表明，TAROT取得了优越性能，成为该领域的最先进方法。

Conclusion: TAROT通过编码结构和语义先验构建任务自适应语义图，有效解决了少样本表格学习中的特征关系建模问题，为少样本表格学习提供了新的SOTA方案。

Original Abstract: Few-shot tabular learning provides a cost-effective approach for real-world applications where annotation is costly and collecting sufficient samples for new tasks is difficult. Existing Traditional and LLM-based methods have demonstrated effectiveness in few-shot scenarios. However, traditional methods need additional training on unlabeled or generated data, which incur significant computational overhead. In addition, LLM-based methods that directly feed raw tabular data into LLMs raise privacy and compliance concerns. More importantly, both paradigms largely overlook the semantic relationships between features, which provide structural and semantic prior for constructing a semantic graph. Semantic graph is essential for modeling meaningful feature interactions in few-shot scenarios. In this paper, we propose TAROT, a GNN-based framework that encodes the structural and semantic prior by constructing and refining a task-adaptive semantic graph from this prior, thereby improving predictive performance in few-shot tabular learning. TAROT first encodes heterogeneous tabular data into unified node semantic representations via a Unified Semantic Tabular Node Encoder (USTNE). Then, it prompts LLMs to infer the semantic relationship between features based on the task description and feature names to construct a semantic graph. To mitigate structural noise introduced by the hallucination of LLMs, TAROT introduces Task-adaptive Semantic Graph Refinement that prunes spurious or task-unrelated edges and adds missing task-related ones, aligning the graph structure with the downstream objective. Finally, a GNN performs message passing over the refined graph to capture task-related semantic dependencies for prediction. Extensive experiments on various few-shot tabular learning benchmarks demonstrate the superior performance of TAROT, establishing it as a state-of-the-art approach in this domain.

中文摘要翻译: 少样本表格学习为标注成本高昂且难以收集足够样本的现实应用提供了一种经济高效的解决方案。现有的传统方法和基于LLM的方法在少样本场景中已展现出有效性。然而，传统方法需要在未标注或生成的数据上进行额外训练，这带来了显著的计算开销。此外，基于LLM的方法直接将原始表格数据输入LLM引发了隐私和合规性担忧。更重要的是，这两种范式都很大程度上忽略了特征之间的语义关系，而特征语义关系为构建语义图提供了结构和语义先验。语义图对于在少样本场景中建模有意义的特征交互至关重要。本文提出了TAROT，一个基于GNN的框架，通过从结构和语义先验中构建并优化任务自适应语义图来编码这些先验信息，从而提升少样本表格学习的预测性能。TAROT首先通过统一语义表格节点编码器(USTNE)将异构表格数据编码为统一的节点语义表示。然后，它提示LLM根据任务描述和特征名称推断特征间的语义关系，构建语义图。为缓解LLM幻觉引入的结构噪声，TAROT引入了任务自适应语义图精炼机制，剪除虚假或任务无关的边，并补充缺失的任务相关边，使图结构与下游目标对齐。最后，GNN在精炼后的图上进行消息传递，捕获任务相关的语义依赖以进行预测。在多个少样本表格学习基准上的大量实验证明了TAROT的优越性能，使其成为该领域的最先进方法。

</details>


### [159] [Tree-Structured Orthonormal Decomposition of the Aitchison Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11646)
*Daisuke Yamada,Qijun Zhang,Travis Pence,Barbara B. Bendlin,Federico Rey,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyILR提出了一种与任意树拓扑结构对齐的Aitchison切空间规范正交分解方法，在微生物组和单细胞基准测试中生成稳定、可解释的多尺度特征，并与softmax分类器建立了理论联系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 成分数据（如微生物组、基因组数据）的特征通常具有已知的层次结构（如分类树、系统发育树），但现有方法要么忽略这种结构，要么丢弃内在的Aitchison几何性质，要么仅适用于二叉树，要么生成不完整的坐标系。

Method: PolyILR在每个内部节点定义加权局部几何以捕获完整分支结构，然后将这些局部几何提升为全局标准正交基，使得每个坐标对应树上的特定位置，从而构建与任意树拓扑对齐的规范正交分解。

Result: 在微生物组和单细胞基准测试中，PolyILR生成稳定且可解释的特征，支持多尺度树分辨率下的推理，并与softmax分类器建立了新的理论联系。

Conclusion: PolyILR提供了一种与任意树拓扑对齐的规范正交分解方法，既保留了Aitchison几何结构，又支持多尺度层次化分析，有望应用于概率建模。

Original Abstract: Compositional data -- vectors encoding relative proportions -- arise across scientific domains, including ecology, geochemistry, and genomics. The features in these data often come with known hierarchical structure (e.g., taxonomies, phylogenies, ontologies), yet existing methods either ignore this structure, discard the intrinsic Aitchison geometry, are designed for binary trees, or yield incomplete coordinate systems. We describe PolyILR, a canonical orthonormal decomposition of the Aitchison tangent space aligned with any tree topology. Our construction defines a weighted local geometry at each internal node capturing full branching structure, then lifts these to a global orthonormal basis where every coordinate corresponds to a specific tree location. On microbiome and single-cell benchmarks, PolyILR yields stable, interpretable features and enables inference at multiscale tree resolution. We also establish a novel theoretical connection to softmax classifiers, suggesting possible applications to probabilistic modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 成分数据——编码相对比例的向量——出现在包括生态学、地球化学和基因组学在内的科学领域中。这些数据中的特征通常具有已知的层次结构（如分类学、系统发育学、本体论），然而现有方法要么忽略这种结构，要么丢弃内在的Aitchison几何性质，要么仅适用于二叉树，要么生成不完整的坐标系。我们描述了PolyILR，一种与任意树拓扑对齐的Aitchison切空间的规范正交分解。我们的构造在每个内部节点定义了捕获完整分支结构的加权局部几何，然后将这些局部几何提升为全局标准正交基，其中每个坐标对应树上的特定位置。在微生物组和单细胞基准测试中，PolyILR生成稳定、可解释的特征，并支持多尺度树分辨率下的推理。我们还建立了与softmax分类器的新理论联系，暗示了其在概率建模中的潜在应用。

</details>


### [160] [Structure-Preserving Neural Surrogates with Tractable Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11650)
*Handi Zhang,Adrienne M. Propp,Brooks Kinch,Houman Owhadi,Nathaniel Trask*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于外微积分和混合有限元空间的GP回归方法，构建结构保持的降阶模型，实现具有封闭形式后验不确定性的实时偏微分方程求解器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 科学机器学习虽能近实时求解PDE，但缺乏传统仿真器的理论支撑，无法支撑现代验证与确认（V&V）。本文旨在构建兼具物理结构保持和不确定性量化能力的实时代理模型。

Method: 在Raviart-Thomas和dgP0元构成的H(div)-L²子空间上，由轻量Transformer约束降阶动力学；将守恒律建模为GP描述体积间通量；将训练构造为带等式约束的最优恢复问题（ORP），通过Schur补策略高效求解；最终得到边界通量的封闭形式后验估计，用于Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射。

Result: 给出了线性泛函的RKHS后验误差界以支持不确定性量化；数值实验证明了后验分布作为误差估计代理的准确性。

Conclusion: 混合有限元空间与GP回归之间的新型接口，结合ORP的等式约束优化训练，实现了结构保持的实时降阶模型，具备严格的不确定性量化能力。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in scientific machine learning provide a means of near-real-time solution to partial differential equations (PDEs), but lack the theoretical underpinnings of conventional simulators that support contemporary verification and validation. In this work, we construct data-driven reduced-order models that serve as structure-preserving, real-time surrogates. Remarkably, the exterior calculus that imposes physical conservation structure also exposes topological structure that we use to build a Gaussian process (GP) representation of uncertainty in state-flux relationships, ultimately yielding a Dirichlet-to-Neumann map for quantities of interest with closed-form expressions for posterior uncertainty. We specifically propose structure-preserving $H(\mathrm{div})$--$L^2$ subspaces of conventional Raviart--Thomas and $dgP_0$ elements prescribed by a lightweight transformer. Reduced-order dynamics consistent with this subspace are learned by posing a conservation law in which a GP describes the fluxes between volumes. This work hinges on a novel interface between mixed FEM spaces and GP regression; when training is posed as the optimal recovery problem (ORP), the resulting GP regression can be written as an optimization problem with equality constraints that impose a conservation structure, amenable to a fast Schur-complement training strategy. The trained model can then be solved in real time with closed-form estimators for boundary fluxes driven by prescribed Dirichlet data. The paper includes RKHS posterior error bounds for linear functionals to support uncertainty quantification, as well as numerical experiments demonstrating the accuracy of the posterior distribution as a surrogate for error estimation.

中文摘要翻译: 科学机器学习的最新进展为偏微分方程（PDE）的近实时求解提供了途径，但这些方法缺乏传统仿真器所具备的理论基础，而后者支撑着当代的验证与确认工作。本文构建了数据驱动的降阶模型，作为保持结构的实时代理。值得注意的是，强加物理守恒结构的外微积分同时揭示了拓扑结构，我们利用该拓扑结构构建了状态-通量关系中不确定性的高斯过程（GP）表示，最终导出感兴趣量的Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射，并附带后验不确定性的封闭形式表达式。我们具体提出由轻量Transformer约束的传统Raviart-Thomas和dgP₀元上的结构保持H(div)-L²子空间。通过构建一个守恒律来学习与该子空间一致的降阶动力学，其中GP描述体积间的通量。本文的核心贡献在于混合有限元空间与GP回归之间的新型接口；当训练被构造为最优恢复问题（ORP）时，所得GP回归可写为带有强加守恒结构的等式约束的优化问题，这适用于快速的Schur补训练策略。训练后的模型可实时求解，并针对给定的Dirichlet数据驱动的边界通量给出封闭形式估计器。本文给出了线性泛函的RKHS后验误差界以支持不确定性量化，以及证明后验分布作为误差估计代理的准确性的数值实验。

</details>


### [161] [DeepRHP: A Hybrid Variational Autoencoder for Designing Random Heteropolymers as Protein Mimics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11651)
*Shuni Li,Zhiyuan Ruan,Andy Shen,Ivan Jayapurna,Ting Xu,Haiyan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepRHP，一种基于半监督变分自编码器的混合模型，用于指导合成随机杂聚物（RHP）设计，使其能模拟蛋白质行为，并通过预测稳定膜蛋白的单体组成验证了模型有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 合成随机杂聚物（RHP）有潜力模拟蛋白质行为与功能，但缺乏高效的计算工具来指导RHP的设计，因此需要开发一种能有效捕捉化学特征和序列模式的计算方法。

Method: DeepRHP是一种改进的变分自编码器（VAE）模型，采用半监督学习框架。它在经典VAE基础上增加了一个基于特征的VAE，使隐空间能够同时捕捉关键化学特征的结构信息和单个RHP的序列模式，从而以混合方式整合任意相关特征。

Result: DeepRHP成功预测了能够稳定膜蛋白（如水通道蛋白Z）在非原生环境中的潜在单体组成，并与已发表的研究结果交叉验证，模型预测与真实RHP功能之间具有良好一致性。

Conclusion: 混合自编码器架构在指导蛋白质及其他生物化合物的RHP设计方面具有强大潜力，DeepRHP可作为有效的计算工具辅助RHP设计。

Original Abstract: Synthetic random heteropolymers (RHPs), consisting of a predefined set of monomers, offer an approach toward the design of protein-like materials. These RHPs, if designed appropriately, can mimic protein behavior and function. As such, there is a need for computational tools to efficiently guide RHP design. We bridge this gap by developing DeepRHP, a modified variational autoencoder (VAE) model under a semi-supervised framework. By equipping a classical VAE with an additional feature-based VAE, DeepRHP forces the latent space to capture structures of critical chemical features as well as individual RHP sequence patterns. In this sense, our method is versatile by allowing any relevant features to be incorporated in a hybrid manner. We demonstrate the effectiveness of DeepRHP by suggesting potential monomer compositions that stabilize membrane proteins (e.g. Aquaporin Z) in non-native environments and cross-validating our prediction with published results. The concordance between our model and true RHP function suggests strong potential in utilizing hybrid autoencoder architectures to guide RHP design for proteins and other biological compounds.

中文摘要翻译: 合成随机杂聚物（RHPs）由一组预定义的单体组成，为设计类蛋白质材料提供了一种途径。如果设计得当，这些RHPs可以模仿蛋白质的行为和功能。因此，需要开发计算工具来有效指导RHP设计。我们通过开发DeepRHP——一种在半监督框架下改进的变分自编码器（VAE）模型——弥补了这一差距。通过在经典VAE中增加一个基于特征的VAE，DeepRHP迫使隐空间同时捕捉关键化学特征的结构信息以及单个RHP的序列模式。从这个意义上说，我们的方法具有通用性，允许以混合方式融入任何相关特征。我们通过预测在非原生环境中稳定膜蛋白（例如水通道蛋白Z）的潜在单体组成，并与已发表的结果进行交叉验证，证明了DeepRHP的有效性。模型预测与真实RHP功能之间的一致性表明，利用混合自编码器架构来指导蛋白质及其他生物化合物的RHP设计具有巨大潜力。

</details>


### [162] [IAPO: Input Attribution-Aware Policy Optimization for Tool Use in Small Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11652)
*Yifan Yang,Zhen Zhang,Jiayi Tian,Liyan Tan,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出输入归因感知策略优化（IAPO），一种通过对齐学生模型与教师模型在输入组件上的归因来改进多模态小语言模型（SLM）工具调用能力的强化学习算法，在Qwen2.5-VL-3B上平均提升视觉问答准确率3%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工具调用奖励设计存在两个根本局限：一是通过精确匹配评估工具使用正确性，这在多模态任务中往往不适用，因为可能存在多条有效工具调用路径且缺乏标注轨迹；二是稀疏的二元奖励对底层决策过程缺乏改进指导，使多模态SLM难以从中学习。

Method: 提出输入归因感知策略优化（IAPO），一种强化学习算法，通过将模型在输入组件（如文本、视觉区域）上的归因对齐到更强的教师模型的归因，来改进多模态SLM的工具使用能力。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-3B模型上，相比现有视觉工具调用方法，IAPO在六个测试集上的视觉问答准确率平均提升3%，其原因是帮助模型关注最相关的输入证据。

Conclusion: 通过归因对齐的强化学习方法能有效克服多模态SLM在工具调用训练中面临的稀疏奖励和缺乏标注轨迹等困难，显著提升模型性能。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates reinforcement learning (RL) methods for improving tool-calling capabilities in multimodal small language model (SLM) agents. While existing works have explored various reward designs to improve agentic tool-calling ability, these approaches face inherent limitations for SLM training, especially under multimodal scenarios. First, many existing methods evaluate tool use correctness through exact matching against certain ground-truth or predefined formats. However, this assumption is often unsuitable for multimodal tasks, where multiple tool use paths may be valid and annotated tool trajectories are typically unavailable. Second, such sparse and brittle binary rewards provide little guidance on how to improve the underlying decision process, making them particularly difficult for multimodal SLM to learn from. To address these issues, we propose Input Attribution-Aware Policy Optimization (IAPO), an RL algorithm for improving tool use in multimodal SLM by aligning the model's attribution across input components with that of a stronger teacher. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B show that the proposed method improves visual question answering accuracy by an average of 3% across six test sets compared with existing visual tool use work, by helping the model attend to the most relevant input evidence.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了用于提升多模态小语言模型（SLM）智能体工具调用能力的强化学习方法。现有工作虽然探索了各种奖励设计来改进智能体的工具调用能力，但这些方法在SLM训练中面临固有局限性，尤其是在多模态场景下。首先，许多现有方法通过精确匹配特定真实标注或预定义格式来评估工具使用正确性。然而，这种假设在多模态任务中往往不适用，因为可能存在多条有效的工具调用路径，且标注的工具轨迹通常不可用。其次，这种稀疏且脆弱的二元奖励对底层决策过程几乎无法提供改进指导，使得多模态SLM尤其难以从中学习。为了解决这些问题，我们提出输入归因感知策略优化（IAPO），一种通过将模型在输入组件上的归因与更强的教师模型对齐，来改进多模态SLM工具使用的强化学习算法。在Qwen2.5-VL-3B上的实验表明，与现有的视觉工具调用工作相比，所提方法通过帮助模型关注最相关的输入证据，在六个测试集上的视觉问答准确率平均提升3%。

</details>


### [163] [Sparse probes and murky physics: a case study of interpretability challenges in a foundation model for continuum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11657)
*Katherine Rosenfeld,Maike Sonnewald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）和物理启发的度量（enstrophy）对连续介质动力学基础模型Walrus进行机械可解释性分析，发现其内部特征呈现片段一致性但在标准物理分解上不清晰，并将输出偏差与特定特征使用变化相关联。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在已有强理论、基准和物理直觉的科学领域，生成式AI仿真器被广泛使用，但关键问题在于：这类基础模型如何通过内部机制复现已知的连续介质动力学，其内部行为是否与已知物理一致，以及内部表示与模型成功或失败之间的关系。

Method: 以Polymathic团队的Walrus模型为研究对象，采用机械可解释性方法，使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）探测选定层，以enstrophy（涡度拟能）作为物理引导度量对超过20,000个特征进行筛选和优先级排序；以剪切流为简单测试床，比较多个剪切流设置下的特征募集情况。

Result: 不同设置下特征呈现片段一致性（部分特征在相似角色中重复出现），但该结构是间歇性的，无法清晰映射到标准物理分解；数值模拟与仿真器的直接对比揭示了系统性的输出级差异（能量/结构过于扩散或过于局域化），部分差异可与特定SAE特征使用变化相关联。

Conclusion: 该工作突显了科学基础模型的若干开放问题：如何稳健地优先选择具有机械意义的特征、如何将稳定结构与分析伪影（包括单层和SAE的局限性）分离，以及如何利用已有基准判断"不同"的内部表示何时真正具有信息价值而不仅仅是有效。

Original Abstract: Generative AI emulators are increasingly used in scientific domains where we already have strong theory, benchmarks, and physical intuition. This raises a central evaluation and interpretability question: when a foundation-style model can reproduce known continuum dynamics, what internal mechanism supports that behavior, is the internal behaviour consistent with known physics, and how does it relate to where the emulator succeeds or fails? We investigate a cross-domain foundation model for continuum dynamics, Walrus by Polymathic, using mechanistic interpretability guided by physical principles. We apply a sparse autoencoder (SAE) to probe a selected layer, and address the practical challenge of triaging a large feature set (over 20,000) using enstrophy as a physically grounded metric. As a deliberately simple testbed, we focus on shear flow and compare feature recruitment across multiple shear-flow setups, i.e. parameter values in the numerical simulation. Across setups we find evidence of piecewise consistency, with subsets of features recurring in similar roles, but this structure is intermittent and does not map cleanly onto standard physical decompositions. In parallel, direct comparisons between numerical simulation and the emulator reveal systematic output-level discrepancies, including regimes where energy/structures become too diffuse or too localized. We connect parts of these discrepancies to changes in specific SAE feature usage. Our work highlights open questions for scientific foundation models: how to robustly prioritize mechanistically meaningful features, how to separate stable structure from analysis artifacts (including single-layer and SAE limitations), and how to use established benchmarks to decide when "different" internal representations are genuinely informative rather than merely effective.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式人工智能仿真器越来越多地应用于我们已具备强大理论、基准和物理直觉的科学领域。这引发了一个核心的评估与可解释性问题：当一个基础风格模型能够复现已知的连续介质动力学时，其内部支持该行为的机制是什么？内部行为是否与已知物理一致？以及这种机制与仿真器的成功或失败之间的联系是什么？我们研究了Polymathic团队开发的一个跨领域连续介质动力学基础模型Walrus，采用物理原则引导的机械可解释性方法。我们使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）探测选定层，并以涡度拟能（enstrophy）作为物理引导度量，解决了对超过20,000个特征集进行筛选的实际挑战。作为刻意简化的测试平台，我们聚焦于剪切流，比较了多个剪切流设置（即数值模拟中的参数值）下的特征募集情况。在不同设置下，我们发现片段一致性的证据，部分特征在相似角色中重复出现，但这种结构是间歇性的，并不能清晰映射到标准物理分解上。与此同时，数值模拟与仿真器之间的直接对比揭示了系统性的输出级差异，包括能量/结构变得过于扩散或过于局域化的区域。我们将部分差异与特定SAE特征使用的变化联系起来。我们的工作突显了科学基础模型的开放问题：如何稳健地优先选择具有机械意义的特征，如何将稳定结构与分析伪影（包括单层和SAE的局限性）分离，以及如何利用已有基准判断"不同"的内部表示何时真正具有信息价值而不仅仅是有效。

</details>


### [164] [Bergson: An Open Source Library for Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11660)
*Lucia Quirke,Louis Jaburi,David Johnston,William Z. Li,Gonçalo Paulo,Guillaume Martres,Girish Gupta,Stella Biderman,Nora Belrose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bergson 是一个开源库，为大规模语言模型和预训练数据集提供多种数据归因方法，支持分布式训练和磁盘梯度存储，并首次开源实现了 MAGIC、SOURCE 和 TrackStar 三种前沿方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 数据归因技术在可解释性方面潜力巨大，但在大规模应用中需要大量工程投入，且许多前沿技术缺乏开源工具支持，阻碍了该领域的快速进展。

Method: 开发了 Bergson 开源库，原生支持磁盘梯度存储和多节点分布式训练，提供研究者友好的工具，并首次开源实现了 MAGIC、SOURCE 和 TrackStar 三种数据归因方法。

Result: 该库能够扩展至非常大的语言模型和预训练数据集，首次提供了三种前沿数据归因方法的开源实现。

Conclusion: Bergson 通过提供可扩展的开源工具，能够加速数据归因领域的研究进展，降低工程门槛，促进该领域更广泛的探索和应用。

Original Abstract: Data attribution is a promising field in interpretability that aims to explain model behavior through the influence of its training data, with applications including debugging undesirable model behavior and training dataset curation. However, significant engineering effort is required to perform it at scale, and many cutting edge techniques lack open-source tooling and support. Bergson is an open source library that aims to enable faster progress in the field by providing a host of techniques that scale to very large language models and pre-training datasets. The library natively supports on-disk gradient stores and multi-node distributed training, and provides quality of life tools for researchers. Finally, we introduce the first open-source implementations of three leading data attribution methods: MAGIC, SOURCE, and TrackStar. The library is available at https://github.com/EleutherAI/bergson .

中文摘要翻译: 数据归因是可解释性领域的一个前景广阔的方向，旨在通过训练数据的影响来解释模型行为，其应用包括调试不良模型行为以及训练数据集的整理。然而，在大规模执行数据归因时需要大量的工程投入，且许多前沿技术缺乏开源工具和支持。Bergson 是一个开源库，旨在通过提供一系列可扩展至非常大的语言模型和预训练数据集的技术来加速该领域的进展。该库原生支持磁盘梯度存储和多节点分布式训练，并为研究者提供生活质量提升工具。最后，我们首次开源实现了三种领先的数据归因方法：MAGIC、SOURCE 和 TrackStar。该库可在 https://github.com/EleutherAI/bergson 获取。

</details>


### [165] [Spectrally Regularized Latent Flow Matching for Turbulence Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11691)
*Khalid Rafiq,Aditya G. Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种频谱正则化的潜流匹配框架，通过在压缩阶段使用带权重的对数谱损失函数，显著提升了湍流生成中耗散范围幅度的表示精度，将高波数保留功率从25%提升至94%，并改善了采样效率与保真度之间的权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有潜扩散和流匹配方法在合成湍流生成中系统性地低估了耗散范围的振幅，这是一个关键失败模式，亟需解决。

Method: 提出潜流匹配框架，在压缩阶段引入频谱正则化：用带区域权重的对数谱目标函数替代MSE训练的VAE，以提升耗散范围的重建保真度。

Result: 在Re_f≈2250的256² DNS数据集上：重建耗散范围保留功率从25%提升至94%，无条件生成从20%提升至79%；DD偏差从-0.70降至-0.117（仅需20次函数评估）；编码器-解码器交换实验表明改进主要来自编码器诱导的潜空间重组；两种管线均恢复二阶结构函数和S₃的正确符号。

Conclusion: 频谱正则化的潜空间能打破MSE训练带来的质量天花板，显著提升湍流生成 fidelity；但S₃幅度的残余差距表明相位相干三元组组织仍是未来生成模型需要补充优化的方向。

Original Abstract: Latent diffusion and flow matching have emerged as leading approaches for synthetic turbulence generation, yet they systematically under-represent dissipation-range amplitudes. We introduce a latent flow matching framework with a spectrally regularized compression stage that directly targets this failure mode. On a 256^2 DNS dataset at Re_f \approx 2250, replacing an MSE-trained VAE with a zone-weighted log-spectral objective raises deep-dissipation retained spectral power from 25% to 94% in reconstruction and from 20% to 79% in unconditional generation. The improved latent representation also yields a substantially better sampling cost-fidelity tradeoff: the MSE-trained latent space imposes a fundamental quality ceiling near DD bias -0.70 that no integrator or step-count can overcome, while the spectrally regularized latent space reaches DD bias -0.117 at just 20 function evaluations. Mechanistically, encoder-decoder swap experiments show that the improvement is driven primarily by encoder-induced latent reorganization rather than decoder capacity, while a support-amplitude decomposition reveals that MSE-trained models behave as conservative suppression models, minimizing pointwise error by attenuating intermittent high-wavenumber structure. Both pipelines recover the second-order structure function and the correct sign of S_3, indicating the correct cascade direction without explicit supervision. A small residual gap in the magnitude of S_3 suggests that phase-coherent triadic organization remains a complementary axis to amplitude fidelity for future generative turbulence models.

中文摘要翻译: 潜扩散模型和流匹配已成为合成湍流生成的主要方法，但它们系统地低估了耗散范围的振幅。我们引入了一个潜流匹配框架，其压缩阶段采用了频谱正则化，直接针对这一失败模式。在Re_f≈2250的256² DNS数据集上，用带区域权重的对数谱目标函数替换MSE训练的VAE，使深层耗散范围的保留频谱功率在重建中从25%提升至94%，在无条件生成中从20%提升至79%。改进的潜表示还带来了显著更优的采样成本-保真度权衡：MSE训练的潜空间在DD偏差约-0.70处存在无法通过任何积分器或步数克服的根本质量天花板，而频谱正则化的潜空间仅需20次函数评估即可达到DD偏差-0.117。机制上，编码器-解码器交换实验表明，改进主要由编码器诱导的潜空间重组驱动，而非解码器容量；而支撑-振幅分解揭示了MSE训练的模型表现为保守抑制模型，通过衰减间歇性高波数结构来最小化逐点误差。两种管线均恢复了二阶结构函数和S₃的正确符号，表明无需显式监督即可获得正确的级联方向。S₃幅值存在的微小残留差距表明，对于未来的生成式湍流模型，相位相干三元组组织仍是幅度保真度之外的一个互补优化轴。

</details>


### [166] [Noise-Aware Framework for Correcting Corrupted Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11695)
*Ha-Linh Nguyen,Hong-Anh Nguyen,Minh-Duc La,Phong Lam,Thu-Trang Nguyen,Son Nguyen,Hieu Dinh Vo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CANOLA是一种通过噪声感知学习和迭代软标签精炼来纠正数据集中错误标签的新框架，在六个数据集上相比SOTA方法实现了19%-52%的错误率降低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实世界数据集常包含大量错误标签，严重损害模型性能，需要有效的标签纠正方法来提升训练数据的质量。

Method: 提出CANOLA框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 噪声感知学习，显式估计数据集的噪声分布并融入深度神经网络训练，使模型降低不可靠监督信号的权重；(2) 迭代软标签精炼，将模型预测与观测标签谨慎融合，以渐进稳定的方式修复标签。

Result: 在六个广泛使用的数据集上，CANOLA在错误率降低方面相对SOTA方法提升19%-52%。经CANOLA纠正后的数据训练出的模型在下游任务中表现显著提升，简单分类器在纠正数据上的表现甚至优于复杂模型中心方法达67%。

Conclusion: CANOLA通过噪声感知学习与渐进式软标签精炼相结合，有效纠正错误标签，显著提升模型鲁棒性和泛化能力，是一种高效实用的标签纠正框架。

Original Abstract: High-quality labeled data is essential for training reliable ML/DL models. However, real-world datasets often contain a considerable proportion of corrupted labels, which can severely degrade model performance. To address this problem, we propose CANOLA, a novel framework for correcting corrupted labels through noise-aware learning and iterative label refinement. CANOLA explicitly estimates the underlying noise distribution of the dataset and incorporates this information into the training of a noise-aware Deep Neural Network. By incorporating noise characteristics during learning, CANOLA enables the model to down-weight unreliable supervision signals and focus on trustworthy patterns, thereby improving robustness and generalization. Label correction is performed via cautious, iterative soft label refinement, in which model predictions are blended with observed labels to prevent premature or erroneous updates. This progressive refinement allows the dataset to be repaired in a stable and controlled manner. We evaluate CANOLA on six widely used datasets under realistic noisy labeling scenarios. Experimental results show that CANOLA consistently outperforms SOTA label correction methods, achieving relative improvements ranging from 19% to 52% in error reduction. Moreover, models trained on datasets corrected by CANOLA obtain substantial downstream performance gains. Even simple classifiers trained on CANOLA's corrected data can outperform complex model-centric approaches by margins of up to 67%.

中文摘要翻译: 高质量的标注数据对于训练可靠的机器学习和深度学习模型至关重要。然而，真实世界的数据集通常包含相当比例的错误标签，这会严重损害模型性能。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了CANOLA，一种通过噪声感知学习和迭代标签精炼来纠正错误标签的新框架。CANOLA显式地估计数据集的潜在噪声分布，并将该信息融入噪声感知深度神经网络的训练中。通过在训练过程中融合噪声特征，CANOLA使模型能够降低不可靠监督信号的权重并关注可信模式，从而提升鲁棒性和泛化能力。标签纠正通过谨慎的迭代软标签精炼来实现，其中模型预测与观测标签进行融合，以防止过早或错误的更新。这种渐进式精炼使得数据集能够以稳定可控的方式得到修复。我们在六个广泛使用的数据集上，在现实噪声标注场景下评估了CANOLA。实验结果表明，CANOLA在错误率降低方面始终优于最先进的标签纠正方法，实现了19%到52%的相对提升。此外，在经CANOLA纠正后的数据集上训练的模型在下游任务上获得了显著的性能提升。即使是在CANOLA纠正数据上训练的简单分类器，其表现也能超过复杂的以模型为中心的方法，提升幅度高达67%。

</details>


### [167] [A Data-Centric Framework for Detecting and Correcting Corrupted Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11699)
*Ha-Linh Nguyen,Hong-Anh Nguyen,Minh-Duc La,Thu-Trang Nguyen,Son Nguyen,Hieu Dinh Vo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Relabeler，一个端到端的数据中心框架，利用局部和全局关系检测并修正训练数据中的错误标签，在多个数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实世界数据集中的噪声标签会严重降低机器学习/深度学习模型的准确性和可靠性，亟需有效的方法来检测和纠正这些被破坏的标签。

Method: Relabeler是一个端到端的数据中心框架，包含两个核心步骤：(1) 联合利用数据实例间的局部和全局关系来检测潜在噪声样本；(2) 基于输入特征和观测到的噪声标签为每个实例估计最可能的干净标签，进行标签修正。

Result: 在多个数据集、噪声类型和噪声率下，Relabeler在标签修正精度上最高提升58%，在下游任务性能上提升6%，一致优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: Relabeler通过端到端的标签检测与修正机制，有效提升了模型在噪声标签下的训练质量与下游性能，具有广泛的适用性和优越性。

Original Abstract: The performance of machine learning and deep learning models largely depends on the quality of the training data. However, the quality of the real-world datasets is often compromised by noisy labels, which can substantially degrade model accuracy and reliability. To address this challenge, we propose Relabeler, an end-to-end data-centric framework for detecting and correcting corrupted labels. For corrupted label detection, Relabeler jointly leverages both local and global relationships among data instances to identify potentially noisy samples. After detecting suspicious instances, Relabeler further performs label correction by estimating the most probable clean label for each instance based on both its input features and observed noisy label. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, noise types, and noise rates demonstrate that Relabeler consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 58% improvement in label correction precision and 6% improvement in downstream task performance.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习与深度学习模型的性能在很大程度上取决于训练数据的质量。然而，真实世界数据集的质量常常因噪声标签而受损，这会显著降低模型的准确性和可靠性。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了Relabeler——一个用于检测和纠正被破坏标签的端到端数据中心框架。在检测被破坏标签时，Relabeler联合利用数据实例之间的局部和全局关系来识别潜在的噪声样本。在检测出可疑实例后，Relabeler进一步通过基于每个实例的输入特征和观测到的噪声标签来估计最可能的干净标签，从而进行标签修正。跨多个数据集、噪声类型和噪声率的广泛实验表明，Relabeler一致优于最先进的基线方法，在标签修正精度上实现了最高58%的提升，在下游任务性能上实现了6%的提升。

</details>


### [168] [RLCSD: Reinforcement Learning with Contrastive On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11709)
*Leyi Pan,Shuchang Tao,Yunpeng Zhai,Lingzhe Zhang,Zhaoyang Liu,Bolin Ding,Aiwei Liu,Lijie Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中，特权上下文导致的分布差异信号集中于风格标记而非任务标记，引发“特权诱导的风格漂移”并导致训练不稳定或输出长度萎缩。为此提出RLCSD，通过对比正确提示与错误提示下的师生差距来抑制风格偏移，使信号更聚焦于任务承载标记，在数学和逻辑推理任务上优于GRPO和现有OPSD方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有同策略自蒸馏方法通过特权上下文（如已验证的解决方案）为推理模型提供稠密的token级监督，但本文发现这种分布差距产生的学习信号集中在风格标记上，而非任务承载标记，因为被提示的模型倾向于输出更直接、更短的文本。这种“特权诱导的风格漂移”会导致训练不稳定或响应长度萎缩。

Method: 提出RLCSD（对比同策略自蒸馏强化学习），通过对比正确提示下的师生差距与错误提示下的师生差距，抑制因条件化于提示而无论正确与否都会诱发的风格偏移，从而提取出更集中于任务承载标记的学习信号。

Result: 在Qwen3（1.7B/4B/8B）和Olmo-3-7B-Think上的数学与逻辑推理实验中，RLCSD在性能上一致优于GRPO和已有的OPSD方法。

Conclusion: 对比原则具有通用性：可以嵌入现有OPSD方法以改进它们，其核心洞察还能扩展到更广泛的跨模型同策略蒸馏场景。

Original Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides dense, token-level supervision for reasoning models by aligning a model's own distribution with the distribution it produces under privileged context, typically a verified solution. However, we show that the learning signal drawn from this distributional gap concentrates on style tokens rather than task-bearing ones, as the hinted model tends to produce more direct, shorter outputs. We term this pathology \emph{privilege-induced style drift}, which destabilizes training or causes response length to shrink. To address this, we propose \textbf{RLCSD} (Reinforcement Learning with Contrastive on-policy Self-Distillation), which mitigates this drift by contrasting the teacher-student gap under a correct hint against that under a wrong hint, suppressing the style shift that conditioning on a hint tends to induce regardless of correctness, and yielding a signal that is more concentrated on task-bearing tokens. Experiments on Qwen3 (1.7B/4B/8B) and Olmo-3-7B-Think across mathematical and logical reasoning show that RLCSD consistently outperforms GRPO and prior OPSD methods. We further show that the contrastive principle is general: it plugs into existing OPSD methods to improve them, and its underlying insight extends to the broader cross-model on-policy distillation setting.

中文摘要翻译: 同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）通过将模型自身的分布与其在特权上下文（通常是一个已验证的解决方案）下产生的分布对齐，为推理模型提供稠密的token级监督。然而，我们发现从这种分布差距中提取的学习信号集中于风格标记而非任务承载标记，因为被提示的模型倾向于产生更直接、更短的输出。我们将这种病态现象称为“特权诱导的风格漂移”，它会破坏训练稳定性或导致响应长度萎缩。为解决此问题，我们提出RLCSD（对比同策略自蒸馏强化学习），该方法通过对比正确提示下的师生差距与错误提示下的师生差距来缓解风格漂移，抑制了因条件化于提示而无论正确与否都会诱发的风格偏移，从而得到更集中于任务承载标记的信号。在Qwen3（1.7B/4B/8B）和Olmo-3-7B-Think上的数学与逻辑推理实验中，RLCSD一致优于GRPO和先前的OPSD方法。我们进一步表明，对比原则具有通用性：它可以嵌入现有OPSD方法以改进它们，其核心洞察还能扩展到更广泛的跨模型同策略蒸馏设置中。

</details>


### [169] [Capacity-Constrained Online Convex Optimization with Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11711)
*Alexander Ryabchenko,Idan Attias,Daniel M. Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有硬容量约束的延迟在线凸优化问题，即最多只能跟踪C个未完成的延迟轮次。通过将问题归约到一种新的"延迟加权"OCO问题，并设计随机调度器与延迟加权FTRL算法，在凸损失和强凸损失下首次给出了容量约束OCO的遗憾界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有延迟在线学习假设学习者可以跟踪所有未完成的延迟轮次，但实际中跟踪资源有限，未被跟踪的反馈会永久丢失。因此需要研究在硬容量约束下如何设计算法并保证亚线性遗憾。

Method: 引入半透视模型来刻画延迟信息；通过归约将原问题转化为"延迟加权"OCO问题，设计随机调度器对跟踪决策进行随机化并对观测结果进行重要性加权；针对基础问题提出延迟加权FTRL及其赌博机版本。

Result: 首次给出容量约束OCO在凸损失和强凸损失下的遗憾界；一阶反馈下容量C=Ω(log T)足以恢复标准延迟OCO的速率（至多对数因子）；赌博机反馈下遗憾率由(1+σ_max/C)的幂次调节，当C<σ_max时遗憾界优雅退化，仍保持亚线性。

Conclusion: 该工作为资源受限场景下的延迟在线优化提供了理论保证，揭示了容量约束与延迟反馈之间的相互作用机制。

Original Abstract: Online learning with delayed feedback typically assumes that the learner can track all pending rounds until their feedback arrives. In practice, tracking resources are finite, and feedback from untracked rounds is permanently lost. In this paper, we study delayed online convex optimization (OCO) under a hard capacity constraint, where at most $C$ pending rounds can be tracked at any time. To model delay information, we introduce a semi-clairvoyant model that refines the clairvoyant assumption from prior work: rather than requiring delays to be known at prediction time, the learner observes delay expirations online, consistent with the classical unconstrained delayed setting. Our approach proceeds via a reduction to a novel ``delayed and weighted'' OCO problem, using a scheduler that randomizes tracking decisions and importance-weights the resulting observations. For this base problem, we propose and analyze Delayed-Weighted FTRL and its bandit analogue, establishing regret bounds that explicitly characterize the interaction between time-varying weights and delayed feedback. Combining these base learners with our schedulers yields the first regret guarantees for capacity-constrained OCO under convex and strongly convex losses, for both first-order and bandit feedback. For first-order feedback, capacity $C = Ω(\log T)$ suffices to recover standard delayed OCO rates up to logarithmic factors. For bandit feedback, the regret rates are modulated by powers of $(1 + σ_{\text{max}}/C)$, where $σ_{\text{max}}$ is the maximum number of pending observations at any time. This allows the regret bound to degrade gracefully when $C < σ_{\text{max}}$, while remaining sublinear.

中文摘要翻译: 具有延迟反馈的在线学习通常假设学习器可以跟踪所有未完成的轮次直至其反馈到达。然而在实践中，跟踪资源是有限的，未跟踪轮次的反馈会永久丢失。本文研究了硬容量约束下的延迟在线凸优化（OCO）问题，其中任意时刻最多只能跟踪C个未完成的轮次。为建模延迟信息，我们引入了一种半透视模型，该模型细化了先前工作中的透视假设：学习器不需要在预测时知晓延迟，而是在线观测到延迟到期，这与经典的无约束延迟设置一致。我们的方法通过一种归约进行，将原问题转化为一种新颖的"延迟加权"OCO问题，并使用一个随机化跟踪决策并对观测结果进行重要性加权的调度器。针对这一基础问题，我们提出并分析了延迟加权FTRL及其赌博机版本，建立了明确刻画时变权重与延迟反馈之间相互作用的遗憾界。将这些基础学习器与我们的调度器相结合，首次为一阶反馈和赌博机反馈下凸损失和强凸损失的容量约束OCO提供了遗憾保证。对于一阶反馈，容量C=Ω(log T)足以在至多对数因子的范围内恢复标准延迟OCO的速率。对于赌博机反馈，遗憾率由(1+σ_max/C)的幂次调节，其中σ_max是任意时刻的最大待处理观测数。这允许在C<σ_max时遗憾界优雅退化，同时保持亚线性。

</details>


### [170] [ICA Lens: Interpreting Language Models Without Training Another Dictionary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11722)
*Sida Liu,Feijiang Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ICALens，一种基于独立成分分析（ICA）的紧凑型可解释性透镜，用于高效发现语言模型表示中的可解释方向。通过优化的GPU并行FastICA流程和LLM专用稳定性方案，ICALens无需训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）即可恢复紧凑、人类可解释的方向，在SAEBench上稀疏探针性能与SAE相当，且在小到中等预算下探针扰动性能更优。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）已成为语言模型可解释性的标准工具，但训练、存储和评估大型过完备字典代价高昂，限制了快速探索。因此，作者希望探究：在训练另一个神经字典之前，激活几何中已经有多少可解释结构是可见的？直觉是可解释方向在token上具有选择性，比随机方向更不服从高斯分布，因此ICA作为一种寻找非高斯方向的经典方法值得重新审视。

Method: 提出ICALens工作流，包含：（1）优化的GPU并行FastICA管道，提高ICA在LLM激活上的计算效率；（2）LLM专用的稳定性配方，解决现成ICA实现在LLM激活上脆弱的问题；（3）更好的拟合诊断工具，用于检查和评估恢复的可解释方向。无需逐层的基于梯度的字典训练。在GPT-2 Small、Gemma 2 2B和Qwen 3.5 2B Base三个模型上进行层间分析。

Result: ICALens能够高效恢复紧凑、人类可解释的方向。在SAEBench基准上，ICA在稀疏探针（sparse probing）任务中与公开SAE相当，并在小到中等预算下的目标探针扰动（targeted probe perturbation）任务中优于SAE。

Conclusion: ICA不应被视为一个弱基线，而应被视为一种高效且互补的初始透镜，用于探索语言模型表示。ICALens为LLM可解释性提供了稳定、高效且可审计的ICA分析方案。

Original Abstract: Finding interpretable directions in language-model representations is critical for understanding and controlling model behavior. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become the standard tool for this purpose, but using them as the default first lens often requires training, storing, and evaluating large overcomplete dictionaries. This bottleneck limits rapid exploration and raises a fundamental question: how much interpretable structure is already visible from activation geometry before training another neural dictionary? Our intuition is simple: many interpretable directions are selective on tokens, and these directions should look less Gaussian than random directions. We therefore revisit independent component analysis (ICA), a classical method for finding non-Gaussian directions, as a compact lens for language-model interpretability. We find that ICA has been underestimated for LLM interpretability, because prior uses often relied on off-the-shelf ICA implementations that are brittle on LLM activations and lacked systematic tools for inspecting and evaluating the recovered directions. To bridge these gaps, we introduce ICALens, the first practical workflow for stable, efficient, and auditable ICA analysis of LLM representations. It combines an optimized GPU-parallel FastICA pipeline with LLM-specific stability recipes and better fitting diagnostics, enabling efficient and reliable layer-wise analysis. Across GPT-2 Small, Gemma 2 2B, and Qwen 3.5 2B Base, ICALens efficiently recovers compact, human-interpretable directions without per-layer gradient-based dictionary training. On SAEBench, ICA is competitive with public SAEs in sparse probing and outperforms them in targeted probe perturbation under small-to-medium budgets. These results suggest that ICA should not be viewed as a weak baseline, but as an efficient and complementary first lens for exploring language-model representations.

中文摘要翻译: 在语言模型表示中发现可解释方向，对于理解和控制模型行为至关重要。稀疏自编码器（SAE）已成为此目的的标准工具，但将其作为默认首选透镜往往需要训练、存储和评估大型过完备字典。这一瓶颈限制了快速探索，并引出一个根本性问题：在训练另一个神经字典之前，从激活几何中已经能看到多少可解释结构？我们的直觉很简单：许多可解释方向在token上具有选择性，这些方向看起来应该比随机方向更不服从高斯分布。因此，我们重新审视独立成分分析（ICA）——一种寻找非高斯方向的经典方法——作为语言模型可解释性的紧凑透镜。我们发现ICA在LLM可解释性中被低估了，因为先前的使用通常依赖现成的ICA实现，这些实现在LLM激活上很脆弱，并且缺乏用于检查和评估恢复方向的系统性工具。为弥补这些不足，我们引入ICALens，这是首个用于LLM表示稳定、高效且可审计ICA分析的实用工作流。它结合了优化的GPU并行FastICA管道、LLM特定的稳定性配方以及更好的拟合诊断工具，实现了高效可靠的逐层分析。在GPT-2 Small、Gemma 2 2B和Qwen 3.5 2B Base上，ICALens无需逐层基于梯度的字典训练即可高效恢复紧凑、人类可解释的方向。在SAEBench上，ICA在稀疏探针任务上与公开SAE具有竞争力，并且在小到中等预算下的目标探针扰动任务中优于SAE。这些结果表明，ICA不应被视为一个弱基线，而应被视为一种高效且互补的初始透镜，用于探索语言模型表示。

</details>


### [171] [RCAP: Robust, Class-Aware, Probabilistic Dynamic Dataset Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11761)
*Atif Hassan,Swanand Khare,Jiaul H. Paik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RCAP是一种鲁棒的、类别感知的概率性动态数据集剪枝算法，通过闭式解估计每类应保留的样本比例，并基于类级聚合损失自适应调整，在高剪枝率下显著提升了最差组别准确率，仅用10%数据即可在类别不平衡数据集上超越全量训练效果，并实现8.69倍加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有动态数据剪枝方法在高剪枝率下难以保持强最差组别准确率，特别是在平衡和不平衡数据集上表现不佳。

Method: 提出RCAP算法：(1) 使用闭式解估计每个类别应包含的训练子集样本比例；(2) 在每一轮训练中，基于类级聚合损失自适应调整该比例；(3) 采用自适应采样策略，优先选择高损失样本来填充每类子集。

Result: 在6个数据集（从平衡到高度不平衡）、5种模型、3种训练范式（从头训练、迁移学习、微调）下，RCAP在所有剪枝率下均优于SOTA方法。仅用10%数据时，在类别不平衡数据集上比全量训练提升>1%性能，平均加速8.69倍。

Conclusion: RCAP是一种鲁棒且高效的动态数据集剪枝方法，能够在显著降低计算成本的同时保持甚至提升模型性能，尤其适用于类别不平衡场景。

Original Abstract: Dynamic data pruning techniques aim to reduce computational cost while minimizing information loss by periodically selecting representative subsets of input data during model training. However, existing methods often struggle to maintain strong worst-group accuracy, particularly at high pruning rates, across balanced and imbalanced datasets. To address this challenge, we propose RCAP, a Robust, Class-Aware, Probabilistic dynamic dataset pruning algorithm for classification tasks. RCAP applies a closed-form solution to estimate the fraction of samples to be included in the training subset for each individual class. This fraction is adaptively adjusted in every epoch using class-wise aggregated loss. Thereafter, it employs an adaptive sampling strategy that prioritizes samples having high loss for populating the class-wise subsets. We evaluate RCAP on six diverse datasets ranging from class-balanced to highly imbalanced using five distinct models across three training paradigms: training from scratch, transfer learning, and fine-tuning. Our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art dataset pruning methods, achieving superior worst-group accuracy at all pruning rates. Remarkably, with only $10\%$ data, RCAP delivers $>1\%$ improvement in performance on class-imbalanced datasets compared to full data training while providing an average $8.69\times$ speedup. The code can be accessed at https://github.com/atif-hassan/RCAP-dynamic-dataset-pruning

中文摘要翻译: 动态数据剪枝技术旨在通过在模型训练期间定期选择具有代表性的输入数据子集，在减少计算开销的同时最小化信息损失。然而，现有方法在平衡和不平衡数据集上，尤其是在高剪枝率下，往往难以保持良好的最差组别准确率。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了RCAP——一种用于分类任务的鲁棒的、类别感知的概率性动态数据集剪枝算法。RCAP采用闭式解来估计每个类别应被包含在训练子集中的样本比例，并在每一轮训练中基于类级聚合损失自适应调整该比例。随后，它采用一种自适应采样策略，优先选择高损失样本来填充每类子集。我们在六个不同数据集（从类别平衡到高度不平衡）上，使用五种不同模型，在三种训练范式（从头训练、迁移学习和微调）下对RCAP进行了评估。我们的方法在所有剪枝率下均一致地优于最先进的数据集剪枝方法，取得了卓越的最差组别准确率。值得注意的是，仅使用10%的数据，RCAP在类别不平衡数据集上相比全量数据训练实现了超过1%的性能提升，同时提供了平均8.69倍的加速。代码可访问https://github.com/atif-hassan/RCAP-dynamic-dataset-pruning获取。

</details>


### [172] [AI4Land: Scalable Deep Learning for Global High-Resolution Land Use Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11793)
*Amirpasha Mozaffari,Marina Castaño,Stefano Materia,Etienne Tourigny,Oscar Molina-Sedano,Jordi Varela-Agrelo,Dario Garcia-Gasulla,Miguel Castrillo Melguizo,Mario Acosta,Amanda Duarte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AI4Land数据驱动框架，利用U-Net架构将粗分辨率情景数据与静态地理特征结合，重建高分辨率年度土地覆盖图，旨在减少陆地碳循环不确定性并提升气候模拟预测能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 陆地碳循环的不确定性严重制约了气候预测精度，而地球系统模型中地表过程表征的变异性是主要不确定性来源之一，亟需高分辨率、时空一致的地表变量重建方法。

Method: 采用两阶段U-Net架构框架：第一阶段（本文重点）将粗分辨率情景数据与静态地理特征融合，重建年度土地覆盖图；第二阶段（计划中）利用高分辨率地图预测叶面积指数等动态生物物理变量。模型基于地球观测数据训练，并在MareNostrum5超级计算机上实现GPU加速。

Result: 模型能够学习生成空间显式且物理一致的地表覆盖模式，可扩展至缺乏直接观测数据的时间段。在GPU加速的HPC基础设施上实现了全球尺度的气候AI流水线部署。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了开源仿真器套件，旨在与数字孪生平台（如Destination Earth）实时耦合，通过按需提供真实且动态演变的地表条件，减少关键不确定性并提升下一代气候模拟的预测能力。

Original Abstract: Uncertainty in the terrestrial carbon cycle remains a major constraint in climate projections, partly driven by the uncertainties affecting the land surface representation and variability in Earth system models. To address this limitation, we present a data-driven framework AI4Land, for generating high-resolution historical reconstructions and future projections of key land surface variables. The framework follows a two-phase approach using a U-Net architecture. In the first phase, which is the focus of this work, it reconstructs annual land use and land cover by integrating coarse-resolution scenario data with static geophysical features. In a planned second phase, the resulting high-resolution maps will be used to predict dynamic biophysical variables, particularly leaf area index, at finer temporal scales. Trained on Earth observation data, the models learn to reproduce spatially explicit and physically consistent land surface patterns, extending temporal coverage to periods lacking direct observations. AI4Land was developed and trained on MareNostrum5, demonstrating how GPU-accelerated HPC infrastructure enables global-scale climate AI pipelines. The final product is a suite of open-source emulators designed for real-time coupling with digital twin platforms, such as those developed under the Destination Earth initiative. By delivering realistic and evolving land surface conditions on demand, this work aims to reduce critical uncertainties and improve the predictive power of next-generation climate simulations.

中文摘要翻译: 陆地碳循环的不确定性仍然是气候预测中的主要制约因素，部分源于地球系统模型中地表过程表征及其变异性所受到的不确定性影响。为解决这一局限，我们提出了一个数据驱动框架AI4Land，用于生成关键地表变量的高分辨率历史重建与未来预估。该框架采用基于U-Net架构的两阶段方法。在第一阶段（本文重点），通过将粗分辨率情景数据与静态地理特征相融合，重建年度土地利用与土地覆盖。在计划中的第二阶段，生成的高分辨率地图将用于预测更细时间尺度上的动态生物物理变量，特别是叶面积指数。模型基于地球观测数据训练，学习生成空间显式且物理一致的地表模式，并将时间覆盖范围扩展至缺乏直接观测的时期。AI4Land在MareNostrum5上开发和训练，展示了GPU加速的高性能计算基础设施如何支撑全球尺度的气候AI流水线。最终产出是一套开源仿真器，专为与数字孪生平台（如Destination Earth倡议下开发的平台）实时耦合而设计。通过按需提供真实且动态演变的地表条件，本工作旨在减少关键不确定性，并提升下一代气候模拟的预测能力。

</details>


### [173] [Multimodal Ordinal Modeling of Alzheimer's Disease Severity Using Structural MRI and Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11794)
*Boris-Stephan Rauchmann,Jonathan Laib,Buse Ercik,Robert Perneczky,Sergio Altares-López*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于注意力增强的多模态机器学习框架，结合有序回归进行阿尔茨海默病严重程度自动分期，在多个数据集上验证了多模态有序模型优于单模态和非有序基线，并通过可解释性分析展示了临床可信的模型行为。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前阿尔茨海默病的临床分期方法耗时且易变，缺乏准确、可扩展的自动化工具，因此需要一种高效、可解释的自动分期方法。

Method: 构建注意力增强的多模态机器学习框架，整合T1加权MRI、人口统计学和遗传学变量，采用有序回归预测头进行AD严重程度分期；使用ADNI、AIBL和NIFD数据集进行分层划分训练和验证，严格保持测试集独立以避免数据泄露；采用Grad CAM++和SHAP进行可解释性分析。

Result: 单模态T1-MRI模型相邻阶段准确率0.963、QWK 0.444；表格模型QWK 0.433。多模态非有序基线MAE最低（0.340）；多模态有序模型相邻阶段准确率最高（0.970）、QWK最高（0.549），表明有序公式更好地捕捉了CDR量表的顺序结构。

Conclusion: 基于注意力的多模态有序回归学习是一种鲁棒、可解释且可扩展的AD自动严重程度分期方法，可支持AI辅助的临床决策。

Original Abstract: Neurodegenerative diseases such as Alzheimer's disease (AD) require accurate and scalable tools for assessing disease severity, yet current clinical staging remains time-intensive and prone to variability. We propose an attention-enhanced multimodal machine learning framework with ordinal regression for automated and interpretable AD severity staging. The framework integrates T1-weighted MRI with demographic and genetic variables and compares unimodal and multimodal architectures using ordinal and non-ordinal prediction heads. Models were trained and validated using cohort-stratified splits derived from the ADNI, AIBL, and NIFD datasets. A strictly held-out test set was constructed using subjects excluded from all training, validation, preprocessing, and hyperparameter tuning procedures, with subject-level splitting employed throughout to prevent data leakage. Among unimodal approaches, the T1-weighted MRI model achieved slightly higher adjacent-stage accuracy (0.963) and agreement with clinical staging (QWK 0.444) than the tabular model (QWK 0.433). Integrating imaging, demographic, and genetic information improved overall performance. The multimodal non-ordinal baseline achieved the lowest prediction error (MAE 0.340), whereas the ordinal multimodal model achieved the highest adjacent-stage accuracy (0.970) and strongest agreement with clinical staging (QWK 0.549). These findings indicate that ordinal formulations better capture the ordered structure of the CDR scale and yield predictions more consistent with clinical staging. Explainability analyses using Grad CAM++ and SHAP demonstrated anatomically and clinically plausible model behavior, supporting transparent decision-making. Overall, attention-based multimodal learning with ordinal regression represents a robust, interpretable, and scalable approach for automated AD severity staging and AI-assisted clinical decision support.

中文摘要翻译: 阿尔茨海默病（AD）等神经退行性疾病需要准确且可扩展的工具来评估疾病严重程度，然而当前的临床分期方法仍然耗时且易产生变异性。我们提出了一种基于注意力增强的多模态机器学习框架，结合有序回归，用于自动化且可解释的AD严重程度分期。该框架整合了T1加权MRI与人口统计学及遗传学变量，并比较了使用有序和非有序预测头的单模态与多模态架构。模型使用来自ADNI、AIBL和NIFD数据集的人群分层划分进行训练和验证。严格保留的测试集由排除在所有训练、验证、预处理和超参数调优过程之外的受试者构建，并在全程采用受试者级划分以防止数据泄露。在单模态方法中，T1加权MRI模型在相邻阶段准确率（0.963）和与临床分期的一致性（QWK 0.444）上略高于表格模型（QWK 0.433）。整合影像、人口统计学和遗传学信息提升了整体性能。多模态非有序基线实现了最低的预测误差（MAE 0.340），而多模态有序模型达到了最高的相邻阶段准确率（0.970）和与临床分期的最强一致性（QWK 0.549）。这些发现表明，有序公式能更好地捕捉CDR量表的顺序结构，并产生与临床分期更一致的预测。使用Grad CAM++和SHAP的可解释性分析展示了在解剖学和临床上合理的模型行为，支持透明的决策过程。总体而言，基于注意力的多模态有序回归学习是一种鲁棒、可解释且可扩展的AD自动严重程度分期方法，可用于AI辅助的临床决策支持。

</details>


### [174] [Space-sampled Value Decay: Forgetting Mechanisms for Non-stationary Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11797)
*Felix Störck,Fabian Hinder,Barbara Hammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“空间采样值衰减”（Space-sampled Value Decay）的显式遗忘机制，用于非平稳强化学习环境下的价值型深度强化学习架构，旨在无需任务ID或上下文等额外信息的情况下应对环境漂移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 啮齿类动物等生物能够在无明确信息提示的情况下适应环境参数的漂移，而现有的非平稳强化学习方法通常需要任务ID或上下文等完美信息。本文旨在开发一种简单有效的显式遗忘机制，使深度强化学习模型能在无额外信息条件下应对环境变化。

Method: 提出Space-sampled Value Decay（空间采样值衰减）方法，作为一种显式遗忘机制，集成到基于值的深度强化学习架构（如DQN和SAC）中，以缓解环境漂移带来的负面影响。

Result: 在非平稳环境中对DQN和SAC的改进版本进行评估，展示了该方法在提升回报方面的正面效果，同时也讨论了其存在的局限性。

Conclusion: Space-sampled Value Decay作为一种简单有效的显式遗忘机制，能够在非平稳强化学习任务中改善模型性能，但仍存在一定局限性有待进一步解决。

Original Abstract: Studies on rodents such as mice have shown the capabilities to adapt their behavior when dealing with changing parameters (``drift'') of the environment even if no information about change is provided (uncertainty) -- a behavior that can be modeled by forgetting mechanisms. Non-stationary Reinforcement Learning (NSRL) deals with adapting state-of-the-art RL methods to deal with changing environments: these however usually require (partially) perfect information about the drift such as ``task IDs'' or ``context''. To mitigate the effects of drift, this work develops \emph{Space-sampled Value Decay} as an explicit forgetting mechanism for value-based deep RL architectures as a simple yet effective approach. In particular we demonstrate and discuss positive effects but also limitations in achieved returns for modifications of Deep Q-networks (DQN) and Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) when evaluated on non-stationary environments.

中文摘要翻译: 针对小鼠等啮齿类动物的研究表明，即使环境参数发生漂移且未提供任何变化信息（不确定性），它们也能调整自身行为以适应环境变化——这种行为可以通过遗忘机制来建模。非平稳强化学习旨在改进现有强化学习方法以应对变化的环境，但现有方法通常需要关于漂移的（部分）完美信息，例如"任务ID"或"上下文"。为了缓解漂移的影响，本文提出了一种名为"空间采样值衰减"的方法，将其作为基于值的深度强化学习架构中的显式遗忘机制，该方法简单而有效。具体而言，我们在非平稳环境下对深度Q网络和软演员-评论家算法的改进版本进行了评估，展示并讨论了该方法在提升回报方面的正面效果及其局限性。

</details>


### [175] [From Uniform to Learned Graph Priors: Diffusion for Structure Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11831)
*Qi Shao,Hao Guo,Jiawen Chen,Duxin Chen,Wenwu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Diff-prior，一种扩散参数化的自适应先验，用于校准神经关系推理（NRI）方法中的潜图分布，生成更可靠的边缘后验和结构发现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有NRI方法使用过于简化的因子化图先验（接近均匀分布），将边视为独立实体，与现实系统不匹配，导致边缘后验分散且不明确，限制了结构发现的可靠性。

Method: 提出Diff-prior，一种扩散参数化的自适应先验，将先验整合重构为可学习的去噪式校准过程。Diff-prior在结构采样前直接作用于编码器的边缘分布，通过扩散模型学习结构化先验，对模糊的边缘后验进行结构化校准，引导其向更接近真实底层结构的分布靠拢。

Result: 在标准基准上的实验表明，Diff-prior提高了多种NRI架构的结构推理性能，生成了更具决定性的边缘后验。

Conclusion: Diff-prior通过可学习的去噪校准机制有效解决了NRI方法中图先验过于简化的问题，为结构化变量提供了一种通用的训练范式，提升了关系推理的可靠性。

Original Abstract: Neural relational inference (NRI) methods discover interaction graphs from trajectories through variational reasoning on discrete potential edges. However, these methods typically rely on oversimplified, factorized graph priors. Such priors, typically nearing uniform distributions, treat edges as independent entities. This systemic misalignment does not match the real-world systems and yields diffuse and indecisive edge posteriors limiting the reliability of structural discovery. To address this, we propose \textit{Diff-prior}, a diffusion-parameterized adaptive prior used to calibrate latent graph distribution rather than generate graphs. Our core insight is to reframe prior integration as a learnable denoising-style calibration that organizes scattered, uncertain edge posteriors into a more reliable overall structure which can be trained by the diffusion model. Diff-prior learns an adaptive structure prior that performs structured calibration on the edge posteriors during inference, guiding it towards a distribution closer to the underlying structure. The diff-prior operates before structural sampling and acts as a denoising calibrator directly on the encoder edge distribution, which provides a generic training paradigm over structured variables. Experiments on standard benchmarks validated our framework, and the results indicate that Diff-prior improves the performance of structure inference and generates more decisive edge posteriors across multiple NRI-family architectures. The code is available on https://github.com/Hardy158118/Diffprior.

中文摘要翻译: 神经关系推理（NRI）方法通过离散潜在边上的变分推理，从轨迹中发现交互图。然而，这些方法通常依赖于过度简化的因子化图先验。此类先验通常接近均匀分布，将边视为独立实体。这种系统性错位与现实系统不匹配，导致边缘后验分散且不明确，限制了结构发现的可靠性。为解决这一问题，我们提出\textit{Diff-prior}，一种扩散参数化的自适应先验，用于校准潜图分布而非生成图。我们的核心见解是将先验整合重新构建为一种可学习的去噪式校准过程，该过程将分散、不确定的边缘后验组织成更可靠的总体结构，且可通过扩散模型进行训练。Diff-prior学习一种自适应结构先验，在推理过程中对边缘后验执行结构化校准，引导其向更接近底层结构的分布靠拢。Diff-prior在结构采样之前运行，直接作为编码器边缘分布上的去噪校准器，为结构化变量提供了一种通用的训练范式。在标准基准上的实验验证了我们的框架，结果表明Diff-prior提高了结构推理的性能，并在多种NRI系列架构上生成了更具决定性的边缘后验。代码可在https://github.com/Hardy158118/Diffprior获取。

</details>


### [176] [TaskFusion: Continual Anomaly Detection for Heterogeneous Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11844)
*Dayananda Herurkar,Federico Raue,Joachim Folz,Jörn Hees,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对表格数据的持续异常检测方法，通过特征空间对齐、任务融合增强和异常暴露技术，在21个异构数据集上显著优于现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 表格数据的持续异常检测面临特征模式异构、分布偏移和严重类别不平衡等挑战，现有持续学习方法因依赖固定输入空间而难以有效处理来自不同领域的顺序数据流。

Method: 方法包含三个核心部分：(1) AGF模型将任务特定特征映射到共享空间并对齐分布以降低表征漂移；(2) Taskfusion增强，包括任务内边界感知插值和跨任务混合以传递异常结构；(3) 使用表格数据集蒸馏存储合成回放样本，结合增强数据通过异常暴露目标实现鲁棒检测。

Result: 在21个异构数据集上的评估表明，该方法在持续异常检测性能上显著优于顺序微调及其他持续学习基线，有效减少了灾难性遗忘，并在异构数据集上保持了稳定的检测能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的持续学习方法能够有效克服表格数据中特征异构、分布漂移和类别不平衡等挑战，为持续异常检测提供了可行且鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Continual anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging and remains largely underexplored, particularly in settings with heterogeneous feature schemas, distribution shifts, and severe class imbalance. In many real-world applications, data arrive sequentially from diverse domains, rendering conventional continual learning methods ineffective due to their reliance on a fixed input space. We propose a continual learning (CL) method, which can overcome these challenges and continually learn from different tasks. Our method consists of three main parts: our AGF model, Taskfusion augmentation, and outlier exposure. The AGF-model maps task-specific features into a shared space, then aligns distributions to reduce representation drift, and learns anomaly decision boundaries in the aligned space. To improve stability, we introduce Taskfusion augmentation, combining boundary-aware interpolation within tasks to refine the model anomaly boundaries and cross-task mixing to transfer anomaly structure across datasets. To handle class imbalance and memory constraints, we employ tabular dataset distillation to store compact synthetic replay samples, which are jointly used with augmented data in an outlier exposure objective for robust anomaly detection. We evaluate the approach on 21 heterogeneous datasets across multiple domains. Results show that our approach substantially improves continual anomaly detection performance over sequential fine-tuning and other CL baselines while reducing catastrophic forgetting and maintaining stable detection across heterogeneous datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 表格数据中的持续异常检测具有挑战性，并且在具有异构特征模式、分布偏移和严重类别不平衡的设置中仍然 largely 未被充分探索。在许多实际应用中，数据以顺序方式来自不同领域，这使得传统的持续学习方法因其依赖于固定输入空间而效果不佳。我们提出了一种持续学习（CL）方法，能够克服这些挑战并持续从不同任务中学习。我们的方法包含三个主要部分：AGF模型、Taskfusion增强和异常暴露。AGF模型将任务特定特征映射到共享空间，然后对齐分布以减少表征漂移，并在对齐空间中学习异常决策边界。为了提高稳定性，我们引入了Taskfusion增强，结合任务内的边界感知插值来细化模型异常边界，以及跨任务混合以跨数据集传递异常结构。为了处理类别不平衡和内存限制，我们采用表格数据集蒸馏来存储紧凑的合成回放样本，这些样本与增强数据一起在异常暴露目标中用于鲁棒的异常检测。我们在多个领域的21个异构数据集上评估了该方法。结果表明，我们的方法在持续异常检测性能上显著优于顺序微调和其他持续学习基线，同时减少了灾难性遗忘并在异构数据集上保持了稳定的检测能力。

</details>


### [177] [Fine-tuning Multi-modal LLMs with ART: Art-based Reinforcement Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11854)
*Michal Chudoba,Sergey Alyaev,Petra Galuscakova,Tomasz Wiktorski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ART方法，通过仅优化MLLM的原始视觉输入（像素阵列）实现参数高效微调，无需修改预编译计算图，在数学和工具使用基准上达到与LoRA相当的准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PEFT方法（LoRA和Soft Prompting）都需要修改预编译LLM的计算图，在高吞吐量引擎（如vLLM）中无法得到完全支持，因此需要一种不修改计算图的高效微调方法。

Method: ART通过反向传播梯度到原始像素阵列来优化多模态大语言模型的视觉输入（而非模型权重），将软提示方法应用到预编译计算图上。优化后的视觉输入可风格化为与任务相关的计算艺术品。

Result: 在多个规模的Qwen架构和多个文本基准上验证了有效性，在数学和结构化工具使用基准上取得了与LoRA相当的准确率。

Conclusion: ART提供了一种无需修改计算图的PEFT替代方案，通过仅优化视觉输入即可向冻结的MLLM注入信息，同时保持了与LoRA竞争的准确性。

Original Abstract: There are two main Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) techniques for Large Language Models (LLMs). While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) introduces additional weights between the LLM layers, Soft Prompting introduces additional fine-tuning-specific raw tokens to an LLM input. However, both require modification to the computational graphs of precompiled, preoptimized LLMs. As a result, neither is fully supported in high-throughput engines like vLLM. We propose fine-tuning with ART (Art-based Reinforcement Training). The method injects information into a frozen Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) by optimizing only its raw visual input, thus enabling the soft-token approach on pre-compiled computational graphs. It relies on backpropagation of gradients back into a plain pixel array and thus supports any fine-tuning objective. Moreover, the optimized visual input can be stylized as task-relevant computational artworks. The approach's effectiveness is confirmed for different sizes of a popular open Qwen architecture and for several textual benchmarks. Specifically, ART reaches accuracy competitive with LoRA across mathematics and structured-tool-use benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）主要有两种参数高效微调（PEFT）技术。低秩适配（LoRA）在LLM层之间引入额外权重，而软提示（Soft Prompting）则向LLM输入引入额外的微调特定原始token。然而，这两种方法都需要修改预编译、预优化LLM的计算图。因此，它们在高吞吐量引擎（如vLLM）中都未得到完全支持。本文提出基于艺术强化训练（ART）的微调方法。该方法通过仅优化冻结的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的原始视觉输入来注入信息，从而在预编译计算图上实现软token方法。它依赖于将梯度反向传播回普通像素阵列，因此支持任何微调目标。此外，优化后的视觉输入可以风格化为与任务相关的计算艺术品。该方法在流行开源Qwen架构的不同规模上以及在多个文本基准上的有效性得到了验证。具体而言，ART在数学和结构化工具使用基准上达到了与LoRA相当的准确率。

</details>


### [178] [RePAIR: Predictive Self-Supervised Representation Learning in Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11860)
*Christoph Koller,Johannes Fürnkranz,Timo Bertram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RePAIR是一种结合MAE、JEPA和BERT的新型自监督表示学习架构，通过对序列潜在状态进行大规模掩码并利用轻量级预测器在低维嵌入空间中修复序列间隙，从而学习紧凑且有意义的表示。在国际象棋领域实验表明，该方法能生成语义丰富的棋盘表示，无需依赖昂贵的强化学习即可推理棋子移动。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自监督表示学习方法（如MAE、JEPA、BERT）各有优势但尚未被有效融合，且缺乏一种能够高效编码序列数据（如连续棋局状态）为紧凑语义表示的统一架构。作者希望设计一种既能大规模掩码学习上下文语义，又能通过轻量级预测器在嵌入空间进行隐式推理的框架。

Method: RePAIR架构的核心是对潜在状态序列进行大规模掩码（类似BERT和MAE），然后利用一个轻量级的Predictor在低维嵌入空间中修复被掩码的序列间隙（类似JEPA）。该架构包含编码器-预测器结构，编码器将输入序列映射为潜在表示，预测器在嵌入空间中对被掩码的部分进行预测补全。

Result: 在国际象棋领域实验中，RePAIR的编码器能够精炼棋盘表示，使得有意义的国际象棋概念在潜在空间中聚类出现；模型能够不依赖昂贵的强化学习即可推理棋子移动，成功重建被掩码的棋盘状态；所得表示空间支持通过观察棋局路径轨迹进行直观快速的棋局分析。

Conclusion: RePAIR有效融合了MAE、JEPA和BERT的优势，为序列数据中的对象编码提供了紧凑且有语义意义的表示学习框架，并在国际象棋领域验证了其无需强化学习即可进行语义推理的潜力，为后续可解释表示学习提供了新方向。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we introduce Representation Prediction via Autoencoding using Iterative Refinement (RePAIR) - a novel self-supervised representation learning architecture that synthesizes Masked Autoencoders (MAE), Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA), and Bidirectional Encoder Representations from Transformers (BERT). We demonstrate how it can be used to encode objects in sequential data like consecutive chess positions into compact yet meaningful representations. The basic principle of the architecture is to mask large portions of a sequence of latent states, similar to BERT and MAE. Then, we apply a lightweight Predictor to the latent representations that repairs gaps in the sequence in a lower-dimensional embedding space akin to JEPA. Our experiments in the domain of chess show that the Encoder refines the board representations such that meaningful chess concepts emerge clustered in the latent space. Furthermore, reconstructions of the masked board states show that the model is able to reason about the piece movements without relying on costly reinforcement learning methods. Lastly, we find that the resulting representation space allows for quick and intuitive dissections of chess games by observing the game path trajectories in this semantically rich space.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了RePAIR（通过迭代精炼的自编码进行表示预测）——一种新颖的自监督表示学习架构，该架构综合了掩码自编码器（MAE）、联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）和双向编码器表示从Transformer（BERT）的思想。我们展示了如何利用它将连续棋局位置等序列数据中的对象编码为紧凑而有意义的表示。该架构的基本原理是：类似于BERT和MAE，对潜在状态序列的大部分区域进行掩码；然后，类似于JEPA，将一个轻量级预测器应用于潜在表示，在低维嵌入空间中修复序列中的空缺。我们在国际象棋领域的实验表明，编码器能够精炼棋盘表示，使得有意义的国际象棋概念在潜在空间中形成聚类。此外，对掩码棋盘状态的重建表明，该模型能够在不依赖昂贵的强化学习方法的情况下推理棋子的移动。最后，我们发现由此产生的表示空间允许通过在该语义丰富的空间中观察棋局路径轨迹，对国际象棋对局进行快速直观的剖析。

</details>


### [179] [MemNovo: Look Back at the Spectrum for Balanced De Novo Peptide Sequencing from Mass Spectrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11868)
*Dongxin Lyu,Jingbo Zhou,Hongxin Xiang,Yuqiang Li,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了现有基于Transformer的从头肽段测序模型在推理时过度依赖生成序列先验而忽视质谱证据的问题，并提出了一种无需训练、即插即用的记忆增强机制MemNovo，通过在解码阶段引入持久化谱记忆库和超保守残差连接来重平衡序列与谱信息，显著提升了肽段测序精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Transformer编码器-解码器模型在从头肽段测序中虽表现优异，但其自回归解码器在推理时存在病态行为：过度依赖已生成序列的先验信息，逐步忽略输入质谱中的细粒度物理证据，导致生成序列在生物学上合理但与输入谱不忠实对应。作者旨在纠正这一不平衡问题。

Method: 提出MemNovo，一种无需训练、即插即用的推理时重平衡机制。具体包括：1) 建立持久化谱记忆库（persistent spectral memory bank），存储编码器输出的谱特征；2) 在最终解码阶段通过超保守残差连接（ultra-conservative residual connection）将检索到的谱特征直接注入解码器状态，从而缓解信息瓶颈，恢复解码状态与原始谱之间的互信息。

Result: 在Nine Species基准上使用Casanovo和InstaNovo两个代表性基线模型进行实验，MemNovo一致提升了氨基酸精度和肽段精度：对Casanovo实现高达39.1%的肽段精度相对提升，对InstaNovo实现高达3.9%的提升，且计算开销可忽略不计。
