</details>


### [209] [Claw-SWE-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating OpenClaw-style Agent Harnesses on Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12344)
*Mengyu Zheng,Kai Han,Boxun Li,Haiyang Xu,Yuchuan Tian,Wei He,Hang Zhou,Jianyuan Guo,Hailin Hu,Lin Ma,Chao Xu,Guohao Dai,Lixue Xia,Yunchao Wei,Yunhe Wang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Claw-SWE-Bench，一个多语言SWE-bench风格基准测试和适配器协议，用于公平评估通用智能体（如OpenClaw）的代码修复能力。通过标准化提示、运行时预算、工作空间契约、补丁提取和评估器，该基准涵盖8种语言350个GitHub issue实例。实验表明，适配器设计对智能体编码性能至关重要（从19.1%提升至73.4%），且模型选择和智能体框架选择对Pass@1影响相当（约29pp vs 27pp）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 通用智能体（如OpenClaw）作为自主工具使用者日益普及，但SWE-bench难以衡量其编码能力，因为通用智能体本身不满足SWE-bench所需的干净Docker工作空间、补丁和预测合约要求。需要一种标准化的基准和适配器协议，使异构智能体框架在公平设置下可比。

Method: 提出Claw-SWE-Bench基准和适配器协议，包括固定提示、运行时预算、工作空间契约、补丁提取过程和评估器。从SWE-bench-Multilingual和SWE-bench-Verified-Mini中筛选350个GitHub issue实例（8种语言，43个仓库），并推出80实例的Lite子集用于快速验证（通过成本感知、排序感知程序从17个校准列中选择）。

Result: 在完整基准上，OpenClaw使用最小直接差异适配器仅得19.1% Pass@1，而完整适配器在相同GLM 5.1骨干下达到73.4%。在OpenClaw×9模型和5框架×2模型的扫描中，模型选择改变Pass@1达29.4个百分点，框架选择改变27.4个百分点。准确性相似的系统在总API成本上可能差异显著。

Conclusion: Claw-SWE-Bench将智能体框架（harness）和成本核算作为SWE风格编码智能体评估的一级评估维度，提供了完整基准和低成本参考集，用于可重复比较。适配器设计是释放通用智能体编码能力的关键。

Original Abstract: General-purpose agents such as OpenClaw are increasingly used as autonomous tool users, but their coding ability is difficult to measure under SWE-bench: a generic agent does not by itself satisfy the clean Docker workspace, patch, and prediction contract required for scoring. We introduce Claw-SWE-Bench, a multilingual SWE-bench-style benchmark and adapter protocol that makes heterogeneous agent harnesses, or claws, comparable under fair settings including a fixed prompt, runtime budget, workspace contract, patch extraction procedure, and evaluator. The full benchmark contains 350 GitHub issue-resolution instances across 8 languages and 43 repositories, drawn from SWE-bench-Multilingual and SWE-bench-Verified-Mini after future-commit cleanup. We also release Claw-SWE-Bench Lite for faster validation, which is an 80-instance subset selected by a cost-aware, rank-aware procedure over 17 calibration columns. On the full benchmark, OpenClaw with a minimal direct-diff adapter scores only $19.1\%$ Pass@1, whereas the full adapter reaches $73.4\%$ with the same GLM 5.1 backbone, showing that adapter design is essential for enabling OpenClaw-style harnesses to perform coding tasks effectively. Across an OpenClaw $\times$ nine-model sweep and a five-claw $\times$ two-model sweep, model choice changes Pass@1 by $29.4$ pp and harness choice by $27.4$ pp under fixed models; systems with similar accuracy can differ substantially in total API cost. Claw-SWE-Bench therefore treats harness and cost accounting as first-class axes of SWE-style coding-agent evaluation, providing both a full benchmark and a low-cost reference set for reproducible comparison. The data is available at https://github.com/opensquilla/claw-swe-bench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/TokenRhythm/Claw-SWE-Bench.

中文摘要翻译: 诸如OpenClaw之类的通用智能体越来越多地被用作自主工具使用者，但它们的编码能力很难在SWE-bench下衡量：通用智能体本身并不满足评分所需的干净Docker工作空间、补丁和预测合约。我们提出了Claw-SWE-Bench，这是一个多语言SWE-bench风格的基准测试和适配器协议，通过在固定提示、运行时预算、工作空间契约、补丁提取过程和评估器下实现公平设置，使异构智能体框架（即"claws"）具有可比性。该完整基准包含来自8种语言和43个仓库的350个GitHub issue解决实例，这些实例在清理未来提交后从SWE-bench-Multilingual和SWE-bench-Verified-Mini中抽取。我们还发布了Claw-SWE-Bench Lite用于更快速的验证，这是一个通过成本感知、排序感知程序从17个校准列中选出的80实例子集。在完整基准上，使用最小直接差异适配器的OpenClaw仅得分19.1% Pass@1，而完整适配器在相同GLM 5.1骨干下达到了73.4%，这表明适配器设计对于使OpenClaw风格的框架有效执行编码任务至关重要。在OpenClaw×九个模型的扫描以及五个框架×两个模型的扫描中，模型选择使Pass@1改变29.4个百分点，而在固定模型下框架选择使Pass@1改变27.4个百分点；精度相似的系统在总API成本上可能存在显著差异。因此，Claw-SWE-Bench将框架和成本核算作为SWE风格编码智能体评估的一级维度，同时提供完整基准和低成本参考集用于可重复比较。数据可在https://github.com/opensquilla/claw-swe-bench和https://huggingface.co/datasets/TokenRhythm/Claw-SWE-Bench获取。

</details>


### [210] [Anatomy of Post-Training: Using Interpretability to Characterize Data and Shape the Learning Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12360)
*Leon Bergen,Usha Bhalla,Sidharth Baskaran,Max Loeffler,Raphael Sarfati,Dhruvil Gala,Ryan Panwar,Santiago Aranguri,Thomas Fel,Atticus Geiger,Matthew Kowal,Siddharth Boppana,Daniel Balsam,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo,Thomas McGrath,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的后训练流程，利用可解释性协议将偏好数据中的潜在概念显式化，从而让从业者能够审计和塑造模型的学习信号，避免学习到虚假关联等不良行为。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的语言模型后训练阶段主要依赖标量奖励优化，但这种方法将多样化的期望目标简化为单一分数，使得从业者无法了解数据实际教会了模型什么，导致模型学习到虚假关联，产生过度风格化、谄媚等不良行为。本文旨在解决这一问题，让从业者能够在优化之前检查偏好数据集，并在概念层面决定模型应该学习哪些行为。

Method: 提出了一种以数据为中心的后训练流程：首先使用可解释性协议对偏好数据进行分析，统计性地识别出区分偏好生成与非偏好生成的潜在概念；然后将这些概念显式化，用于细粒度的用户反馈；最后通过特征干预或数据干预的方式统一了多种基于可解释性的训练协议，实现对奖励信号的塑造。

Result: 实验表明，该流程能够诊断现有偏好数据中的不良信号，减轻模型的脱靶学习（学习到非预期的特征），同时还能帮助放大或塑造期望的属性，如安全护栏和模型个性。

Conclusion: 可解释性可以将后训练从优化不透明的代理奖励转变为审计和塑造学习信号本身的过程，使模型行为更加可控和透明。

Original Abstract: Language-model post-training is the main stage at which model behavior is shaped, yet it still largely involves optimization of scalar rewards that summarize diverse desiderata. This abstraction gives practitioners little visibility into what their data actually teaches models, allowing spurious correlations to be learned by a model and inducing undesirable behaviors such as over-stylization and sycophancy. To address this problem, we ask: can we inspect a preference dataset before optimization and decide, at the level of concepts, which behaviors a model should be allowed to learn? Motivated by this, we introduce a data-centric post-training pipeline that uses interpretability protocols to develop statistical hypotheses for the latent concepts separating preferred from dispreferred generations, making them explicit for fine-grained user feedback. Building on this view, we unify several interpretability-based training protocols as ways of shaping rewards via feature or data interventions. Empirically, we show that our pipeline diagnoses undesirable signals in existing preference data, mitigates off-target learning, and can also help amplify or shape desired properties such as safeguards and model personality. More broadly, our results suggest that interpretability can turn post-training from optimizing opaque proxy rewards into a process of auditing and sculpting the learning signal itself.

中文摘要翻译: 语言模型的后训练是塑造模型行为的主要阶段，然而该过程主要仍然依赖标量奖励优化来概括多样化的期望目标。这种抽象化使得从业者几乎无法了解数据实际教会了模型什么，导致模型学习到虚假关联，并引发过度风格化、谄媚等不良行为。为解决这一问题，我们提出：能否在优化之前检查偏好数据集，并在概念层面决定模型应该被允许学习哪些行为？受此启发，我们引入了一种以数据为中心的后训练流程，该流程利用可解释性协议来统计性地提出关于区分偏好生成与非偏好生成的潜在概念的假设，并将这些概念显式化以供细粒度的用户反馈。基于这一视角，我们将多种基于可解释性的训练协议统一为通过特征干预或数据干预来塑造奖励的方式。实验表明，我们的流程能够诊断现有偏好数据中的不良信号，减轻脱靶学习，并且还能够帮助放大或塑造期望的属性，如安全护栏和模型个性。更广泛地说，我们的结果表明，可解释性可以将后训练从优化不透明的代理奖励转变为审计和塑造学习信号本身的过程。

</details>


### [211] [Latent World Recovery for Multimodal Learning with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12362)
*Hui Wang,Tianyu Ren,Joseph Butler,Christopher Baker,Karen Rafferty,Simon McDade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent World Recovery (LWR)框架，通过模态对齐和可用性感知融合，解决多模态学习中部分模态缺失的问题，并在多组学癌症表型分类和生存预测任务上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生物科学应用中异质模态往往只有部分可用，现有方法需要插补缺失模态或固定模态集合，导致误差传播和灵活性不足。

Method: LWR框架包含两个核心思想：(i) 将不同模态的特定嵌入对齐到共享潜空间；(ii) 仅融合训练和推理时实际可用的模态嵌入，构建统一表示，避免显式重构缺失模态。

Result: 在真实世界的不完整多组学基准数据集上，LWR在癌症表型分类和生存预测等下游任务中表现出有效性能。

Conclusion: 基于邻域的潜空间对齐和可用性感知的模态融合，使得LWR能够在部分观测下实现鲁棒的多模态预测，同时避免了显式重构缺失模态带来的误差传播问题。

Original Abstract: We study multimodal learning under missing modalities, with particular motivation from bioscience applications in which heterogeneous modalities are often only partially available when decisions need to be made. We propose Latent World Recovery (LWR), a framework built on two key ideas: (i) modality-specific embeddings from different modalities are aligned in a shared latent space, and (ii) a unified representation is constructed by fusing only the embeddings of the modalities that are actually available at both training and inference time. Rather than imputing missing modalities or requiring a fixed modality set, LWR treats each modality as a partial perception of an underlying latent state and performs availability-aware representation learning directly from the observed modalities. This combination of neighbor-based latent alignment and availability-aware modality fusion enables robust multimodal prediction under partial observation, while avoiding error propagation from explicit reconstruction of missing modalities. We evaluate the proposed framework on real-world incomplete multi-omics benchmarks and demonstrate that it provides an effective approach to downstream tasks such as cancer phenotype classification and survival prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了缺失模态下的多模态学习问题，其动机主要来自生物科学应用：在这些应用中，需要在做出决策时处理异质模态仅部分可用的情况。我们提出了潜在世界恢复（LWR）框架，该框架基于两个关键思想：(i) 来自不同模态的模态特定嵌入在共享潜空间中对齐；(ii) 通过仅融合训练和推理时实际可用的模态嵌入来构建统一表示。LWR不依赖于插补缺失模态或要求固定的模态集合，而是将每种模态视为对底层潜状态的部分感知，直接从观测到的模态执行可用性感知的表示学习。这种基于邻域的潜对齐与可用性感知的模态融合相结合，使得在部分观测下能够实现鲁棒的多模态预测，同时避免了因显式重构缺失模态而产生的误差传播。我们在真实世界的不完整多组学基准数据集上评估了所提出的框架，并证明其为癌症表型分类和生存预测等下游任务提供了一种有效方法。

</details>


### [212] [On Subquadratic Architectures: From Applications to Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12364)
*Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,Levente Zólyomi,David Stap,Pieter-Jan Hoedt,Niklas Schmidinger,Lukas Hauzenberger,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet三种次二次架构，在代码模型预训练、代码模型蒸馏和时间序列基础模型预训练三个复杂依赖任务上，xLSTM表现最佳，其优势源于更灵活稳定的门控机制带来的鲁棒状态跟踪与记忆累积能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Transformer的二次注意力计算成本高昂，次二次架构虽提供了可扩展替代方案，但哪种设计能构建最有效的序列模型尚不明确，因此需要系统比较领先的次二次架构。

Method: 提出统一公式化框架，从状态跟踪和记忆动态角度分析xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet的底层架构机制，并在代码模型预训练、代码模型蒸馏和时间序列基础模型预训练三个场景中进行实验评估。

Result: xLSTM在三个复杂任务上整体性能最佳；通过门控机制实现了更灵活稳定的记忆校正；在受控合成长度泛化任务上验证了上述发现。

Conclusion: xLSTM在复杂任务上的优势源于其鲁棒的状态跟踪和累积能力，门控机制是关键设计因素。

Original Abstract: Transformers dominate modern sequence modeling, but their quadratic attention incurs substantial computational cost. Subquadratic architectures offer a scalable alternative. However, it remains unclear which designs yield the most effective sequence models. We compare three leading approaches: xLSTM, Mamba-2, and Gated DeltaNet. We evaluate these models on tasks with complex dependencies: (1) code-model pre-training, (2) distillation of code models from large language models, and (3) pre-training of time-series foundation models. Across these settings, xLSTM delivers the strongest overall performance. To explain xLSTM's advantage, we present a unified formulation and analyze the underlying architectural mechanisms, focusing on state tracking and memory dynamics. Our results show that xLSTM enables more flexible and stable memory correction via its gating scheme. We corroborate these findings on controlled synthetic length-generalization tasks. Overall, our findings indicate that xLSTM's gains on complex tasks stem from robust state tracking and accumulation.

中文摘要翻译: Transformer主导了现代序列建模，但其二次注意力机制带来了巨大的计算成本。次二次架构提供了一种可扩展的替代方案。然而，哪些设计能产生最有效的序列模型仍不明确。我们比较了三种领先的方法：xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet。我们在具有复杂依赖关系的任务上评估这些模型：(1) 代码模型预训练，(2) 从大语言模型中蒸馏代码模型，以及(3) 时间序列基础模型预训练。在这些设置中，xLSTM展现了最强的整体性能。为了解释xLSTM的优势，我们提出了一个统一的公式化框架，并从状态跟踪和记忆动态角度分析了底层的架构机制。我们的结果表明，xLSTM通过其门控机制实现了更灵活和稳定的记忆校正。我们在受控的合成长度泛化任务上验证了这些发现。总体而言，我们的研究结果表明，xLSTM在复杂任务上的增益源于其鲁棒的状态跟踪与累积能力。

</details>


### [213] [Breaking Entropy Bounds: Accelerating RL Training via MTP with Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12370)
*Yucheng Li,Huiqiang Jiang,Yang Xu,Jianxin Yang,Yi Zhang,Yizhong Cao,Yuhao Shen,Fan Zhou,Rui Men,Jianwei Zhang,An Yang,Bowen Yu,Bo Zheng,Fei Huang,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bebop系统性地研究了多令牌预测（MTP）在LLM强化学习后训练中的应用，揭示了MTP接受率与模型熵的负线性关系，提出了端到端TV损失函数和拒绝采样策略，在不需在线更新的条件下实现最高1.8倍端到端加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: RL训练中rollout阶段是主要瓶颈，MTP虽可通过推测解码加速，但RL训练期间MTP接受率显著下降导致加速效果有限，亟需系统性研究并在大规模RL管线中高效集成MTP。

Method: 1) 揭示了MTP接受率与RL阶段模型熵上升之间的负线性关系；2) 提出概率拒绝采样替代贪心草稿采样以缓解熵扰动；3) 提出端到端全变差（TV）损失，直接优化多步拒绝采样的接受率；4) 测试多种在线MTP训练策略，发现RL前使用端到端TV损失+拒绝采样训练的MTP可在整个RL过程中保持稳定接受率。

Result: 端到端TV损失带来约10%接受率提升，最高达95%接受率；额外25%推理吞吐增益；在数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务上有效；在Qwen3.5/3.6/3.7模型上实现最高1.8倍异步RL训练端到端加速。

Conclusion: 通过系统性分析MTP在RL中的退化机理并设计针对性优化方法，Bebop有效解决了MTP在RL训练中加速失效的问题，实现了显著且稳定的训练加速，且无需在线更新MTP，具有实用价值。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key component in modern large language models, yet the rollout stage remains the key bottleneck in RL training pipelines. Although Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) offers a natural solution to accelerate rollouts through speculative decoding, many studies have observed that MTP acceptance rates degrade significantly during RL training, leading to limited speedup performance. To address this bottleneck, we present Bebop, a systematic study of MTP in LLM post-training, and offer practical recipes to integrate MTP into large-scale RL pipelines. First, we reveal that the MTP acceptance rate is fundamentally bounded by the fluctuation of model entropy, which demonstrates a clear negative linear relationship with the rise of entropy in the RL stage. Second, we show that probabilistic rejection sampling largely alleviates the disturbance introduced by entropy in RL compared to greedy draft sampling. We further identify that the conventional MTP training objectives (cross-entropy or KL) are suboptimal in such settings, and therefore we propose a novel end-to-end TV loss that directly optimizes multi-step rejection sampling acceptance rate, yielding ~10% acceptance rate improvements, achieving up to 95% acceptance rates and up to 25% extra inference throughput gains across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic tasks. Third, we test various online MTP training strategies during RL and show that pre-RL MTP training with e2e TV loss and rejection sampling achieves a consistent acceptance rate and speedup throughout the entire RL, eliminating the need for costly online MTP updating. We provide extensive experiments and analysis that validate our findings. Experimental results show our method achieves up to 1.8x end-to-end acceleration in async RL training of Qwen3.5, Qwen3.6, and Qwen3.7 models.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习（RL）已成为现代大语言模型的关键组成部分，但rollout阶段仍是RL训练管线中的主要瓶颈。尽管多令牌预测（MTP）通过推测解码为加速rollout提供了自然解决方案，但许多研究发现MTP的接受率在RL训练期间显著下降，导致加速性能有限。为解决这一瓶颈，我们提出Bebop，对LLM后训练中的MTP进行系统性研究，并提供将MTP集成到大规模RL管线中的实用方案。首先，我们揭示了MTP接受率从根本上受模型熵波动的约束，其与RL阶段熵的上升呈现清晰的负线性关系。其次，我们证明相较于贪心草稿采样，概率拒绝采样能大幅缓解RL中熵引入的扰动。我们进一步发现传统MTP训练目标（交叉熵或KL）在此类设置中并非最优，因此提出一种新颖的端到端TV损失，直接优化多步拒绝采样的接受率，带来约10%的接受率提升，在数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务中达到高达95%的接受率和额外25%的推理吞吐增益。第三，我们测试了RL期间多种在线MTP训练策略，并表明在RL前使用端到端TV损失和拒绝采样训练的MTP在整个RL过程中能保持一致的接受率和加速效果，无需昂贵的在线MTP更新。我们提供了大量实验与分析来验证我们的发现。实验结果表明，我们的方法在Qwen3.5、Qwen3.6和Qwen3.7模型的异步RL训练中实现了最高1.8倍的端到端加速。

</details>


### [214] [APPO: Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12384)
*Xucong Wang,Ziyu Ma,Yong Wang,Yuxiang Ji,Shidong Yang,Guanhua Chen,Pengkun Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出APPO方法，将强化学习中的分支和信用分配从粗粒度交互单元（如工具调用）转移到细粒度决策点，通过结合令牌不确定性与策略似然增益的分支分数选择分支位置，并引入过程级优势缩放来分配信用，在13个基准上平均提升近4个点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有智能体强化学习方法在信用分配上依赖于粗粒度的启发式单元（如工具调用边界或固定工作流），难以识别哪些中间决策真正影响最终结果。研究发现有影响力的决策点广泛分布在生成序列中而非集中在工具调用处，且令牌熵单独无法可靠反映其对最终结果的影响。

Method: 提出Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)，核心包括：(1) 使用结合令牌不确定性与策略似然增益的Branching Score选择分支位置，实现针对性探索并过滤虚假高熵位置；(2) 引入过程级优势缩放（procedure-level advantage scaling），在分支展开中更好地分配信用。

Result: 在13个基准测试上，APPO在强智能体RL基线上一致提升近4个点，同时保持高效的工具调用和可解释的行为。

Conclusion: 将分支和信用分配从粗粒度交互单元转移到细粒度决策点能够更有效地识别中间决策的影响，从而显著提升智能体强化学习的性能，且不牺牲工具调用效率和可解释性。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in agentic Reinforcement Learning (RL) have substantially improved the multi-turn tool-use capabilities of large language model agents. However, most existing methods assign credit over coarse heuristic units, such as tool-call boundaries or fixed workflows, making it difficult to identify which intermediate decisions influence downstream outcomes. In this work, we study agentic RL from two perspectives: \textit{where to branch and how to assign credit after branching}. Our pilot analysis shows that influential decision points are broadly distributed throughout the generated sequence rather than concentrated at tool calls, while token entropy alone does not reliably reflect their impact on final outcomes. Motivated by these observations, we propose \textbf{Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)}, which shifts branching and credit assignment from coarse interaction units to fine-grained decision points in the sequence. APPO selects branching locations using a Branching Score that combines token uncertainty with policy-induced likelihood gains of subsequent continuations, enabling more targeted exploration while filtering out spurious high-entropy positions. It further introduces procedure-level advantage scaling to better distribute credit across branched rollouts. Experiments on 13 benchmarks show that APPO consistently improves strong agentic RL baselines by nearly 4 points, while keeping efficient tool-calls and maintaining behavior interpretability.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，智能体强化学习（Agentic RL）的进展显著提升了大型语言模型智能体的多轮工具使用能力。然而，大多数现有方法在粗粒度的启发式单元（如工具调用边界或固定工作流）上进行信用分配，难以识别哪些中间决策影响下游结果。在本工作中，我们从两个角度研究智能体RL：\textit{在何处进行分支，以及分支后如何分配信用}。我们的先导分析表明，有影响力的决策点广泛分布在生成的序列中，而非集中在工具调用处，而令牌熵单独无法可靠反映其对最终结果的影响。受这些观察启发，我们提出了\textbf{Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)}，它将分支和信用分配从粗粒度交互单元转移到序列中的细粒度决策点。APPO使用结合令牌不确定性与策略诱导的后续延续似然增益的分支分数（Branching Score）来选择分支位置，从而实现更具针对性的探索，同时过滤虚假的高熵位置。它进一步引入过程级优势缩放（procedure-level advantage scaling），以在分支展开中更好地分布信用。在13个基准测试上的实验表明，APPO在强智能体RL基线上一致提升近4个点，同时保持高效的工具调用和可解释的行为。

</details>


### [215] [ATLAS: Active Theory Learning for Automated Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12386)
*Noémi Éltető,Nathaniel D. Daw,Kimberly L. Stachenfeld,Kevin J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ATLAS主动学习框架，通过迭代生成可解释的稀疏神经网络假设与设计最优区分实验，在bandit任务中实现5-10倍样本效率提升，加速行为模型的机制发现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 科学发现需要通过机制建模提出最优实验问题以获取信息量最大的数据，但传统方法依赖人工设计实验，效率低下。本文旨在自动化这一过程，实现数据驱动的可解释行为模型发现。

Method: ATLAS（Active Theory Learning for Automated Science）框架包含两个核心迭代步骤：（1）生成机制性假设——实例化为多样化的稀疏神经网络集成（Disentangled RNNs）；（2）设计最优区分这些假设的实验。将该方法应用于从bandit任务行为中恢复强化学习智能体。

Result: 在行为、结构和计算相似性等综合指标上，ATLAS相比随机实验实现了5-10倍的样本效率提升，其性能在与文献中专家设计实验的对比验证中进一步得到确认。

Conclusion: ATLAS展示了在认知科学及其他依赖机制模型发现的科学领域中加速人类可解释洞察的潜力。

Original Abstract: Advancing scientific understanding through mechanistic modeling requires posing the right experimental questions to yield maximally informative data. To automate this pursuit within cognitive science, we introduce ATLAS (Active Theory Learning for Automated Science), an active learning framework for the data-driven discovery of interpretable behavioral models. ATLAS iterates between generating mechanistic hypotheses--instantiated as a diverse ensemble of sparse neural networks (Disentangled RNNs)--and designing experiments that optimally distinguish between them. We test this approach on the problem of recovering reinforcement learning agents from their behavior in bandit tasks. ATLAS designs varied sequences of qualitatively novel experiments with temporal structure tailored to underlying agent characteristics. The models trained on these experiments are evaluated against a comprehensive set of metrics for mechanistic modeling that capture behavioral, structural, and computational similarity. ATLAS achieves a 5-10x improvement in sample efficiency across all metrics compared to random experimentation, and its performance is further validated against expert-designed experiments derived from literature. These in silico results showcase ATLAS's potential to accelerate human-interpretable insights in cognitive science and other domains where scientific inquiry relies on discovering mechanistic models.

中文摘要翻译: 通过机制建模推进科学理解需要提出正确的实验问题以产生最具信息量的数据。为在认知科学中自动化这一探索过程，我们引入了ATLAS（Automated Science的主动理论学习），一个用于数据驱动发现可解释行为模型的主动学习框架。ATLAS在生成机制性假设（实例化为多样化的稀疏神经网络集成，即解缠RNN）与设计最优区分这些假设的实验之间迭代。我们在从bandit任务行为中恢复强化学习智能体的问题上测试了该方法。ATLAS设计了多样的定性新颖实验序列，其时序结构针对底层智能体特征进行定制。在这些实验上训练的模型通过一套全面的机制建模指标进行评估，这些指标捕捉了行为、结构和计算相似性。与随机实验相比，ATLAS在所有指标上实现了5-10倍的样本效率提升，其性能在与文献中专家设计实验的对比验证中进一步得到确认。这些硅基实验结果展示了ATLAS在认知科学及其他依赖机制模型发现的科学领域中加速人类可解释洞察的潜力。

</details>


### [216] [Redesign Mixture-of-Experts Routers with Manifold Power Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12397)
*Songhao Wu,Ang Lv,Ruobing Xie,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将MoE模型中的路由器行与对应专家的主奇异方向对齐，并通过流形幂迭代（MPI）方法实现路由器重设计，实验证明该方法能提升MoE模型性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MoE模型中，路由器矩阵的行作为专家代理，理论上应编码对应专家矩阵的代表性向量以更好地反映token-专家亲和度，但缺乏有效的设计原则来强制实现这种压缩对齐。

Method: 提出流形幂迭代（MPI）方法，采用"幂迭代-再收缩"范式：先在路由器权重上执行幂迭代步骤，再施加范数约束（收缩操作）以确保效率与稳定性，从而使路由器行收敛到对应专家的主奇异方向。

Result: 在1B到11B参数规模的MoE模型上进行预训练，实验证实路由器行与专家主奇异方向的对齐能够实现更有效的MoE模型。

Conclusion: 将路由器行与对应专家的主奇异方向对齐是一种有效的设计原则，MPI方法能够实现这一对齐并提升MoE模型性能。

Original Abstract: Router is the cornerstone component to the Mixture-of-Experts models. Serving as expert proxies, the rows of the router matrix compute their similarity to the MoE inputs to determine which subset of experts is activated. Ideally, each router row is designed to encode the expert matrix into this representative vector, such that its dot-product with token can better reflect token-expert affinity. However, there exists no design principles to enforce this condensation. In this paper, we propose to align each router row with the principal singular direction of the associated expert, as this direction provides the most expressive mathematical description of a matrix. Based on this principle, we propose a router redesign with Manifold Power Iteration (MPI). Specifically, it introduces a "Power-then-Retract" paradigm, where a power iteration step is performed on the router weights, followed by a retraction to impose a norm constraint to ensure both efficiency and stability. Theoretically, we show that MPI drives router rows to converge toward the principal singular directions of associated experts. Empirically, we pretrain MoE model across scales from 1B to 11B parameters to confirm that this alignment facilitates more effective MoE models.

中文摘要翻译: 路由器是混合专家（MoE）模型的基石组件。作为专家的代理，路由器矩阵的行计算它们与MoE输入的相似度，以决定激活哪些专家子集。理想情况下，每个路由器行被设计为将专家矩阵编码成这一代表性向量，使其与token的点积能更好地反映token-专家亲和度。然而，目前尚无设计原则来强制实现这种压缩。本文提出将每个路由器行与对应专家的主奇异方向对齐，因为该方向提供了矩阵最富表现力的数学描述。基于这一原则，我们提出了一种基于流形幂迭代（MPI）的路由器重设计方法。具体而言，该方法引入了一种"幂迭代-再收缩"范式，即在路由器权重上执行幂迭代步骤，随后通过收缩操作施加范数约束以确保效率与稳定性。理论上，我们证明MPI驱动路由器行收敛到对应专家的主奇异方向。实验上，我们在1B到11B参数规模的MoE模型上进行预训练，证实这种对齐有助于构建更有效的MoE模型。

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [Learning-Based Phase Estimation for Multi-Frequency Carrier Phase Ranging under Structured Multipath Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11332)
*Jakub Bonczyk,Jakub Nikonowicz,Łukasz Matuszewski*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对多频OFDM系统中载波相位测距在非高斯、非对称多径环境下的偏差问题，提出了一种不依赖预设统计模型的学习型估计器，在3GPP工业工厂和城市微小区场景中取得了优于传统圆形统计方法的精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在真实工业和城市环境中，多径分量导致载波相位观测呈现非高斯、非对称特性，违背了传统圆形统计（如von Mises假设）的适用条件，使得经典模型驱动估计器产生偏差，亟需一种能够适应实际相位分布特性的鲁棒估计方法。

Method: 提出一种基于学习的估计器，直接对经验相位分布进行学习，无需预先假设统计模型。采用QuaDRiGa仿真框架，在3GPP标准的InF和UMi场景下构建训练数据，使估计器直接适应实际多径环境下的相位分布特征。

Result: 在3GPP标准的工业工厂和城市微小区场景下，传统von Mises圆形均值估计存在显著偏差；所提学习型估计器在多径条件下相比经典估计方法取得了更高的测距精度。

Conclusion: 传统基于圆形统计的载波相位测距方法在复杂多径环境下存在固有的模型偏差，而直接基于经验相位分布学习的数据驱动方法能有效克服这一局限，提升实际部署场景中的定位精度。

Original Abstract: Carrier-phase (CP) ranging is a key enabler of high-precision positioning in modern wireless systems. In multi-frequency OFDM-based sensing, phase observations across subcarriers provide information about the underlying propagation geometry. However, in realistic industrial and urban environments, these observations exhibit non-Gaussian and asymmetric characteristics due to deterministic multipath components, violating standard circular statistical assumptions. In this work, we analyze CP-based ranging as an estimation problem over circular phase observations. We show that conventional model-based estimators, such as circular averaging under von Mises assumptions, become biased under 3GPP-compliant propagation conditions. Using a QuaDRiGa-based simulation framework, we evaluate empirical phase distributions in Industrial Factory (InF) and Urban Microcell (UMi) scenarios and quantify their deviation from classical statistical models. To address these limitations, we propose a learning-based estimator that operates directly on empirical phase distributions without assuming a predefined statistical model. Experimental results show improved accuracy compared to classical estimators, particularly under multipath conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 载波相位测距是现代无线系统中实现高精度定位的关键技术。在多频OFDM感知中，各子载波上的相位观测提供了关于底层传播几何结构的信息。然而，在真实的工业和城市环境中，由于确定性多径分量的存在，这些观测呈现出非高斯和非对称特性，违反了标准圆形统计假设。本文将基于载波相位的测距问题建模为圆形相位观测上的估计问题。我们证明，在3GPP标准传播条件下，传统基于模型的估计器（如von Mises假设下的圆形均值估计）会产生偏差。利用基于QuaDRiGa的仿真框架，我们评估了工业工厂和城市微小区场景下的经验相位分布，并量化了其与经典统计模型之间的偏差。为解决上述局限，我们提出了一种基于学习的估计器，该估计器直接对经验相位分布进行操作，无需假设预定义的统计模型。实验结果表明，与传统估计方法相比，该方法在多径条件下具有更高的测距精度。

</details>


### [218] [Beamforming Gain with Single-RF Movable Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11342)
*Zhenqiao Cheng,Chongjun Ouyang,Hao Jiang,Xingqi Zhang,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种单射频链驱动的可移动天线阵列，分析了其波束赋形增益，证明了在单径信道中可实现随天线数线性增长的增益，在多径信道中建立了相干合并条件与孔径需求，并提出了多用户场景下的最优功率分配闭式解与天线位置搜索算法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索仅通过天线位置优化（而非射频链数量）实现波束赋形增益的可行性，以降低硬件成本和功耗。

Method: 理论分析了单射频链可移动阵列的波束赋形增益；推导了多用户场景下最大化最小功率分配的闭式解；设计了基于元素坐标搜索的天线布置算法。

Result: 在单径信道中波束赋形增益随天线数线性增长；在多径信道中建立了相干合并条件与孔径需求；多用户场景下给出了最优功率分配闭式解；揭示了天线布置增益与孔径资源之间的基本权衡。

Conclusion: 仅通过天线位置优化即可实现波束赋形增益，但需要以增大孔径资源为代价。

Original Abstract: A single-radio-frequency (RF) movable array is investigated, in which all movable elements are driven by a single RF chain with equal amplitude and equal phase. The achievable beamforming gain enabled by antenna placement is analyzed. Linear beamforming gain scaling with the number of antennas is shown to be achievable in single-path channels, while coherent-combining conditions and aperture requirements are established for multipath channels. For multiuser transmission, the optimal max-min power allocation is derived in closed form, based on which an element-wise coordinate-search algorithm is developed for antenna placement design. Numerical results validate the analysis and reveal a fundamental tradeoff: beamforming gains can be achieved through antenna placement alone, but only at the expense of increased aperture resources.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了一种单射频可移动天线阵列，其中所有可移动单元由单个射频链以等幅等相驱动。分析了通过天线布置实现的波束赋形增益。结果表明，在单径信道中可实现随天线数量线性增长的波束赋形增益，同时为多径信道建立了相干合并条件与孔径需求。针对多用户传输，推导了最优最大化最小功率分配的闭式解，并在此基础上开发了一种基于元素坐标搜索的天线布置设计算法。数值结果验证了理论分析，并揭示了一个基本权衡：仅通过天线布置即可实现波束赋形增益，但代价是增加孔径资源。

</details>


### [219] [Additive Noise, Shift Recovery, and Signed Signals in the Cumulative Distribution Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11432)
*Harbir Antil,Ratna Khatri,Aryan Saxena*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了累积分布变换（CDT）在加性扰动下的行为特性，推导了其一阶展开并分析了变换域敏感性，随后基于此结构提出了已知模板和未知模板下的位移恢复方法，并推广至有符号信号场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 量化分析CDT在加性噪声下的敏感性，揭示噪声在低密度区域被放大的机理，并利用该结构设计可解释的位移恢复方法。

Method: 推导加性噪声下CDT的一阶展开（非局部扰动通过原函数加权倒数密度实现）；建立已知模板下的投影估计器；提出未知模板下的联合恢复（去位移-平均迭代）；对有符号信号使用SCDT及特征匹配、交替对齐与平均的方法。

Result: 理论上给出了CDT变换域敏感性的显式描述及高斯噪声下协方差核的解析形式；已知模板下估计器在无噪声时精确、有噪声时满足稳定性界；未知模板下可联合恢复位移和公共模板；数值实验验证了扰动分析与恢复效果。

Conclusion: CDT在加性扰动下具有可解析的敏感性结构，基于此设计的位移估计与模板恢复方法简单有效且具有理论保证，可同时适用于密度信号和有符号信号。

Original Abstract: The cumulative distribution transform (CDT) is a quantile-based transport representation that exactly linearizes one-dimensional translations of positive densities. We study how this structure behaves under additive perturbations and how it can be exploited for shift recovery. Under a local nondegeneracy condition, we derive a first-order expansion showing that additive noise in physical space induces a nonlocal perturbation in CDT space through the primitive of the noise, weighted by the reciprocal density. This yields an explicit description of transform-domain sensitivity and shows, in particular, that perturbations are amplified in low-density regions. When the physical-space perturbation is modeled as a centered Gaussian random field, the induced first-order CDT perturbation is again Gaussian, with an explicit covariance kernel.
  We then use this structure to study recovery in CDT coordinates. In the known-template setting, the transport shift is obtained by projection onto the constant mode, giving an explicit estimator together with exactness in the noiseless case and a stability bound under perturbations. In the unknown-template setting, multiple observations permit joint recovery of the shifts and a common template up to the natural constant-mode gauge, leading to a simple de-shift--and--average procedure. We also consider a signed-signal analogue based on the signed cumulative distribution transform (SCDT), where shifts are estimated numerically by feature matching and unknown templates are recovered by alternating alignment and averaging. Numerical experiments validate the perturbation analysis and illustrate effective recovery for both density-valued and signed signals.

中文摘要翻译: 累积分布变换（CDT）是一种基于分位数的传输表示，能够精确线性化正密度的一维平移。本文研究了该结构在加性扰动下的行为特性，并探讨了如何利用该结构进行位移恢复。在局部非退化条件下，我们推导出一阶展开，表明物理空间中的加性噪声通过噪声的原函数（以倒数密度加权）在CDT空间中诱导出非局部扰动。这给出了变换域敏感性的显式描述，并特别表明扰动在低密度区域会被放大。当物理空间扰动建模为中心高斯随机场时，诱导的一阶CDT扰动仍然是高斯的，并具有显式的协方差核。然后，我们利用该结构研究CDT坐标下的恢复问题。在已知模板设定下，传输位移通过向常数模式投影获得，由此得到显式估计量，且在无噪声情形下精确，在扰动下具有稳定性界。在未知模板设定下，多次观测允许联合恢复位移和公共模板（至多相差常数模式规范），由此得到一个简单的去位移-平均过程。我们还考虑了基于有符号累积分布变换（SCDT）的有符号信号类比方法，其中位移通过特征匹配进行数值估计，未知模板通过交替对齐和平均恢复。数值实验验证了扰动分析，并展示了密度值信号和有符号信号的有效恢复。

</details>


### [220] [Coherent Multiband OFDM Sensing via Low-Complexity Gap Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11449)
*Lorenzo Pucci,Leonardo Pucci,Andrea Giorgetti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对ISAC框架中多带OFDM感知因频谱间隙导致的采样缺失和延迟域栅瓣问题，提出了一种低复杂度的迭代重建方法，包含初始延迟域均衡和基于迭代切趾的数据一致性算子，在中等间隙尺寸下性能接近全频带参考，且复杂度与估计目标数无关。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在ISAC中采用带内多带OFDM感知时，两个对称分配的感知子带之间存在频谱间隙（用于通信），导致频域采样缺失和延迟轮廓中的栅瓣问题，需要有效重建缺失样本并抑制栅瓣。

Method: 提出一种低复杂度迭代重建方法：(1) 初始延迟域均衡阶段；(2) 基于迭代切趾（apodization）的算子，并辅以数据一致性约束（data-consistency enforcement）。

Result: 在多目标场景下，所提方法在中等间隙尺寸下性能接近全频带参考，仅在较大间隙时因残余栅瓣而性能下降。与基于压缩感知的正交匹配追踪（OMP）基线相比，随着目标数量增加，该方法展现出更优的性能趋势，尤其在低信噪比（SNR）条件下，且计算复杂度与估计目标数无关。

Conclusion: 所提出的迭代重建方法能有效处理多带OFDM感知中的频谱间隙问题，在中等间隙下逼近全频带性能，且复杂度低、可扩展性好，适合实际ISAC系统部署。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates coherent multiband orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) sensing within an integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) framework. We consider an intra-band configuration in which two sensing subbands of equal width are allocated symmetrically within the same OFDM channel, while the central portion remains available for communication. We address the reconstruction of missing frequency-domain samples induced by the spectral gap and the suppression of the resulting grating lobes in the delay profile. To this end, we propose a low-complexity iterative reconstruction method consisting of an initial delay-domain equalization stage and an iterative apodization-based operator with data-consistency enforcement. Performance results for multi-target scenarios show that the proposed approach remains close to the full-band reference for moderate gap sizes and degrades only for larger gaps because of residual grating lobes. Compared with the compressed-sensing-based orthogonal matching pursuit (OMP) baseline, it exhibits a more favorable performance trend as the number of targets increases, especially in the practically relevant low-signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) regime, while offering a complexity scaling that is independent of the estimated number of targets.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了集成感知与通信（ISAC）框架下的相干多带正交频分复用（OFDM）感知问题。我们考虑一种带内配置，其中两个等宽的感知子带对称地分配在同一OFDM信道内，而中央部分仍可用于通信。我们解决了由频谱间隙引起的缺失频域样本的重建问题，以及由此产生的延迟轮廓中栅瓣的抑制问题。为此，我们提出了一种低复杂度的迭代重建方法，该方法包含一个初始延迟域均衡阶段和一个基于迭代切趾的算子，并辅以数据一致性约束。多目标场景下的性能结果表明，对于中等间隙尺寸，所提方法性能接近全频带参考，仅在较大间隙时因残余栅瓣而性能下降。与基于压缩感知的正交匹配追踪（OMP）基线相比，随着目标数量的增加，该方法展现出更优的性能趋势，尤其是在实际相关的低信噪比（SNR）条件下，同时其计算复杂度与估计目标数量无关。

</details>


### [221] [Antenna Coding and Digital Precoding for Limited Feedback MIMO Systems Using Pixel Antennas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11588)
*Zhetong Li,Hongyu Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对像素天线MIMO系统中全信道状态信息获取开销过大的问题，提出了一种基于有限反馈的联合码本设计方案，通过离线低复杂度码本构造算法，显著优于传统固定配置天线系统。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 像素天线虽能通过天线编码技术提供更多波束操控自由度，但受限于独特的硬件约束，在发射端获取完整信道状态信息会产生难以承受的开销，因此需要设计高效的有限反馈方案。

Method: 首先推导了实际功率约束下的最优数字预编码器，为简化天线编码器与数字预编码器的联合码本构建提供理论依据；然后提出了一种低复杂度的离线码本构建算法，用于设计天线编码器与数字预编码器的码本。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方案显著优于采用固定配置传统天线的无约束MIMO系统。

Conclusion: 通过有限反馈和预定义码本设计，像素天线MIMO系统能够在避免全CSI获取开销的同时，实现优于传统天线的性能，验证了该方案的有效性和实用性。

Original Abstract: Pixel antennas enable antenna coding, a technique that can provide more degrees of freedom in wave manipulation, to enhance wireless communications. However, acquiring full channel state information (CSI) at the transmitter incurs prohibitive overhead due to the unique hardware constraints from pixel antennas. This paper thus proposes a limited feedback multi-input multi-output (MIMO) system using pixel antennas, where the antenna coder and digital precoder are designed based on pre-defined codebooks and efficient index feedbacks. We first derive the optimal digital precoder under practical power constraints that provides insights on simplifying the joint codebook construction for antenna coder and digital precoder. We then develop a low-complexity offline codebook construction algorithm that enables subsequent codebook designs for the antenna coder and digital precoder. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme significantly outperforms unconstrained MIMO systems using conventional antennas with fixed configurations.

中文摘要翻译: 像素天线使能天线编码技术，该技术可在波束操控中提供更多自由度，从而增强无线通信。然而，由于像素天线的独特硬件约束，在发射端获取完整的信道状态信息会带来过高的开销。因此，本文提出了一种使用像素天线的有限反馈多输入多输出系统，其中天线编码器和数字预编码器基于预定义码本和高效索引反馈进行设计。我们首先推导了实际功率约束下的最优数字预编码器，这为简化天线编码器和数字预编码器的联合码本构建提供了深刻见解。随后，我们提出了一种低复杂度的离线码本构建算法，该算法支持后续天线编码器和数字预编码器的码本设计。仿真结果表明，所提方案显著优于采用固定配置传统天线的无约束MIMO系统。

</details>


### [222] [Measurement-Based Analysis of Outdoor Massive MIMO Channel Characteristics over FR3 Frequency Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11622)
*Enrui Liu,Pan Tang,Haiyang Miao,Qi Zhen,Jianhua Zhang,Sen Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文在8 GHz和15 GHz频段下，利用大规模MIMO平台对城市宏蜂窝（UMa）场景的信道特性进行了实验研究，发现高频段表现出更强的方向性，而低频段多径分布更广、性能更稳定，为多频段MIMO建模和6G系统设计提供了指导。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 由于低频段频谱资源日益紧张以及移动通信需求不断增长，FR3频段引起了广泛关注。本文旨在实验研究FR3频段（8 GHz和15 GHz）在UMa场景下的信道特性，为多频段MIMO建模和6G系统设计提供实验依据。

Method: 利用基于时分复用（TDM）的大规模MIMO平台，在8 GHz和15 GHz两个频点上对城市宏蜂窝（UMa）场景进行信道测量实验，提取了均方根时延扩展（RMS DS）和角度扩展（AS）等关键参数，并与3GPP TR 38.901模型进行了对比分析。

Result: （1）视距（LOS）下RMS时延扩展几乎不变，非视距（NLOS）下从8 GHz到15 GHz时延扩展减小；（2）方位角扩展（ASA、ASD）和仰角扩展（ESA、ESD）均随频率升高而减小；（3）15 GHz信道在LOS和NLOS下容量略优于8 GHz，归因于其更集中的多径能量和更大的主导奇异值。

Conclusion: 高频段表现出更强的方向性，多径能量更集中；低频段多径分布更广、性能更稳定。这些发现为多频段MIMO建模和6G系统设计提供了有价值的指导。

Original Abstract: The Frequency Range 3 (FR3) band is attracting increasing attention due to limited lower-frequency spectrum and growing mobile communication demand. This study experimentally investigates channel characteristics in Urban Macro (UMa) scenarios at 8 GHz and 15 GHz using a large-scale MIMO platform with time-division multiplexing (TDM). Key parameters, including root mean square (RMS) delay spread (DS) and angular spread (AS), were extracted and compared with 3rd Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) TR 38.901. Results reveal clear frequency-dependent behaviors: RMS delay spread remains nearly constant under line of sight (LOS) but decreases from 8 GHz to 15 GHz in non-line of sight (NLOS), indicating reduced multipath dispersion at higher frequencies. Both azimuthal spreads (including ASA and ASD) and elevation spreads (including ESA and ESD) exhibit a corresponding decrease with increasing frequency, demonstrating a consistent trend towards more directional propagation across all angular domains. Capacity analysis indicates that the 15 GHz channel slightly outperforms 8 GHz in both LOS and NLOS scenarios due to more concentrated multipath energy and larger dominant singular values. Higher frequencies exhibit greater directionality, whereas lower frequencies provide broader multipath distributions and more stable performance, offering valuable guidance for multi-band MIMO modeling and 6G system design.

中文摘要翻译: 由于低频段频谱资源有限以及移动通信需求的不断增长，频率范围3（FR3）频段正受到越来越多的关注。本文利用基于时分复用（TDM）的大规模MIMO平台，在8 GHz和15 GHz两个频点上对城市宏蜂窝（UMa）场景的信道特性进行了实验研究。提取了均方根（RMS）时延扩展（DS）和角度扩展（AS）等关键参数，并与第三代合作伙伴计划（3GPP）TR 38.901进行了对比。结果揭示了明显的频率依赖特性：视距（LOS）条件下RMS时延扩展几乎保持不变，但在非视距（NLOS）条件下，从8 GHz到15 GHz，时延扩展减小，表明更高频率下多径色散减弱。方位角扩展（包括ASA和ASD）和仰角扩展（包括ESA和ESD）均随频率升高而相应减小，表明在所有角度域中均呈现出朝向更定向传播的一致趋势。容量分析表明，由于多径能量更集中且主导奇异值更大，15 GHz信道在LOS和NLOS场景下均略优于8 GHz信道。更高频率表现出更强的方向性，而更低频率则提供更广泛的多径分布和更稳定的性能，这为多频段MIMO建模和6G系统设计提供了有价值的指导。

</details>


### [223] [Quantization Limitations of Leakage Suppression in Self-Calibrating Monostatic Integrated Sensing and Communication MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11665)
*Jan Adler,Florian Gast,Gerhard P. Fettweis,Rafael F. Schaefer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了量化噪声如何限制数字预编码方案在单站感知应用中抑制发射-接收射频链路间泄漏的性能，并推导出预测量化噪声影响的闭式解，通过数值分析和硬件测试平台验证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在单站感知应用中，发射链路信号直接泄漏到接收链路是主要挑战，数字预编码虽理论上能有效抑制泄漏，但实际部署中性能严重下降，亟需查明导致性能退化的主要因素。

Method: 推导了一个闭式解，用于预测量化噪声对任意数字联合泄漏估计与泄漏抑制预编码方案性能的影响，并进行了数值分析和硬件测试平台验证。

Result: 量化噪声是导致实际部署中数字预编码泄漏抑制性能严重退化的主要因素；所推导的闭式解能够准确预测量化噪声对泄漏抑制性能的影响，并在硬件测试平台上得到验证。

Conclusion: 量化噪声是限制数字预编码泄漏抑制实际性能的关键瓶颈，所提出的闭式解为评估和优化实际系统中的泄漏抑制性能提供了理论依据。

Original Abstract: Power leaking directly from transmitting into receiving radio-frequency chains is a key challenge in the realization of monostatic sensing applications with multi-antenna communication front-ends, to which a promising solution is digitally precoding transmitted signals for improved leakage suppression. While digital transmit precodings perform well in theory, real-world deployments typically exhibit severely degraded leakage suppression. This work investigates quantization noise as a primary factor limiting the performance of such precoding schemes. A closed-form solution predicting the impact of quantization noise on the performance of arbitrary digital joint leakage estimation and leakage suppression precodings is derived, numerically analyzed, and validated in a hardware testbed.

中文摘要翻译: 在多天线通信前端实现单站感知应用时，发射射频链路信号直接泄漏到接收射频链路是一个关键挑战，而数字预编码发射信号以改善泄漏抑制是一种有前景的解决方案。尽管数字发射预编码在理论上表现良好，但在实际部署中泄漏抑制性能通常会严重退化。本文研究了量化噪声作为限制此类预编码方案性能的主要因素。推导了一个闭式解，用于预测量化噪声对任意数字联合泄漏估计与泄漏抑制预编码性能的影响，并进行了数值分析和硬件测试平台验证。

</details>


### [224] [Parametric Channel Estimation with Hardware Impaired Hybrid Beamformers: Sensing, Communications, and Power Efficiency Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11829)
*Enrique T. R. Pinto,Silvio Mandelli,Marcus Henninger,Markku Juntti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合波束赋形架构、硬件非理想性（ADC量化分辨率、功率放大器/低噪声放大器非线性）对感知与通信性能的联合影响，并提出了双各向同性概念和MS-SAGE算法用于参数信道估计，结果表明中等分辨率ADC在功耗与性能间取得最佳折衷。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全数字阵列功耗和硬件成本高，混合波束赋形是更经济的替代方案；低分辨率ADC可降低功耗但引入量化误差，功放和低噪放的非线性也会影响系统性能。硬件非理想性对通信性能的影响已广泛研究，但对感知性能的影响尚不充分，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 定义了导频-合并器对的双各向同性概念（形式化完美能量公平的波束扫描）；提出了多起点空间交替广义期望最大化（MS-SAGE）算法，用于解决混合波束赋形系统中参数信道估计（PCE）的优化问题；通过数值实验评估了波束赋形架构和ADC分辨率对PCE、感知和通信性能的影响。

Result: 中等分辨率ADC可实现最节能的配置，在功耗与性能间达到最佳折衷；全数字波束赋形架构配合高分辨率ADC可被混合波束赋形架构配合中等分辨率ADC替代，性能损失不大但功耗和硬件成本更低。

Conclusion: 中等分辨率ADC在实际系统中具有最佳性价比，混合波束赋形架构可在降低功耗和成本的前提下替代全数字高分辨率架构。

Original Abstract: Due to high power consumption and hardware costs of fully digital arrays, hybrid beamformers are often considered as a more economic alternative. Furthermore, using high resolution analog to digital converters (ADCs) can also have prohibitive power consumption, which leads to lower resolution converters being considered for radio frequency (RF) front end design. The finite quantization resolution as well as the nonlinearities caused by the power amplifiers (PAs) and low noise amplifiers (LNAs) can have a substantial impact on system performance. While widely studied for communications, the impact of hardware impairments on sensing performance is considerably less explored. In this work, we study the interplay between hybrid beamforming architectures, hardware impairments, and sensing and communications performance. Additionally, we define the concept of double-isotropy for pilot-combiner pairs, formalizing the notion of a perfectly energy-fair beam sweep. The multiple start (MS) space alternating generalized expectation maximization algorithm (SAGE) is also introduced, aimed at addressing the optimization issues arising from parametric channel estimation (PCE) in hybrid beamformed systems. We then provide a set of numerical results assessing the impacts of beamformer architecture and ADC resolution on PCE, sensing, and communications performance. The results show that medium resolution ADCs lead to the most power efficient configurations, with the best tradeoff between power consumption and performance for the majority of beamforming architectures. Additionally, fully digital beamforming architectures with high resolution converters can often be substituted for a hybrid beamformer setup with medium resolution converters without significant performance loss at a lower power consumption and overall hardware cost.

中文摘要翻译: 由于全数字阵列的高功耗和硬件成本，混合波束赋形器通常被视为更经济的选择。此外，使用高分辨率模数转换器（ADC）也会带来高昂的功耗，这促使在射频（RF）前端设计中考虑使用低分辨率转换器。有限的量化分辨率以及由功率放大器（PA）和低噪声放大器（LNA）引起的非线性会对系统性能产生显著影响。虽然硬件非理想性对通信性能的影响已被广泛研究，但其对感知性能的影响却鲜有探索。在本工作中，我们研究了混合波束赋形架构、硬件非理想性以及感知与通信性能之间的相互作用。此外，我们定义了导频-合并器对的双各向同性概念，形式化了完美能量公平波束扫描的概念。还引入了多起点（MS）空间交替广义期望最大化（SAGE）算法，旨在解决混合波束赋形系统中参数信道估计（PCE）所引发的优化问题。随后，我们提供了一组数值结果，评估了波束赋形架构和ADC分辨率对PCE、感知和通信性能的影响。结果表明，中等分辨率ADC能够实现最节能的配置，在大多数波束赋形架构中，功耗与性能之间达到最佳折衷。此外，全数字波束赋形架构配合高分辨率转换器通常可以替换为混合波束赋形器配合中等分辨率转换器的配置，在不显著损失性能的情况下，实现更低的功耗和整体硬件成本。

</details>


### [225] [REACH: Interpretability-Driven Feature Identification and Architecture Compression for Multi-Channel Vehicular Channel Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11857)
*Simbarashe Aldrin Ngorima,Albert Helberg,Marelie H. Davel*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 论文提出了REACH框架，一种基于梯度的可解释性方法，用于分析多通道混合信噪比训练如何提升IEEE 802.11p车载通信中深度学习信道估计器的分布外泛化能力。通过输入级和滤波器级归因，实现了输入降维和架构压缩，并在压缩过程中发现分布外泛化能力下降速度慢于分布内精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多通道混合SNR训练能提升深度学习信道估计器在IEEE 802.11p车载通信中的分布外泛化能力，但其内部机制尚未被解释清楚。本文旨在揭示这一内在机制。

Method: 提出REACH框架，一个基于梯度的可解释性框架，包含两个层面：(1) 输入级归因，识别在所有信道条件下一致相关的时频特征子集，实现输入维度缩减；(2) 滤波器级归因，揭示近乎通用的内部表示，解释观察到的分布外泛化能力。基于滤波器分类，进行相关性引导的架构压缩。

Result: 输入维度缩减可在性能损失极小的情况下实现；相关性引导的架构压缩大幅减少了参数量和浮点运算次数，归一化均方误差退化小于1 dB；在压缩加剧时，分布外泛化能力的退化速度慢于分布内精度。

Conclusion: REACH框架成功解释了多通道混合SNR训练提升分布外泛化的内部机制，并通过输入降维和架构压缩验证了分析结果，尤其发现分布外泛化能力在压缩下更具鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Multi-channel mixed-SNR training improves out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation of deep learning channel estimators for IEEE 802.11p vehicular communications, yet the internal mechanism responsible for this remains unexplained. This work presents REACH (Relevance-based Explanation and Architectural Compression for cHannel estimators), a gradient-based interpretability framework that operates at two levels. Input-level attribution identifies a subset of time-frequency features consistently relevant across all evaluated channel conditions, enabling input dimensionality reduction with minimal performance loss. Filter-level attribution reveals a near-universal internal representation, providing a representational account of the observed OOD generalisation. Guided by the resulting filter taxonomy, relevance-guided architecture compression substantially reduces both the number of parameters and the number of floating-point operations (FLOPs) with sub-1 dB normalised mean square error (NMSE) degradation, and OOD generalisation degrades more slowly than within-distribution accuracy under increasing compression.

中文摘要翻译: 多通道混合信噪比训练能够提升用于IEEE 802.11p车载通信的深度学习信道估计器的分布外泛化能力，但其背后的内部机制尚未得到解释。本文提出REACH（基于相关性的信道估计器解释与架构压缩）框架，这是一个基于梯度的可解释性框架，在两个层面上运行。输入级归因识别出一个在所有评估信道条件下均持续相关的时频特征子集，从而能够在性能损失极小的情况下实现输入维度缩减。滤波器级归因揭示了近乎通用的内部表示，为观察到的分布外泛化能力提供了表征层面的解释。基于由此产生的滤波器分类，相关性引导的架构压缩大幅减少了参数量和浮点运算次数，归一化均方误差退化小于1 dB，并且在压缩加剧时，分布外泛化能力的退化速度慢于分布内精度。

</details>


### [226] [On the Robustness of AFBM Sensing to Power Amplifier Nonlinearities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11879)
*Eya Gourar,Henrique L. Senger,Gustavo P. Gonçalves,Kuranage R. R. Ranasinghe,Hyeon Seok Rou,Bruno S. Chang,Yahia Medjahdi,Giuseppe T. F. de Abreu,Didier Le Ruyet*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了功率放大器非线性对仿射滤波器组调制（AFBM）感知性能的影响，发现AFBM的模糊函数和整体感知性能对非线性失真具有显著鲁棒性，使其成为硬件受限下集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统的理想候选方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AFBM在ISAC中具有低带外发射、低峰均功率比和对双弥散信道鲁棒等优点，但通常需要高线性功率放大器，而ISAC要求高发射功率以确保可靠感知，两者之间存在根本矛盾。因此需要探究PA非线性对AFBM感知性能的影响。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示AFBM有效调制矩阵结构如何决定失真在模糊函数中的传播方式，并结合仿真实验验证AFBM在PA非线性条件下的感知性能。

Result: 理论分析表明AFBM调制矩阵结构决定了失真在模糊函数中的传播特性；仿真结果进一步证实AFBM的模糊函数和整体感知性能对PA非线性失真具有显著的敏感性不敏感性。

Conclusion: AFBM对PA非线性失真具有出色鲁棒性，是实际硬件损伤受限的ISAC部署中极具可行性的候选方案。

Original Abstract: We investigate the impact of power amplifier (PA) nonlinearities on the sensing performance of affine filter bank modulation (AFBM). While AFBM offers several advantageous properties for integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) - including reduced out-of-band emission (OOBE), low peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR), and natural robustness to doubly-dispersive (DD) channel effects - mitigating waveform distortion typically requires highly linear PAs. This creates a fundamental contradiction with ISAC applications, which demand high transmit power for reliable sensing. Our analytical results reveal that the structure of the effective AFBM modulation matrix dictates how distortion propagates within the ambiguity function (AF). Furthermore, simulations demonstrate that both the AF and the overall sensing performance of AFBM remain remarkably insensitive to such nonlinearities. These findings highlight the robustness of AFBM, making it a highly viable candidate for practical ISAC deployments constrained by hardware impairments.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了功率放大器非线性对仿射滤波器组调制感知性能的影响。尽管AFBM具有若干有利于集成感知与通信的特性——包括降低带外发射、低峰均功率比以及对双弥散信道效应的自然鲁棒性——但减轻波形失真通常需要高线性功率放大器。这与ISAC应用形成了根本性矛盾，因为ISAC需要高发射功率来实现可靠感知。我们的分析结果表明，有效AFBM调制矩阵的结构决定了失真如何在模糊函数中传播。此外，仿真实验证明，AFBM的模糊函数和整体感知性能对这种非线性失真仍然极不敏感。这些发现凸显了AFBM的鲁棒性，使其成为硬件损伤受限的实际ISAC部署中极具可行性的候选方案。

</details>


### [227] [Efficiency Meets Reliability: Enhanced Generalized Interleaved Transform for Random Multiplexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11890)
*Ming Wang,Shufeng Li,Lei Liu,Yao Ge,Yuhao Chi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RM-MAMP的随机复用通信系统，通过设计存储高效的Logistic混沌映射交织器和高阶置换多项式交织器（将存储从O(N)降至O(1)），并结合高可靠性的交织变换框架（交织相位扰动变换和多层交织耦合变换），在严重病态信道下实现了超过4dB的性能增益，同时保持了与完全随机交织器相当的误码率性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RM与CD-MAMP结合的通信系统面临两大问题：（1）传统交织器存储开销大，难以实用化；（2）在严重病态信道下性能下降严重。现有工作聚焦于交织和变换设计，但无法同时解决这两个问题。

Method: （1）提出Logistic混沌映射交织器，附带定量参数选择准则；（2）提出双阶段高阶置换多项式交织器，两者均将交织器存储从O(N)降至O(1)；（3）提出高可靠交织变换框架，包括交织相位扰动变换和多层交织耦合变换，用于增强等效信道矩阵的非相干性和多样性。

Result: 所提存储高效交织器在误码率性能上与完全随机交织器相当；高可靠变换在严重时变信道下提供超过4dB的性能增益。

Conclusion: RM-MAMP系统在降低存储开销和提升鲁棒性方面均取得了显著优势，验证了所提方法的双重效益。

Original Abstract: To meet the demands of 6G wireless systems operating in high-mobility scenarios, this paper presents a design of a random multiplexing (RM) communication system that is both storage-efficient and highly reliable. In principle, RM with cross-domain memory approximate message passing (CD-MAMP) can achieve replica maximum a posteriori (MAP)-optimal performance by constructing a fully dense equivalent channel matrix. However, its practical implementation is hindered by the large storage overhead of conventional interleavers and by performance degradation in severely ill-conditioned channels, which existing related work (focusing on interleaving and transform designs) fails to address simultaneously. To overcome these issues, we develop a storage-efficient and highly reliable system that integrates RM with CD-MAMP, referred to as RM-MAMP. Specifically, we propose a Logistic chaotic mapping interleaver with a quantitative parameter-selection criterion, and a dual-stage high-order permutation polynomial interleaver, both of which achieve nearly identical bit-error-rate (BER) as fully random interleavers while reducing the interleaver storage from O(N) to O(1) and significantly lowering interleaver signaling overhead. We further propose a highly reliable interleaved transform framework, comprising an interleaved phase perturbation transform and a multi-layer interleaved coupled transform, to enhance the incoherence and diversity of the equivalent channel matrix. Simulation results show that the proposed storage-efficient interleavers maintain BER performance comparable to fully random interleavers, while the highly reliable transforms provide over 4 dB gain in severely time-varying channels, confirming the dual benefits of reduced storage overhead and improved robustness for the enhanced RM-MAMP system.

中文摘要翻译: 为了满足6G无线系统在高移动性场景下的需求，本文设计了一种兼具存储高效和高可靠性的随机复用（RM）通信系统。原则上，采用跨域记忆近似消息传递（CD-MAMP）的RM系统通过构建全密集等效信道矩阵，能够实现副本最大后验（MAP）最优性能。然而，其实际实现受到传统交织器大存储开销以及严重病态信道下性能下降的制约，现有相关工作（聚焦于交织和变换设计）无法同时解决这些问题。为克服上述挑战，我们开发了一种存储高效且高可靠性的系统，将RM与CD-MAMP集成，称为RM-MAMP。具体而言，我们提出了一种带有定量参数选择准则的Logistic混沌映射交织器，以及一种双阶段高阶置换多项式交织器，两者在实现与完全随机交织器几乎相同的误码率（BER）的同时，将交织器存储从O(N)降低至O(1)，并显著降低了交织信令开销。我们进一步提出了一种高可靠性的交织变换框架，包括交织相位扰动变换和多层交织耦合变换，以增强等效信道矩阵的非相干性和多样性。仿真结果表明，所提出的存储高效交织器保持了与完全随机交织器相当的误码率性能，而高可靠性变换在严重时变信道下提供了超过4dB的增益，验证了增强型RM-MAMP系统在降低存储开销和提升鲁棒性方面的双重效益。

</details>


### [228] [NARRAS: Edge-Triggered Distributed Inference for CSI-Based Localization in Vehicular IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11914)
*Rodrigo Oliver,Ricardo Vazquez Alvarez,Alejandro Lancho,Stefano Rini*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为NARRAS的分布式上报策略，用于基于CSI的定位，每个远程天线阵列在活动预算约束下自主决定是否上报观测信息，通过可微分活动惩罚和验证校准的确定性阈值训练，利用信道图正则化提升潜在表征的几何结构，在低上行链路活动下实现更高的定位精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于CSI的定位中，空间分布式天线阵列虽然能提供丰富的信道视图，但将所有阵列的观测数据汇集到融合中心存在资源浪费，因为只有少数阵列携带有用信息，且共享上行链路仅支持有限数量的同时传输。因此需要一种机制，让每个阵列在平均活跃发送者数量预算下，自主决定当前观测是否值得上报。

Method: 提出NARRAS策略：每个远程天线阵列（RAA）结合其近期观测的循环摘要和上次传输的潜在特征记忆，做出本地上报决策。训练时通过可微分活动惩罚和验证校准的确定性阈值来控制显式活动预算，并使用信道图正则化来塑造潜在特征的几何结构。

Result: 在可比的上行链路活动水平下，NARRAS相比学习型和启发式的稀疏上报策略提高了定位精度；密集的全上报模型仍是无需预算约束的有用参考。在低活动率场景下，图正则化进一步降低了高百分位的定位误差。

Conclusion: 几何感知的潜在表征在稀疏上报下具有更强的鲁棒性；NARRAS为任务导向的分布式通信问题提供了一种有效的上报决策框架。

Original Abstract: CSI-based localization with spatially distributed antenna arrays exposes a basic resource trade-off. Each array can provide a rich view of the channel, but forwarding observations from all arrays to a fusion center is wasteful when only a few carry useful information, and the shared uplink supports only a limited number of simultaneous transmissions. We let each array decide locally whether its current observation is worth reporting, subject to a budget on the average number of active transmitters. We refer to this abstraction as Edge-Triggered Distributed Inference (ETDI). It captures a broader class of task-oriented communication problems where resource-constrained devices share an access channel for a common inference task. We instantiate ETDI for CSI-based localization, a common scenario in vehicular IoT networks. Spatially distributed remote antenna arrays (RAAs) encode local channel state information (CSI) from user equipment (UE) transmissions into latent features, and the fusion center estimates the UE position from the subset of reported features. We propose NARRAS, a decentralized reporting policy in which each RAA combines a recurrent summary of its recent observations with a memory of the last latent it transmitted. Training controls an explicit activity budget through differentiable activity penalties and validation-calibrated deterministic thresholds, and uses channel-chart regularization to shape the latent geometry. Experiments show that, at comparable uplink activity, NARRAS improves localization accuracy over learned and heuristic sparse-reporting strategies, while dense full-report models remain useful budget-free references. In low-activity regimes, chart regularization further reduces high-percentile localization errors, suggesting that geometry-aware latent representations are more robust under sparse reporting.

中文摘要翻译: 基于CSI的定位在使用空间分布式天线阵列时暴露出一个基本的资源权衡问题。每个阵列可以提供丰富的信道视图，但当只有少数阵列携带有用信息时，将所有观测数据转发到融合中心会造成浪费，且共享上行链路仅支持有限数量的同时传输。我们让每个阵列在平均活跃发送者数量预算下，自主决定当前观测是否值得上报。我们将这一抽象称为边缘触发分布式推理（ETDI），它涵盖了一类更广泛的任务导向通信问题——资源受限的设备共享一个接入信道以完成共同的推理任务。我们将ETDI应用于基于CSI的定位，这是车联网中常见的场景。空间分布的远程天线阵列（RAA）将从用户设备（UE）传输中获取的本地信道状态信息（CSI）编码为潜在特征，融合中心根据上报的特征子集估计UE位置。我们提出NARRAS，一种去中心化的上报策略，其中每个RAA将其近期观测的循环摘要与上次传输的潜在特征记忆相结合。训练通过可微分活动惩罚和验证校准的确定性阈值来控制显式活动预算，并使用信道图正则化来塑造潜在几何结构。实验表明，在可比的上行链路活动水平下，NARRAS相比学习型和启发式的稀疏上报策略提高了定位精度，而密集的全上报模型仍然是无需预算约束的有效参考。在低活动率场景下，图正则化进一步降低了高百分位的定位误差，表明几何感知的潜在表征在稀疏上报下具有更强的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [229] [Low-Density EEG for Seizure Detection: Evaluating CNN-RNN Architectures on a Behind-the-Ear Montage Setup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11970)
*Annika Stiehl,Patrick Wingert,Nicolas Weeger,Nicole Ille,Christian Uhl,Stefan Geißelsöder*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合深度学习架构在低密度（耳后模拟）脑电图配置下进行自动癫痫检测的有效性。通过系统比较多种CNN-RNN模型，提出的CNN-Merged模型在TUSZ数据集上达到了85.89%的ROC AUC和79.11%的平衡准确率，证明了其在资源受限的可穿戴系统中实现稳健、患者无关的癫痫检测的潜力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全球超过5000万癫痫患者需要自动检测系统来减轻临床医生负担并提高患者癫痫日记的准确性。然而，可穿戴脑电图应用中存在低密度电极配置导致的空间分辨率有限、信噪比降低以及公开训练数据集稀缺等挑战，亟需研究能够弥补空间信息损失的深度学习模型。

Method: 使用Temple University Seizure Corpus (TUSZ, v2.0.3)构建模拟耳后导联脑电图。系统比较了多种CNN-RNN混合架构（包括基于LSTM和GRU的变体），并在多种脑电图导联配置下进行评估。提出CNN-Merged模型，该模型融合了时间和频谱特征表示。

Result: CNN-Merged模型在测试集上达到ROC AUC为85.89%，平衡准确率为79.11%；在不同的参考导联配置下表现出强鲁棒性，有效弥合了传统全头皮记录与资源受限可穿戴系统之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 混合深度学习模型是实现低密度脑电图应用中稳健、患者无关的癫痫检测的一个有前景的方向。

Original Abstract: Epilepsy affects over 50 million individuals globally, underscoring the need for automated seizure detection systems that can alleviate clinicians workload and enhance the accuracy of patient seizure diaries. In wearable EEG applications, however, reliable detection remains challenging due to the limited spatial resolution of low-density electrode configurations, reduced signal-to-noise ratios, and the scarcity of diverse, publicly available training datasets. This study investigates the efficacy of hybrid deep learning architectures for automated seizure detection using a simulated behind-the-ear montage derived from the Temple University Seizure Corpus (TUSZ, v2.0.3). We conduct a systematic comparison of several CNN-RNN models, including LSTM- and GRU-based variants, across multiple EEG montages to evaluate their capacity to compensate for the loss of spatial information inherent to reduced electrode configurations. The proposed CNN-Merged model, which integrates temporal and spectral feature representations, demonstrates superior performance, achieving a ROC AUC of 85.89% and a balanced accuracy of 79.11% on the held-out test set. Furthermore, the model exhibits strong robustness across different reference montages, effectively bridging the performance gap between conventional full-scalp recordings and resource-constrained wearable systems. These findings substantiate the potential of hybrid deep learning models as a promising avenue toward robust, patient-independent seizure detection in low-density EEG applications.

中文摘要翻译: 全球超过5000万人患有癫痫，这凸显了对自动化癫痫检测系统的需求，以减轻临床医生的工作负担并提高患者癫痫日记的准确性。然而，在可穿戴脑电图应用中，由于低密度电极配置的空间分辨率有限、信噪比降低以及多样化的公开训练数据集稀缺，可靠的检测仍面临挑战。本研究使用源自坦普尔大学癫痫库（TUSZ, v2.0.3）的模拟耳后导联脑电图，研究了混合深度学习架构在自动化癫痫检测中的有效性。我们对多种CNN-RNN模型（包括基于LSTM和GRU的变体）在多个脑电图导联配置下进行了系统比较，以评估它们补偿电极配置减少所固有的空间信息损失的能力。所提出的CNN-Merged模型融合了时间和频谱特征表示，在独立测试集上表现出卓越性能，ROC AUC达到85.89%，平衡准确率达到79.11%。此外，该模型在不同参考导联配置下表现出强鲁棒性，有效弥合了传统全头皮记录与资源受限可穿戴系统之间的性能差距。这些发现证实了混合深度学习模型作为在低密度脑电图应用中实现稳健、患者无关的癫痫检测的一条有前景的途径的潜力。

</details>


### [230] [Unlocking the Potential of Movable Antennas: General and Practical Antenna Position Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12024)
*Weidong Mei,Changhao Liu,Dong Wang,Xin Wei,Yiming Wu,Boyu Ning,Zhi Chen,Jun Fang,Rui Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对可移动天线（MA）系统中天线位置优化（APO）缺乏可处理、解析且准确的信道模型的问题，提出了两种通用且有效的APO算法：连续APO用于大规模通信的灵活阵列信号处理，离散APO用于小尺度多径信道重塑，并进一步提出了基于学习的高效算法以减少信道状态信息（CSI）获取开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 可移动天线（MA）在无线通信中具有提升性能的潜力，但天线位置优化（APO）面临缺乏可处理、解析且准确的信道模型的挑战。现有算法多基于简化的理论信道模型，泛化性有限。

Method: 提出两类APO算法：1) 连续APO，用于灵活阵列信号处理以提升大规模通信性能；2) 离散APO，将天线移动区域离散化为多个采样点，基于逐点信道状态信息（CSI）使用离散算法确定最优MA位置，无需解析信道模型。此外，还提出了无需完整逐点CSI的基于学习的更高效APO算法。

Result: 通过数值结果验证了所提算法的有效性，并比较了各类算法的应用场景。

Conclusion: 所提出的连续APO和离散APO算法能够有效解决MA系统中天线位置优化问题，基于学习的方法可进一步降低CSI获取开销，为不同应用场景提供了通用且有效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Recently, movable antenna (MA) has attracted wide attention in wireless communications due to its potential in enhancing wireless communication performance via local movement within a confined region. However, antenna position optimization (APO) has emerged as a major challenge for MAs, due to the lack of a tractable, analytical, and accurate channel model in terms of antenna positions. Although existing works have developed various algorithms for APO, most of them are based on simplified theoretical channel models, which limit their generality. To address this challenge, in this article, we present more general and effective APO algorithms for different purposes, categorized as continuous APO and discrete APO, respectively. Continuous APO is mainly applied for flexible array signal processing to boost large-scale communication performance, while discrete APO is applied for small-scale multi-path channel reshaping. Specifically, the discrete APO discretizes the antenna movement region into multiple sampling points and employs discrete algorithms to determine the optimal MA positions based on the point-wise channel state information (CSI), without the need for an analytical channel model. To reduce the overhead for CSI acquisition, we also present more efficient learning-based APO algorithms that operate without requiring full point-wise CSI. Finally, we compare the application scenarios of the proposed algorithms and validate their effectiveness with numerical results.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，可移动天线（MA）因其能够在有限区域内通过局部移动增强无线通信性能的潜力而受到广泛关注。然而，由于缺乏在天线位置方面可处理、解析且准确的信道模型，天线位置优化（APO）已成为MA面临的主要挑战。尽管现有工作已开发出多种APO算法，但大多数基于简化的理论信道模型，限制了其泛化性。为应对这一挑战，本文针对不同目的提出了更通用且有效的APO算法，分别归类为连续APO和离散APO。连续APO主要应用于灵活阵列信号处理以提升大规模通信性能，而离散APO则应用于小尺度多径信道重塑。具体而言，离散APO将天线移动区域离散化为多个采样点，并基于逐点信道状态信息（CSI）采用离散算法确定最优MA位置，无需解析信道模型。为降低CSI获取开销，我们还提出了更高效的基于学习的APO算法，该算法无需完整的逐点CSI。最后，我们比较了所提算法的应用场景，并通过数值结果验证了其有效性。

</details>


### [231] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Power Allocation in ISAC Systems with Mobile Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12078)
*Zhilin Fu,Sangmin Kim,Sangwon Hwang,Jihwan Moon,Jeongwon Kim,Jaewan Kim,Inkyu Lee*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于软演员-评论家（SAC）的深度强化学习框架，并结合狄利克雷策略，为集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统实现面向移动目标跟踪的动态功率分配，在保障通信性能的同时提升跟踪性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在ISAC系统中，移动目标的跟踪需要在感知和通信之间动态分配功率资源，而传统方法难以适应随机目标运动和环境变化，因此需要一种能够实时优化功率分配的自适应方法。

Method: 首先将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），然后采用基于软演员-评论家（SAC）的深度强化学习方法求解，并引入狄利克雷策略以在随机目标运动下生成归一化的连续动作，同时精心设计奖励函数以平衡感知与通信性能并节约资源。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的方案在维持通信性能的同时，相比其他基线方法显著提升了目标跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 基于SAC结合狄利克雷策略的深度强化学习方法能够有效解决ISAC系统中移动目标跟踪的功率分配问题，实现感知与通信性能的动态平衡。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we study the power allocation for an integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) system which tracks a mobile target. We first model the problem as a Markov decision process, and then tackle it with a soft actor-critic (SAC) based deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach. We also combine a Dirichlet policy, which naturally produces normalized continuous actions under random target motion. To exploit different features of sensing and communication operations, we carefully design a reward function such that the system can dynamically control power allocation to conserve resources. The simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme enhances tracking performance compared to other baselines while sustaining communication performance.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究面向移动目标跟踪的集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统中的功率分配问题。我们首先将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，然后采用基于软演员-评论家（SAC）的深度强化学习方法进行求解。我们还结合了狄利克雷策略，该策略能够在随机目标运动下自然地生成归一化连续动作。为利用感知与通信操作的不同特性，我们精心设计了奖励函数，使系统能够动态控制功率分配以节约资源。仿真结果表明，所提出的方案在维持通信性能的同时，相比其他基线方法提升了跟踪性能。

</details>


### [232] [Characterization of Speech Imagery in Scalp EEG and Comparison with Motor Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12223)
*Bob Van Dyck,Liuyin Yang,Qiang Sun,Ang Li,Marc M. Van Hulle*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了言语想象与手指运动想象在头皮脑电图中的时空特征，发现言语想象表现出更弱、更分散的Alpha主导性增强，而非预期的对侧mu/alpha和低beta去同步化，其主导模式与言语运动模拟不符，更多来自非发音任务相关成分。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 言语想象作为一种内源性、本质语言性的脑机接口范式具有吸引力，但其在头皮脑电图中的主导时空特征尚未被充分刻画，亟需系统性研究。

Method: 采用被试内设计，收集言语想象、手指运动想象和无任务三种条件下的脑电数据，分析不同通道和时间上的频带功率动态，并训练分类器区分想象状态与无任务状态。

Result: 手指运动想象表现出预期的对侧mu/alpha和低beta去同步化；言语想象则呈现更弱、更分散的Alpha主导性增强。经基线归一化后，言语相关的Alpha增强在提示开始后变化不大。分类器区分想象与无任务的平衡准确率：言语想象0.563±0.072，运动想象0.718±0.127。

Conclusion: 言语想象在头皮脑电图中的主导时空模式与手指运动想象不同，更符合大量非发音任务相关成分的贡献，而非清晰发音运动模拟的类似物。

Original Abstract: SSpeech imagery is attractive as a brain-computer interface paradigm for communication because it is endogenous and intrinsically linguistic. Yet despite growing interest, its dominant scalp-EEG spatiotemporal characteristics remain poorly characterized. Here, we asked how speech imagery appears in scalp EEG and compared it against finger motor imagery. Using a within-subject dataset containing speech imagery, finger motor imagery, and no-task trials recorded under the same trial structure, we analyzed band-power dynamics across channels and time. Finger motor imagery showed the expected contralateral mu/alpha and low-beta desynchronization over sensorimotor areas, whereas speech imagery showed a weaker, more distributed alpha-dominant increase. After normalization to each condition's own post-trial interval, the speech-related alpha increase changed only modestly after cue onset, indicating that much of the speech-versus-no-task difference was already present during the instruction period. A classifier discriminating imagery from no-task reached mean balanced accuracies of 0.563 $\pm$ 0.072 for speech imagery and 0.718 $\pm$ 0.127 for motor imagery, with a stronger alpha/beta dependence for motor imagery than for speech imagery. Together, these results provide a clearer group-level characterization of speech imagery in scalp EEG and indicate that its dominant spatiotemporal pattern differs from that of finger motor imagery and is more consistent with substantial non-articulatory task-related contributions than with a clear articulatory-motor analogue.

中文摘要翻译: 言语想象作为一种内源性且本质语言性的脑机接口范式，在通讯领域具有吸引力。然而，尽管其受关注度日益增长，其在头皮脑电图中的主导时空特征仍未被充分刻画。本研究探究言语想象在头皮EEG中的表现，并将其与手指运动想象进行对比。利用一个被试内数据集——包含在相同试次结构下采集的言语想象、手指运动想象和无任务试次——我们分析了跨通道和时间的频带功率动态。手指运动想象在感觉运动区表现出预期的对侧mu/alpha和低beta去同步化，而言语想象则表现出更弱、更分散的Alpha主导性增强。在经各自条件的试次后间隔进行归一化后，言语相关的Alpha增强在提示开始后变化不大，表明言语与无任务间的大部分差异在指令期就已存在。区分想象与无任务的分类器对言语想象达到0.563±0.072的平均平衡准确率，对运动想象达到0.718±0.127，其中运动想象的alpha/beta依赖性强于言语想象。综合来看，这些结果提供了言语想象在头皮EEG中更清晰的群体水平特征，并表明其主导时空模式不同于手指运动想象，且更符合大量非发音任务相关成分的贡献，而非清晰发音运动模拟的类似物。

</details>


### [233] [LLM-Based Digital Twin Intelligence for Application-Aware Network Selection in 6G Heterogeneous Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12293)
*Brahim Mefgouda,Anis Bara,Lina Bariah,Hang Zou,Yuzhi Yang,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的数字孪生（DT）框架，用于未来6G异构无线网络中稳定、应用感知的无线接入技术（RAT）选择。该框架将网络选择从瞬时决策矩阵操作转变为基于演化无线DT状态的决策过程，结合了LLM辅助的多属性决策分支和直接LLM排序分支，并引入历史感知自适应归一化和DT记忆驱动的检索增强上下文学习，有效减少了排名反转和不必要切换问题，提高了服务感知QoS满意度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 未来6G异构无线网络需要支持多种无线接入技术、动态无线环境和多样化的QoS需求。传统网络选择方法仅依赖瞬时无线测量或静态排序规则，无法适应演化的无线状态、服务意图、数据包级QoS行为和候选RAT动态，因此需要更智能、更稳定的接入决策机制。

Method: 构建了一个LLM驱动的数字孪生框架，包括：(1) 融合站点几何、Sionna RT传播描述符、ns-3数据包级QoS仿真、服务上下文、候选RAT信息和决策记忆的数字孪生；(2) 统一意图代理将用户和服务需求转换为结构化决策优先级；(3) 两个互补分支：LLM辅助多属性决策（MADM-LLM-NS）和直接LLM排序（LLM-NS）；(4) 历史感知自适应归一化（HAAN）和DT记忆驱动的检索增强上下文学习（RA-ICL）提升决策稳定性。

Result: 数值结果表明，与代表性的MADM网络选择基线相比，所提框架有效减少了排名反转问题和不必要切换事件，同时提高了服务感知的QoS满意度。

Conclusion: 将LLM与数字孪生结合用于6G异构无线网络的接入选择，将网络选择从瞬时决策转变为基于演化DT状态的决策过程，通过LLM驱动的决策智能和稳定性增强机制，显著优于传统MADM方法。

Original Abstract: Future 6G heterogeneous wireless networks (HWNs) are expected to support multiple radio access technologies (RATs), dynamic wireless environments, and applications with diverse quality-of-service (QoS) requirements. In such environments, network selection (NS) cannot rely only on instantaneous radio measurements or static ranking rules. Instead, access decisions must account for the evolving wireless state, service intent, packet-level QoS behavior, and candidate-RAT dynamics. This paper proposes a large language model (LLM)-based digital twin (DT) framework for stable, application-aware RAT selection under candidate-set evolution. The main idea is to shift NS from an instantaneous decision-matrix operation to a decision process over an evolving wireless DT state. The constructed DT combines site-specific geometry, Sionna RT-based propagation descriptors, ns-3 packet-level QoS emulation, service context, candidate-RAT information, and decision memory. Rather than acting as a general-purpose controller for 6G networks, the LLM is used for DT-grounded decision intelligence in this specific NS task. On top of this DT, a unified intent agent translates user and service requirements into structured decision priorities for two complementary NS branches: an LLM-assisted multi-attribute decision-making branch (MADM--LLM--NS) and a direct LLM-based ranking branch (LLM--NS). To improve decision stability, the framework further introduces history-aware adaptive normalization (HAAN) and DT-memory-driven retrieval-augmented in-context learning (RA--ICL). Numerical results show that the proposed framework reduces rank-reversal problem and unnecessary handover events, while improving service-aware QoS satisfaction compared with representative MADM-based NS baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 未来的6G异构无线网络（HWNs）预计将支持多种无线接入技术（RATs）、动态无线环境以及具有多样化服务质量（QoS）需求的应用。在此类环境中，网络选择（NS）不能仅依赖于瞬时无线测量或静态排序规则。相反，接入决策必须考虑演化的无线状态、服务意图、数据包级QoS行为以及候选RAT的动态变化。本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的数字孪生（DT）框架，用于在候选集演化条件下实现稳定且应用感知的RAT选择。核心思想是将网络选择从瞬时决策矩阵操作转变为基于演化的无线DT状态的决策过程。所构建的数字孪生融合了站点特定几何信息、基于Sionna RT的传播描述符、ns-3数据包级QoS仿真、服务上下文、候选RAT信息以及决策记忆。在此特定网络选择任务中，LLM并非作为6G网络的通用控制器，而是作为基于数字孪生（DT）的决策智能体。在该数字孪生之上，一个统一的意图代理将用户和服务需求转化为结构化的决策优先级，用于两个互补的网络选择分支：LLM辅助的多属性决策分支（MADM-LLM-NS）和直接基于LLM的排序分支（LLM-NS）。为提升决策稳定性，该框架进一步引入了历史感知自适应归一化（HAAN）和DT记忆驱动的检索增强上下文学习（RA-ICL）。数值结果表明，与代表性的基于MADM的网络选择基线相比，所提框架有效减少了排名反转和不必要切换事件，同时提高了服务感知的QoS满意度。

</details>


### [234] [Near Field Multi-Band Localization: CRB, Efficient Estimator, and Threshold SNR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12314)
*Roberto Bomfin,Marco Mezzavilla,Sundeep Rangan,Marwa Chafii*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对单路径SIMO系统，提出了多频段定位的理论框架，推导了AoA和距离的闭式CRB，设计了基于Levenberg-Marquardt优化的单/多频段ML估计器，并解析刻画了阈值SNR，证明多频段处理能同时提升精度和降低SNR需求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有定位方法在多频段场景下缺乏严格的理论界限和解析的阈值SNR刻画，亟需建立统一的理论框架来指导多频段定位系统的设计与性能评估。

Method: 推导了均匀线阵下AoA和距离的闭式CRB及任意阵列的矩阵形式CRB；提出基于结构化Levenberg-Marquardt优化过程的单/多频段最大似然估计器；解析刻画了估计器从发散到逼近CRB的阈值SNR。

Result: 数值仿真表明，所提单/多频段估计器在高于预测TSNR的SNR下达到CRB，且多频段处理同时提高了估计精度并降低了SNR需求。

Conclusion: 该框架为下一代多频段定位提供了严谨的理论基础，并可推广至仰角估计、分布式阵列和多径环境。

Original Abstract: This paper presents a theoretical framework for multi-band localization for a single-path single-input multiple-output (SIMO) system. We derive closed-form Cramer-Rao bounds (CRBs) for angle-of-arrival (AoA) and distance for uniform linear arrays (ULAs), and an intermediate matrix-form formulation for arbitrary array shapes. We also develop benchmark single- and multi-band maximum-likelihood (ML) estimators for AoA-Distance, leveraging a structured Levenberg-Marquardt (LM) refinement procedure. A key contribution is an analytical characterization of the threshold SNR (TSNR) for the proposed estimators. This is the SNR threshold at which the estimator transitions from "off the chart" to CRB-approaching performance, for both TDoA and distance estimation. Numerical simulations confirm that the proposed single- and multi-band estimators achieve the CRB at SNRs above the predicted TSNR, and that multi-band processing simultaneously improves estimation accuracy and reduces SNR requirements. The resulting framework provides a rigorous foundation for next-generation multi-band localization and can be readily extended to elevation estimation, distributed arrays, and multi-path environments.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一个用于单路径单输入多输出（SIMO）系统的多频段定位理论框架。我们推导了均匀线阵（ULA）下到达角（AoA）和距离的闭式克拉美-罗下界（CRB），以及适用于任意阵列形状的中间矩阵形式CRB。我们还开发了基于结构化Levenberg-Marquardt（LM）优化过程的基准单频段和多频段AoA-距离最大似然（ML）估计器。一个关键贡献是对所提估计器的阈值SNR（TSNR）进行了解析刻画——该SNR阈值标志着估计器从"偏离图表"到逼近CRB性能的转变，适用于TDoA和距离估计。数值仿真证实，所提单频段和多频段估计器在高于预测TSNR的SNR条件下能够达到CRB，并且多频段处理同时提高了估计精度并降低了SNR需求。该框架为下一代多频段定位提供了严谨的理论基础，并可便捷地扩展至仰角估计、分布式阵列以及多径环境。

</details>


### [235] [Bending the Rules of Propagation: Caustic Beamforming for Next-Generation Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12321)
*Shicong Liu,Xianghao Yu,Robert Schober*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出"焦散波束成形"作为无线波束控制的新范式，利用焦散波束的自弯曲、自愈和近场无衍射三大特性，在6G网络中实现物理层安全增强、覆盖均匀化以及抗阻塞链路，并讨论了相关硬件架构与未来挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统波束成形技术在面对频繁阻塞和高动态传播环境时变得脆弱，亟需一种更鲁棒的波束控制范式以应对6G网络的复杂通信需求。

Method: 本文系统梳理了焦散波束的数学起源并进行分类，基于其自弯曲、自愈和近场无衍射三大独特传播特性，提出了若干6G应用场景，并通过两个案例（物理层安全和服务稳定性）验证其能力。

Result: 案例研究表明，焦散波束能够绕过潜在窃听者、提供更均匀的覆盖，并维持抗阻塞的通信链路。

Conclusion: 焦散波束成形有望成为6G网络中一种鲁棒的无线波束控制新范式，但仍需进一步研究其硬件实现和开放挑战。

Original Abstract: Conventional beamforming techniques primarily steer energy along desired directions or focus it at specific locations. These techniques become fragile when facing frequent blockage and highly dynamic propagation environments. In this article, we present caustic beamforming as a new paradigm for wireless beam control. First, we classify representative caustic beams according to their underlying mathematical origins and present three unique properties, namely self-bending, self-healing, and near-field non-diffracting. Building on these propagation properties, we then propose several application scenarios in sixth-generation (6G) networks. We undertake two case studies focused on physical layer security and service stability that highlight the capability of caustic beams to bypass potential eavesdroppers, deliver more uniform coverage, and sustain blockage-resilient links. We further discuss the enabling hardware architectures that facilitate practical deployments, and finally outline key open challenges regarding caustic beams that require further research.

中文摘要翻译: 传统波束成形技术主要沿期望方向引导能量或将其聚焦于特定位置。这些技术在面对频繁阻塞和高动态传播环境时变得脆弱。本文提出焦散波束成形作为无线波束控制的新范式。首先，我们根据其数学起源对代表性焦散波束进行分类，并提出三个独特性质，即自弯曲、自愈和近场无衍射。基于这些传播特性，我们进一步提出了第六代（6G）网络中的若干应用场景。我们开展了两项案例研究，聚焦于物理层安全和服务稳定性，突显了焦散波束在绕过潜在窃听者、提供更均匀覆盖以及维持抗阻塞链路方面的能力。我们进一步讨论了促进实际部署的使能硬件架构，并最后概述了焦散波束方面需要进一步研究的关键开放挑战。

</details>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Spatially Masked Regression Reveals Local and Distributed Predictability in Electrophysiological Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11415)
*Maryam Ostadsharif Memar,Nima Dehghani*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 提出空间掩码回归（SMR）框架，通过逐步扩大掩码区域量化神经记录中局部与分布式信息的贡献，发现单个电极信号同时包含局部冗余和全局分布信息。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经记录常被解释为局部测量，但每个传感器的信号也可能反映更广泛网络的分布式活动。这引发了一个基本问题：电极信号在多大程度上反映局部vs分布式信息？具体而言，电极活动有多少由其邻近区域承载，又有多少嵌入在更广泛的阵列中？

Method: 提出Spatially Masked Regression (SMR)框架，从剩余电极重建每个电极的时间序列，同时排除目标电极周围一个可配置的邻域。通过逐步增大掩码，将空间局部性作为实验控制变量，量化在屏蔽邻近通道后预测信息保留多少。

Result: 在颅内EEG（异质电极覆盖）和头皮EEG（运动感觉皮层标准化导联）上均实现了强主体内重建；即使排除局部邻域仍有显著残差预测能力；EEG的跨主体迁移明显强于iEEG。掩码显示邻近电极对重建贡献大但不能完全解释，表明单个通道既反映局部冗余也反映全局分布结构。破坏相位结构或时间顺序的替代数据显著降低性能。

Conclusion: SMR可作为量化神经记录中局部与分布式信息平衡的可解释框架。

Original Abstract: Neural recordings are often interpreted as local measurements, yet the signal at any one sensor can also reflect structured activity distributed across the broader network. This raises a basic question: to what extent does an electrode's signal reflect local versus distributed information in the underlying system? More specifically, how much of an electrode's activity is carried by its immediate neighborhood, and how much is embedded more broadly across the array? We address this with a Spatially Masked Regression (SMR) framework that reconstructs each electrode's timeseries from the remaining electrodes while excluding a configurable neighborhood around the target. By progressively increasing this mask, spatial locality becomes an experimental control for quantifying how much predictive information survives after nearby channels are withheld. We apply SMR to intracranial EEG with heterogeneous electrode coverage and to scalp EEG with standardized montages over sensorimotor cortex. Using distance correlation between original and reconstructed signals, we find strong within-subject reconstruction in both modalities, substantial residual predictability even when local neighbors are excluded, and markedly stronger cross-subject transfer in EEG than in iEEG. Masking shows that nearby electrodes contribute strongly to reconstruction but do not account for all of it, indicating that individual channels reflect both local redundancy and broader distributed structure. Surrogates that preserve selected marginal or spectral properties while disrupting phase structure or temporal ordering substantially reduce performance, supporting the conclusion that SMR depends on structured temporal and cross-channel organization rather than on marginal statistics alone. These results position SMR as an interpretable framework for quantifying the balance between local and distributed information in recordings.

中文摘要翻译: 神经记录通常被解读为局部测量，但单个传感器上的信号也可以反映更广泛网络中分布的结构化活动。这引出一个基本问题：电极信号在多大程度上反映底层系统的局部信息与分布式信息？更具体地说，电极活动有多少由其邻近区域承载，又有多少嵌入在更广泛的阵列中？我们通过空间掩码回归（SMR）框架来解决这一问题，该框架从剩余电极重建每个电极的时间序列，同时排除目标周围一个可配置的邻域。通过逐步增大掩码，空间局部性成为量化在屏蔽邻近通道后预测信息保留多少的实验控制变量。我们将SMR应用于具有异质电极覆盖的颅内EEG以及运动感觉皮层上具有标准化导联的头皮EEG。利用原始信号与重建信号之间的距离相关性，我们发现两种模态均具有强主体内重建能力，即使排除局部邻域后仍有显著残差预测能力，且EEG的跨主体迁移明显强于iEEG。掩码分析表明，邻近电极对重建贡献显著但并不能完全解释全部预测信息，说明单个通道既反映局部冗余也反映更广泛的分布结构。保留边际或频谱特性但破坏相位结构或时间顺序的替代数据会显著降低性能，支持SMR依赖于结构化的时间与跨通道组织而非仅依赖于边际统计这一结论。这些结果将SMR定位为量化神经记录中局部与分布式信息平衡的可解释框架。

</details>


### [237] [End-to-End Machine Learning for Depressive State Classification via EEG and fNIRS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11555)
*Riki Sakurai,Simon Kojima,Mihoko Otake-Matsuura,Shin'ichiro Kanoh,Tomasz M. Rutkowski*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 这项初步研究以11名健康学生为对象，建立了一个基于脑电和功能近红外光谱等生物信号的抑郁症检测框架，旨在推动客观、自动化的临床诊断工具发展。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统精神科诊断依赖临床访谈和患者自我报告，易受主观偏差和经验差异影响。为满足定量评估需求，需要开发基于生物信号的客观检测方法，尤其是对于个体自身可能未察觉的潜伏抑郁状态，以及在老龄化人群中抑郁与痴呆的高共病早期鉴别。

Method: 以11名健康学生为对象的初步实验研究，构建基于脑电（EEG）和功能近红外光谱（fNIRS）等生物信号的抑郁症检测框架。

Result: 建立了基于生物信号的抑郁症检测框架，为后续临床自动化客观诊断工具的开发奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该初步研究成功建立了一个基于生物信号的抑郁症检测框架，是迈向自动化、客观化临床诊断工具的基础性一步。

Original Abstract: The escalating demand for mental healthcare, driven by rising societal stress, highlights the limitations of traditional psychiatric diagnostics. Conventional methods - relying primarily on clinical interviews and patient self-reports - are inherently vulnerable to subjective bias and the varying empirical judgment of practitioners. To address the need for quantitative evaluation, biological signal-based detection, including electroencephalography (EEG) and functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS), has emerged as a promising objective alternative. Such technology is particularly vital for identifying latent depressive states that may be unrecognized by the subjects themselves. Furthermore, in aging populations, the high comorbidity between depression and dementia necessitates early differentiation to prevent mutual symptom exacerbation and maintain Quality of Life (QoL). This pilot study of eleven healthy students establishes a framework for biological signal-based depression detection, serving as a foundational step toward automated, objective diagnostic tools for clinical use.

中文摘要翻译: 社会压力日益加剧导致对心理医疗保健的需求不断攀升，这凸显了传统精神科诊断的局限性。传统方法主要依赖临床访谈和患者自我报告，本质上容易受到主观偏差以及从业者不同经验判断的影响。为了满足定量评估的需求，基于生物信号的检测方法，包括脑电图（EEG）和功能近红外光谱（fNIRS），已成为一种有前景的客观替代方案。这类技术对于识别个体自身可能尚未察觉的潜伏抑郁状态尤为关键。此外，在老龄化人群中，抑郁与痴呆的高共病性要求进行早期鉴别诊断，以防止症状相互恶化并维持生活质量（QoL）。这项以11名健康学生为对象的初步研究建立了一个基于生物信号的抑郁症检测框架，是迈向临床自动化、客观诊断工具的基础性一步。

</details>


### [238] [Large language models selectively converge with human-shared neural semantic representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11598)
*Chen Hong,Ximing Shao,Gangyi Feng*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究结合伪超扫描MEG与人际神经编码建模，比较人类与大语言模型（LLMs）在共享语义神经表征上的维度结构，发现LLMs能捕捉人类共享语义的大部分成分，但其对齐是有选择性的，且与能动性、情感和社会体验相关的维度差异最大。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 人际沟通需要建立共享语义以使听者理解说话者的话语含义，但这种共享神经表征的维度结构尚不清晰。LLMs日益接近人类语言能力和神经响应，但尚不清楚它们是否捕捉到了人脑之间共享的相同语义结构。

Method: 采用讲故事-听故事伪超扫描MEG实验，结合维度分解的人际神经编码建模。由人类和五种近期LLMs对说话者叙述中的内容词在十个语义维度（感知、运动、空间、时间、社会性、生命性、情绪、注意力、因果性、驱动力）上进行评分，并检验这些维度在声学和语音特征之外是否能解释说话者-听者神经同步。

Result: 人类和LLM来源的语义空间均能解释神经同步，但这些共享语义更适合描述为多维神经结构而非单一全局信号。这些模式还预测了听者故事理解的个体差异。然而，相当的整体预测能力掩盖了表征几何中的系统性差异。更大的LLM与人类在语义结构和神经同步上对齐更紧密、重叠更大，但这种对齐是不完全的且具有维度依赖性。与能动性、情感和社会体验密切相关的维度出现了最大分歧。

Conclusion: LLMs能捕捉人类共享神经语义的实质性成分，但其对齐是有选择性的。更大或能力更强的模型能改善近似程度，但社会和情感维度的语义仅能被部分捕捉。

Original Abstract: Interpersonal communication requires building shared semantics that enable listeners to understand speakers' meanings from their unfolding language, but the dimensional structure of this shared neural representation remains unclear. LLMs increasingly approximate human language capability and neural responses, raising the question of whether they capture the same semantic structure shared between human brains. Here, we combined storytelling-listening pseudo-hyperscanning MEG with dimension-resolved interbrain encoding modeling to compare human- and LLM-derived accounts of shared neural semantic representations. Content words from the speaker's narratives were rated by humans and five recent LLMs along ten semantic dimensions (i.e., perception, motor, space, time, socialness, animacy, emotion, attention, causality, and drive). We tested whether these dimensions explained speaker-listener neural synchronization (NS) beyond acoustic and phonological features. Both human- and LLM-derived semantic spaces explained NS, but these shared semantics are better characterized as a multidimensional neural structure rather than a single global signal. These patterns also predicted individual differences in listeners' story comprehension, linking neural alignment to cognition. However, comparable overall prediction concealed systematic differences in representational geometry. Larger LLMs aligned more closely and showed greater overlap with humans in semantic structure and NS, but this was incomplete and dimension-dependent. The largest divergences emerged for dimensions closely tied to agency, affect, and social experience. These findings show that LLMs capture substantial components of human shared neural semantics, but their alignment is selective. Larger or more capable models improve the approximation, whereas socially and affectively grounded dimensions are captured only partially.

中文摘要翻译: 人际沟通需要构建共享语义，使听者能够从说话者展开的语言中理解其含义，但这种共享神经表征的维度结构仍不清楚。大语言模型（LLMs）日益接近人类语言能力和神经响应，这引发了一个问题：它们是否捕捉到了人脑之间共享的相同语义结构。本研究结合讲故事-听故事伪超扫描脑磁图（MEG）与维度分解的人际神经编码建模，比较了人类和LLM来源的共享神经语义表征。说话者叙述中的内容词由人类和五种近期LLMs在十个语义维度（即感知、运动、空间、时间、社会性、生命性、情绪、注意力、因果性和驱动力）上进行评分。我们检验了这些维度在声学和语音特征之外是否能解释说话者-听者神经同步（NS）。人类和LLM来源的语义空间均能解释NS，但这些共享语义更好地被刻画为多维神经结构而非单一全局信号。这些模式还预测了听者故事理解的个体差异，将神经对齐与认知联系起来。然而，相当的整体预测能力掩盖了表征几何中的系统性差异。更大的LLM与人类的对齐更紧密，且在语义结构和NS上显示出更大的重叠，但这种对齐是不完全的且具有维度依赖性。最大的分歧出现在与能动性、情感和社会体验密切相关的维度上。这些发现表明，LLMs能捕捉人类共享神经语义的实质性成分，但其对齐是有选择性的。更大或能力更强的模型能改善近似程度，而社会和情感维度的语义仅能被部分捕捉。

</details>
